kattikāśuktihastaiḥ phetkārai' rbhīmanādairjvaladanalamukhaiḥ vetāḍā bhīṣayanto daśadiśi valaye saṃsthitāḥ krūracittāḥ māṃsāhārāḥ kṣudhārttā nararudhiraratāḥ śuṣkakāyā vivastrā: yaḥ pādau yoginīnāṃ smarati bhayaharau tasya kurvanti rakṣām ||
naṣṭāṅgulyagravṛttaḥ pratidina harato naṣṭanāsauṣṭhakarṇaḥ pūyaklinnavraṇebhyaḥ pravahati bahuśo vakra 'kapaṭhāṅghrihaste saṃtyakto bandhuvargairmṛtaka tanuriva klinnagandhaprabhāvāt rogairmuktaḥ bhavati sucaraṇau yaḥ smared yoginīnām ||
vimalaprabhā yāṃ [ lokadhātu- sajvālaistīkṣṇadaṃṣṭre: phaṇiharisa rabhavyāghramātaṅgavaktraḥ cakrāsīṣu triśūlāṃkuśa kuliśakaraiḥ sarvadivindradūtāḥ bodhau dhyānaikaniṣṭhaṃ paramasukhagataṃ sādhakaṃ tarjayanti ye nāśaṃ te'sya yānti svamanasi caraṇaṃ yasya yogeśvarīṇām ||
vārṣe bhāraṃ vahatyānagaramapi vanāt kardamāpūrṇamārge hemante vastrahīno vrajati himapathaṃ yāti deśāntaraṃ ca grīṣme sūryāṃśutaptaṃ sabhayamarupathaṃ nirjalaṃ yo daridraḥ tairduḥkhairmuktakāmī bhavati sucaraṇau dhyāyatāṃ yoginīnām ||
kakṣākṣiśrotranāsāmukhatanura vatastīvramāyāti gandho daurbhāgyaṃ sarvakālaṃ suratavirahitaṃ sarvanārīviyogāt dveṣaḥ strīṇāṃ sadā syādaśubhaphalavaśāt yeṣu teṣāṃ samastaṃ saubhāgyaṃ varṇarūpa sukhavaradapadaṃ dhyāyatāṃ yoginīnām ||
drohāt kṣubdho narendraḥ pralayayama ivātyantamāraikaniṣṭhaḥ sainyaṃ saṃpreṣayan yo maraṇabhayakaraṃ yeṣu bhṛtyeṣu śīghram teṣāṃ so'pi praśānto bhavati hi varadaḥ sarvasammāna ' dānaiḥ sarvatrailokyanāthaṃ su manasi caraṇaṃ dhyāyatāṃ yoginīnām ||
yeṣāṃ vajraprapātaḥ kvacidapi nabhasaḥ strīprasaṅgācca nityam mṛtyuṃ mūrcchā virāgaṃ punarapi kurute janmajanmāntarebhyaḥ teṣāṃ so'pi praśānto bhavati nidhanatāṃ yāti bhūyo na janmī śrīkarmajñāta divyāga kuliśapadaṃ dhyāyatāṃ yoginīnām ||
yeṣāṃ sarvārthanāśo bhavati dinaniśaṃ strīprasaṅge virāgāt bhūyo jātyantaraiḥ sa prabhavati bahuśo vāsanāśliṣṭacittāt sarvārthaḥ so'vināśī bhavati jinavaraiḥ pūjitaṃ saṃstutaṃ yat prajñānāṃ viśvarūpaṃ hyasamasamapadaṃ dhyāyatāṃ yoginīnām ||
yoginyo'rkendurāhutrividhapathagatāḥ piṅgaleḍāvadhūtyo tayorjñānakāyaḥ bhāvābhāvapraṇaṣṭau samagaticaraṇau tau eko'sāvakṣaraṃ tat sahajasukhapadaṃ tau tadeva smaredyaḥ atroktaṃ tasya śīghraṃ sakalabhayaharaṃ syāt phalaṃ janmanīha ||
sthānaṃ śrīyoginīnāṃ kuliśa maṇigṛhaṃ tatpraveśāya mārgo niḥkleśī naṣṭumāro gadita iha mayā stotrapūrvo jinoktaḥ paṭale ] sanmārgaṃ niyamoddeśaḥ yeṣāṃ mārgo vinaṣṭo'nṛtatamasi sadā mārasaṅgergatānāṃ te'nenāyāntvadūraṃ kuliśamaṇigṛhaṃ dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāṅgo gururiha jagataḥ labdhamārgeṇa puṃsām ||
saṃsthito dharmacakre devānāṃ maulicūḍāmaṇikaranikaraiścumbitaḥ pādapadmaḥ sanmārga darśa māno divasakara iva dhvastasarvāndhakāro dhyātvā dhūmādimārgaiḥ sahajasukhapadaṃ kālacakro bhavet saḥ ||
yo devāhinarāsurendramukuṭaiḥ saṃghuṣṭapādāmbujaḥ samyagjñānadivākarastrijagataḥ śrīdharmacakre sthitaḥ mārakleśamṛgapracaṇḍavadhakaḥ śrīśākyasiṃhaḥ sadā ṭīkāṃ tatkṛpayā likhāmi jagatastatprajñayā coditaḥ ||
kakārāt kāraṇe śānte lakārācca layo'tra vai |
cakārāñcalacittasya ṛkārāt kālo'kṣarasukhajñānamupāyaḥ kramabandhanāt ||
karuṇātmakaḥ |
jñeyākāraṃ jagaccakraṃ śrī prajñā śūnyatātmikā ||
asya śrīkālacakrasya vajrayogasya sarvataḥ satyadvaye sthitasyāsyābhidhānaṃ vācakaṃ bhavet ||
asmin tantre mayā ṭīkā sugatavyākṛtena vai |
mañjuśrīcoditenaiva lokanāthena likhyate ||
bālapaṇḍitamūrkhāṇāṃ tantre guhyaprakāśikā |
hitā māteva putrāṇāṃ sukhārthaṃ sarvadehinām ||
sanmārgaṃ vajrasattvasya yayā vindanti yoginaḥ |
tasmāt sā likhyate kṣipraṃ ṭīkeyaṃ vimalaprabhā ||
iti mūla e tantrānusāriṇyāṃ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ sakalamā ravighnavināśataḥ parameṣṭadevatā sanmārganiyamoddeśaḥ prathamaḥ ||

po vimalaprabhāyā tantradeśanoddeśaḥ [
lokadhātu- karaṇamucyate, paścāllaghutantre prayojana - prayojanādikañceti |
iha prathamaṃ tāvadabhidheyābhi- dhāna - sambandha - prayojana - prayojanaprayojanānyabhisaṃ vīkṣya vaineyajanānāṃ svacittābhiprāyeṇeha janmani buddhatvaphalapradam |
mahāsukhāvāse paramādibuddhavajradhātumahā- maṇḍale vajrasiṃhāsanasthena buddhabodhisattvakrodharājadevanāgadevatādeva tīgaṇaparivṛtena traidhātu- kavanditacaraṇāravindena dhātukaikacakravartinā paramādibuddhena nirandha yena śrīkāla- cakrabhagavatā sucandrādhyeṣitena |
dvādaśasāhasraṃ paramādibuddhaṃ niranvayaṃ kālacakraṃ tantrarājaṃ* vajradharajñānakāyasākṣibhūtayā nāmasaṅgītyāliṅgitaṃ sarvatantreṣu vajrapadasākṣibhūtam, udghāṭitabuddharatnakaraṇḍakam, laukikalokottarasatyāśritaṃ catasṛbhiḥ sambodhibhiścaturvajraḥ pariśuddhaṃ catuḥ kāya - ṣaṭkula- dvādaśasatya ṣoḍaśatattva - ṣoḍaśaśūnyatā - ṣoḍaśa karuṇātmakābhidheya- vācakam, laukikadaśalokottarekādaśābhiṣekaprakāśakam karmamudrā - jñānamudrā -mahāmudrā- laukikalokottarasiddhiprakāśakam, lokadhātvadhyātmābhiṣekasādhanajñānapañcapaṭalātmakam, pañcakalpātmakaṃ vā, narādisakalasattvānāṃ samyaksambuddhatvalābhāya sandeśitam |
ato laghutantrasaṅgītikaraṇāya mañjuśriyastathāgatavyākaraṇam, anāgate'dhvani brahma ṛṣīṇāṃ vaineyārthaṃ mama ṭīkākaraṇāya ca, anyeṣāṃ yamāntakādīnāṃ tantradeśanārtham, sambhalaviṣayādi- ṣavati - koṭigrāmanivāsināṃ sarvasattvānāṃ ca mārgalābhāyeti |
iha paramāṇusandohapūrvaṅgamo lokadhātuḥ, lokadhātupūrvaṅgamāḥ sattvāḥ, sattvapūrva- ṅgamo'bhiṣeko laukikalokottaraśca, abhiṣekapūrvaṅgamaṃ puṇyasambhāraṃ maṇḍalacakrabhāvanābhyā- samakaniṣṭhabhuvanaparyantaṃ laukikasiddhisādhanam, laukikasiddhipūrvaṅgamaṃ puṇyajñānasambhārābhyāṃ pūrvajanmaśūnyatākaruṇāsaṃvṛtinirālambavāsanābalena paramāṇusandohātītaṃ maṇḍalādivikalpa- bhāvanārahitaṃ mahājñānamudrāsiddhisādhanamiti |
ataḥ prathamo lokadhātukalpaḥ, tato'dhyātmakalpaḥ, tato'bhiṣekakalpaḥ, tato laukika- siddhisādhanākalpaḥ, tataḥ paramākṣarajñānasādhanākalpaḥ |
evaṃ yathānukrameṇa pañcakalpātmakaṃ tantrarājaṃ pañcapaṭalātmakaṃ vā |
anu prajñopāyātmako vajrayoga ādibuddho niranvayaḥ kālacakro bhagavānabhidheyaḥ |
sa cānayā pañcapaṭalasvabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ |
lokadhātupaṭalaḥ |

iti lokadhātupaṭale ||
rajaḥ tato'dhyātmapaṭale prathamaṃ garbhādhānasaṃ grahasvabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ, śuklā layavijñānajñāna saṃyogasvabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ, pratyekaikamāsena garbhotpādasva- bhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ, matsyādidaśāvasthāsvabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ, catuḥkāyacaturvaśuddhisva- bhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ catasṛbhiḥ sambodhibhiḥ garbhotpattisvabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ, ṣaṭśatā- dhikaikaviṃśatisahasraśvāsa - sa prati nāḍīsahasra-ṣaṣṭho 'ttaratriśata sandhipradeśa- ṣaḍdhātu- ṣaḍrasa - ṣaḍindriya ṣaḍviṣaya - ṣaṭkarmendriyaviṣaya-ṣavijñāna - ṣaṭsaṃskāra-ṣaḍvedanā- ṣaṭsaṃjñā - ṣaḍrūpa - ṣaḍjñāna - ṣaṭcakra- ṣaṇmaṇḍalādha dhaṃ ' svabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ, nābhyabjā' didaleṣu nāsārandhrayoḥ pañcaskandha- pañcadhātu- vāmasavyamaṇḍalapravāhasvabhāva- tayā'vasthitaḥ, madhyamāyāṃ sakalatantranirgamasvabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ prāṇādivāyusvabhāva- tayā'vasthitaḥ, dhātubhyo devatācihnotpādasvabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ, candrārkāgnyariṣṭasvabhāva- tayā'vasthitaḥ karmavipākaṣaḍgatiṣūtpādasvabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ, nāḍikādigrahaṇacaraṇa- cchedasvabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ |

|| iti jñānapaṭale
|| paṭale ] tantra deśanoddeśaḥ ata uktādanena krameṇa pañcapaṭalasvabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ kālacakro bhagavānatrā- bhidheyaḥ'
asya pratipādakaṃ paṭalasamūhaṃ tantrarājamabhidhānam
anayorabhidheyā- bhidhānayoḥ parasparaṃ vācyavācakalakṣaṇaḥ sambandhaḥ
vācyo bhagavān kālacakraḥ pañcapaṭala- svabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ vācakaṃ kālacakramabhidhānaṃ pañcapaṭalātmakam
atha ādibuddho bhagavān vācyaḥ
vācakamā dibuddhamabhidhānamiti vācyavācakalakṣaṇaḥ
abhidheyābhidhāna- sambandhaḥ
ato maṇḍala praveśāllaukikābhiṣekadānanena puṇyasambhārārthakaraṇaṃ prayojanam,
mahāprajñācaturthaṃ lokottarābhiṣekadānaniranvayamahāmudrāsiddhilābhāya puṇyajñānasambhārārthaṃkaraṇaṃ prayojana prayojanamiti
etānyevābhidheyalakṣaṇānyabhisaṃvīkṣya vaineyajanānāṃ niyamarahitānāṃ svacittābhi- prāyeṇeti
niyamaḥ śrāvakakoṭisaṃvaraḥ pañca kāmopabhogarahitaḥ śīlasaṃvara iti, tena rahitā niyamarahitāḥ, teṣāṃ svacittābhiprāyaḥ pañca kāmopabhogābhilāṣaḥ, tathā dvīndriyeṇa mahāsukhābhilāṣa iti svacittābhiprāyo niyamarahitānāmiti
anena svacittābhi- prāyeṇeha janmani buddhatvaphalapradamiti
iha janmani manuṣyajanmani buddhatva phalapradaṃ tantrarājam, na punardevādipañcagatiṣu janmani, tatkasya hetoḥ ?
akarmabhūmijātitvāditi
iha devādīnāmapi manuṣyajanmalābhād buddhatvaphalapradaṃ bhaviṣyatīti niyamo bhagavataḥ
puṇyajñānasambhārābhyāṃ ṣaḍdhātvātmakena mahāpuruṣapuṅgavene 'ti
na cānyathā kukarmaṇi manuṣyajanmanīha buddhatvaphalapradaṃ bhaviṣyatīti niyamastathāgatasya athāsmin mantrayāne buddhabhagavatoktam
tadyathā- caṇḍālaveṇukārādyāḥ pañcānantaryakāriṇaḥ
janmanīhaiva buddhāḥ syurmantracaryānucāriṇaḥ
// iti bhagavato vākyaṃ satyam
iha janmani yat prākkṛtaṃ pañcānantaryādikaṃ raudraṃ karma tadasmin mantrayāne mahāmudrākṣarasukhasamādhinā dhvaṃsayitvā'parāgantukākuśalāpraveśāya vajradharmodayagṛhe caturmāravighnādipraveśadvāreṣu maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣādibodhipākṣika - dharmakapāṭaṃ datvā tatra vajrasattvaṃ sādhayitvā mahāmudrayā sārddhaṃ pañcānantaryakāriṇo'pīha janmani buddhatvaphalalābhino bhavantīti tathāgataniyamaḥ, na punarmantrayāne praviṣṭāḥ santaḥ pañcānantaryādikaṃ raudraṃ karma kurvanto'pīha janmani buddhatvaphalalābhino bhavantīti tathāgatasammatam

bhāṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- dvādaśasāhasrikamiti
caturaśītisahasrādhikatrilakṣākṣarasamūhaṃ dvātriṃśadakṣarānuṅgapā' dvātriṃśadakṣarānuṣṭubhaḥ dvādaśasāhasrikamiti paramādibuddhamiti paramādibuddhā- abhidheyavācakatvāt, asyābhidheyasyābhidhānasvabhāvatayā'vasthitatvāditi paramādibuddhaṃ kālacakram, kālacakrābhidheyavācakatvāt kālacakrābhidhānamiti niranvayamiti niranvayābhidheyavācakatvāt ihānvayaḥ prajñāpakṣa upāyapakṣaśca so'nvayo bhinnapakṣo nirasto yasmāt tat tantraṃ niranvayam prajñopāyātmakaṃ yogatantramityadvayamucyate jineneti iha prayogā pāyātmakābhidheye tantrarāje prajñopāyapakṣabhedāt prajñopāyātmakābhi- dheyābhāvo bhavati, prajñopāyātmakābhidheyābhāvāt advayajñānābhāvaḥ advayajñānābhāvād buddhatvasyāpyabhāvaḥ, buddhatvābhāvāt saṃsāra iti ihābhidhānābhidheyasambandhena vinā prajñopāyātmako'dvayayogo vācyavāca kalakṣaṇo na bhavati yogatantra upāyanāmni na ca yogaśabda upāyārthavācakaḥ prajñārthavācako vā yogaśabdaḥ prajñopāyārthavācaka iti tathā cāha- yogo nopāyakāyena naikayā prajñayā bhavet prajñopāyasamāpattiryoga uktastathāgataiḥ ||
ato yasmin tantre prajñopāyātmako'bhidheyo bhavati, tat tantraṃ na prajñātantraṃ nopāya- tantraṃ paramārthataḥ lokasaṃvṛtyā daśajñānādibhedena dhātuskandhaviśuddhitaḥ, prajñopāyapakṣa ukto mṛdusattvāśayavaśāt tathāgateneti evaṃ yoginīnāmnyapi iha yoge śīlamasyā- stīti yogī, evaṃ yoginī ca ato yogatantraṃ yoginītantraṃ ca bhavati, parāpekṣikatvā- diti tasmāt prajñopāyātmakaṃ tantraṃ yogatantraṃ niranvayaṃ kālacakraṃ paramārthasatyata iti vajradharajñānakāyasākṣibhūtayā nāmasaṅgītyāliṅi gatamiti
ādibuddhābhidhānatvāt iha yathā nāmasaṅgītiratītānāgatapratyutpannaistathāgatairbhāṣitā bhāṣiṣyate bhāṣyate tathā- dibuddhamapi ādiśabdo'nādinidhanārthaḥ anādikāle anādibuddhadeśitaṃ deśayiṣyate deśyata iti naikena śākyamuninā dīpaṅkaratathāgatenāpīti atra vajrapāṇirāha - yātītairbhāṣitā buddhairbhāṣiṣyante hyanāgatāḥ pratyutpannāśca sambuddhāḥ yāṃ bhāṣante māyājāle mahātantre yā cāsmin mahāvajradharairhṛṣṭarameye maṃntradhāribhiḥ punaḥ punaḥ ||
saṃpragīyate ||
iti (nā sa a pa ) dvātriṃśadakṣarānuṅgapā gha pustake idaṃ vākyaṃ nāsti brahmatvasyā ekayā gha pustake 'nopāyatantra iti nāsti bho sucandrarājādhyeṣitena paramādibuddhamiti bho dvādaśasahasramiti caturaśītisahasrādhikatrilakṣākṣarasamūhaṃ dvātriṃśadakṣarānuṣṭapād dvādaśasāhasrikamiti niranvayamiti abhin paṭale tantra deśanoddeśaḥ ato nāmasaṅgītikādhyeṣaṇavacanāt sarvatathāgatairmantrayānaṃ deśitamiti
iha yatsamāje dīpaṅkaraśākyamunimadhye na kenacit tathāgatena mantrayānaṃ deśitam' iti, tat tena kālena tena samayeneti āryaviṣayadharmadeśanākālena, āryaviṣayaparṣado na deśitam, caturvarṇābhimānābhavya sattvāśayavaśāditi na punaranyakālenānyaviṣaye lokadhātuparṣadi na deśitamiti atastathāgatapravacanāt sarvatathāgatā yānatrayadeśakāḥ, anyathā sarvajñatābhāvo vajrayānādeśakatvāditi ataḥ sarve tathāgatā yānatrayadeśakāścaturaśīti- sahasra 'dharmaskandhadeśakāḥ sattvāśayavaśāditi
ato vajradharajñānakāyasākṣibhūtayā nāmasaṅgītyāliṅgitamiti sarvatantra rājeṣu vajrapadasākṣibhūtamiti
iha vajrayāne sattvāśayavaśena sarvatantra- rājeṣu vajrapadaṃ guptam, caturthaṃ tatpunastatheti tathāgatavacanāt caturthaṃ tṛtīyaṃ na bhavati, caturthamiti vacanāt tatpunastatheti vacanāt prajñājñānaṃ tadeva caturthamato bhagavato vacanāt vajrapadaṃ guptamiti sarvatantrarājeṣu vajrapadaṃ prakaṭaṃ na bhavati, gurupāramparya- krameṇāvagantavyam; tantraṃ tantrāntareṇa bodhavyamiti tathāgatavacanāt iha mantranaye dvidhā vajrapadam — ekaṃ laukikasaṃvṛtyā dvitīyaṃ paramārthataḥ tayorlokasaṃvṛtyā vajrapadaṃ māraṇādau samayasiddhidāyakam, paramārthasatyena vajrapadaṃ caturthaṃ tatpunastathā mahāmudrāsiddhi - phaladāyakamiti ata evāsminnādibuddhe vajrapadaṃ prakaṭamuddeśanirdeśapratinirdeśairbhagavatā prakāśitam asyaiva sādhanāya mahāmudrābhāvanā dhūmādinimittamārgaḥ prakāśitaḥ śūnye ekāgramanaḥ kṛtvā dinamekaṃ parīkṣayediti ato bhagavato vacanāt paramādibuddhe vajrapadaṃ mahāmudrābhāvanāmārge dhūmādikaḥ prakaṭaḥ, na gurupāramparyakrameṇāgataḥ, nādhiṣṭhāni gurvājñayeti asya ca mārgasya pratyayo'sti, arhāna śaṃ dinamekaṃ parīkṣayediti bhagavato vacanāt iha na cānyanmantrādisādhanaṃ dhūmādinimittaṃ vihāya dinaikena yoginā parīkṣaṇīyam nimittamapi tridhā - ādinimittam, madhyanimittam, antanimittañceti ādinimittaṃ dhūmādimārgaḥ, ṣaḍaṅgayogena viśva niṣpattirakṣaṇa- lābhaḥ madhyanimittaṃ paramākṣarakṣaṇairaṣṭādaśaśatairādibhūmilābhaḥ, pañcā- bhijñā'dṛṣṭvā 'rthasandarśanaṃ laukikasiddhiprāptiriti animittaṃ ' buddhatvaṃ vajradharatvamekaviṃśatisahasraiḥ ṣaṭśatādhikaiḥ paramākṣarakṣaṇairdvādaśadaśabhūmilābhāt mahāmudrā- siddhiriti etad vajrapadādikaṃ nimittapūrvakaṃ prakaṭaṃ tantrarāje paramādibuddhe bhagavatā deśitam ataḥ sarvatantrarājeṣu vajrapadasākṣibhūtaṃ tantraṃ tantrāntarānveṣaka - nāmiti ṅa na deśitam bho Ses iti ka pustake 'sahasra' śabdo nāsti ka tantrānusāreṇa; bho rGyud gSan Dag Gis (tantrāntareṇa) bho Byin Gyis brLbs Pa (adhiṣṭhitaṃ ) kha ni kha kṣarakṣaṇa; bho hGur Ba Med Pahi sKad Cig ( akṣarakṣaṇa ) bho adṛṣṭārtha bho antanimittaṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu-

anyathāṣṭaśate varṣagate sati yuṣmatputrapautrādayo mlecchadharme pravṛtti kṛtvā sambhalādiṣaṇṇavatimahāviṣayeṣu mlecchadharmadeśanāṃ kariṣyanti
mlecchadevatāviṣavitvā' (vismillāha ) mantreṇa kattikayā grīvāyāṃ paśu hatvā tatasteṣāṃ svadevatāmantreṇāhatānāṃ paśūnāṃ māṃsa bhakṣayiṣyanti, svakarmaṇā mṛtānāṃ māṃsamabhakṣaṃ kariṣyanti, so'pi dharmo yuṣmākaṃ pramāṇam, “yāgādheyaśavaḥ' (yāgārthāḥ paśavaḥ) sṛṣṭāḥ iti smṛtivacanāt mlecchadharmavedadharmayoviśeṣo nāsti prāṇātipātataḥ
tasmāt yusmat-kule putrapautrādayaḥ teṣāṃ mlecchānāṃ pratāpaṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃgrāme māradevatāvatāraṃ vā'nāgatedhvani aṣṭavarṣaśate gate sati mlecchā bhaviṣyanti
teṣu mleccheṣu jāteṣu satsu ṣaṇṇavatikoṭigrāmanivāsino'pi caturvarṇādayaḥ sarve mlecchā bhaviṣyanti, mahājanā yena gataḥ sa panthāḥ iti brahma ṛṣivacanāt
iha mlecchadharme vedadhamaṃ'pi devatāpitra (ya) thaṃ prāṇātipātaḥ kartavyaḥ, kṣatradharme'pi ca, tarpayitvā pitṛn devān khādan māṃsaṃ na doṣabhāg iti brāhmaṇavacanāt; tathā doṣaṃ tatra na paśyāmi yo duṣṭa duṣṭamācaret” iti
*. gha
pustake pāyaśa: ' ṣaṇṇavati' sthāne 'navati' iti pāṭhaḥ
evaṃ vedadharmaṃ pramāṇīkṛtya mlecchadharmaparigrahaṃ kariṣyanti
tena kāraṇenānāgate'dhvani ' mlecchadharmāpraveśāya yuṣmadbhyo mayā niyamo dattaḥ, tasmād bhavadbhirmamājñā kartavyā iti
evamuktenānena yaśo rājño vacanena sadaṇḍājñāsahitena śirasi vidyutpatitairiva brahma ṛṣibhiḥ sūryaratha ukta:- he sūryaratha, tvaṃ yaśo narendraṃ vijñāpaya - nāsmākaṃ vajra- kulābhiṣekadharmapravṛttirvedoktajātidharmaṃ vihāyeti
tasmād vayaṃ tavājñayā varamāryaviṣayaṃ gamiṣyāmaḥ, śītāhimavatordakṣiṇaṃ laṅkādīpāntaram iti
teṣāṃ brahma ṛṣīṇāṃ vacanena sūryaratho yaśo narendra vijñāpayati - he mahārājādhirāja, parameśvara, dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣa- lakṣaṇāśītyanuvyañjanāṅgaparipūrṇa, śrīśākyakulatilaka, paramakāruṇika, karuṇāṃ kuruṣva kuśala meṃ pravṛttānām
athāsmābhiravaśyaṃ tavājñā kartavyā
tadā vayaṃ vajraku- lābhiṣekapravṛtti na kariṣyāmaḥ
tavājñayā varaṃ śotānadīdakṣiṇe himavato laṅkādvopasya madhye āryaviṣayaṃ gamiṣyāmaḥ iti
atha sūryarathavacanāt yaśorājā āha - śīghraṃ sambhalaviṣayānnirgacchantu bhavanto yena śītānadyuttare ṣaṇṇavatikoṭigrāmanivāsinaḥ sarve sattvāḥ prāṇātipātādyakuśa- lakarmapathān parityajya kālacakrabhagavato'dhiṣṭhānena samyakjñānamārgalābhino bhaviṣyanti iti
tena yaśorājājñayā sarve amī brahma ṛṣayaḥ kalāpagrāmānnirgatāḥ ; daśame dine vanāntaraṃ praviṣṭāḥ
teṣāṃ vanamadhye praviṣṭānāṃ pañcābhijñābalena yaśorājñā jñātam, yathaiṣāṃ brahma ṛṣīṇāṃ āryaviṣaye gamanena ṣaṇṇavatikoṭigrāmanivāsināṃ sarvasattvānāṃ vaiṣamyacittaṃ bhaviṣyati, iha yena kāraṇena vajrayānoktaḥ samyagjñānamārgo na bhavati, tena kāraṇena amī ṛṣayaḥ śroyaśorājño bhayena svasthānaṃ parityajya svakuṭumbādi gṛhītvā āryaviṣayaṃ gatāḥ
sarva mokṣārthina iti kṣatriyādayo janāścintayanto durbhagā bhaviṣyanti, gambhīroṃ- dāradharmābhājane ' abhavyacittāt
evaṃ ' 'sarvajanānāṃ svacittābhiprāyaṃ jñātvā yaśonarendraḥ sarvaviṣṇubrahmarudrakulamohanaṃ nāma samādhi samāpannaḥ
tena samādhinā devatādhiṣṭhānabalena sarve te ṛṣayastasminneva vane mohitāḥ santastasmin vananivāsibhi sa ( rā ) barādibhiḥ sarva ṛṣayo bandhayitvā punareva mahāmaṇḍala gṛhamānītāḥ, yaśonarendrasya pādamūle prakṣiptāḥ santaḥ prabuddhā yaśonarendraṃ paśyanti
tadeva maṇḍalagṛhaṃ malayodyāne taṃ dṛṣṭvā vismayamāpannā idaṃ vacanamāhuḥ - aho mahāṃścaryamidam, vayaṃ mahāvanādaprabuddhāḥ santaḥ kenānītā mahāmaṇḍala- gṛhe ? ityevametadvacanaṃ śrutvā brahma ṛṣīṇāṃ yaśorājño mantriṇā nirmitakāyena sāgarama- tinoktam - he sūryarathādayo brahma - ṛṣayo mā vismayaṃ kuruta
ayaṃ yaśorājā dvīpāntara paṭale ] sunasā deśakādhyeṣakamūlatantralaghutantrasambandhoddeśaḥ prādeśiko na bhavati
ko'pi mahābodhisattvo yuṣmākamanugrahārthī buddhādhiṣṭhānenābhūt, tasmādasya pādayoḥ śaraṇaṃ gatvā āddhibuddhatantrarāje laukikalokottarasiddhisādhanamārgābhi- ṣekādhyeṣaṇaṃ kuruta iti
atha tena sāgaramativacanena buddhādhiṣṭhānena sūryarathādayo brahma- ṛṣayaḥ prabuddhā idaṃ vacanamāhuḥ - sādhu sādhu sāgaramate, yena te vacanenāsmākaṃ cittaprabo- dho'bhūt, tasmādidānīṃ ratnatrayaśaraṇaṃ gatvā kālacakratantrarāje laukikalokottarasiddhi -
sādhanamārgābhiṣekādhyeṣaṇāṃ kuryyāḥ', sakalasattvānāṃ samyaksambuddhatvalābhāye haiva janmani” iti
evamuktvā taiḥ ṛṣibhiḥ brahmakule sūryaratho rājā'mantritaḥ - he sūryaratha, tvaṃ vedādiśāstrakapustaka sarvalaukika lokottaraśāstraprameyagrāhakahṛdayaḥ, tenāsmākamadhyeṣaṇāvaca- nena yaśorājño'dhyeṣaṇāṃ kuru
vayamapi maṇḍalapūrvaṅgamaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatvā sarve'dhyeṣaṇāṃ kariṣyāmaḥ
atha tena brahma ṛṣivacanena sūryaratho ratnasuvarṇamayaiḥ puṣpaimaṃṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā yaśonare- ndrasya pādamūle ratnapuṣpāñjaliṃ prakṣipya jānuyugmena bhūgatena hastayugmena śirasi gatena brahma- ṛṣibhiḥ sārddhaṃ yaśaḥ pādau praṇamya dakṣiṇaṃ jānumaṇḍalaṃ pṛthivyāṃ saṃsthāpya lalāṭe karapuṭaṃ datvā yaśonarendramadhyeṣayati- deśayatu bhagavāṃstantrarājamādibuddham, yasmin buddhatvāya pañcānantaryakāriṇo'pi vyākṛtā ihaiva janmani vajradharabhagavatā paramākṣarasukhena saṃgṛhītā mahāmudrālābhino vyākṛtāḥ
iyaṃ ṣaḍdvādaśasāhasrikāṃ ādibuddhaṃ sucandrarājñastathāgatena idaṃ yada deśitam tadevālpagranthenādibuddhamalpatantrarājaṃ saṅgīti kṛtvā deśayatu brahma ṛṣīṇāṃ śāsye iti
atha sūryarathādhyeṣaṇaṃ śrutvā brahma ṛṣīṇāmadhimukticittavaśāt tathāgatādhiṣṭhānabalena laghusaṅgītikāra katvena sragdharāvṛtteḥ sarvajñadeśakādisaṃgrāha- kaistantrarājaṃ deśayati
teṣāṃ ca suśabdavādināṃ suśabdagrahavināśāya arthaśaraṇatāmāśritya kacidavṛtte'pa- śabdaḥ kacid vṛtte yatibhaṅgaḥ kacidavibhaktikaṃ padam kacidvarṇasvaralopaḥ kacid vṛtte dīrgho hrasvaḥ, hrasvo'pi dīrghaḥ kacit pañcamyarthe saptamī, caturthyarthe ṣaṣṭhī kutracit parasmai- padini dhātāvātmanepadam, ātmanepadini parasmaipadam kacidekavacane bahuvacanam, bahuvacane ekavacanam, pulliṅge napuṃsakam, napuṃsake pulliṅgam kacit tālavyaśakāre dantyamūrddhanyau kacit mūrddhānye dantyatālavyau kacid dantye tālavyamūrddhanyau evamanye'pyanusartavyāstantra- deśakopadeśakeneti
tathā mūlatantre bhagavānāha - sucandra sarvabuddhānāṃ deyaṃ nityeṣṭavastukam śiṣyebhyaśca re gurūṇāñca bhāryāduhitṛputrakam gandho bhavati medinyāṃ toye rūpaṃ raso'nale vāyau sparśo'kṣare kvacid śabdadharmadhātumahānaye * nabhe vimalaprabhāyāṃ gandhadhūpādidīpebhiḥ khānapānādivāsasaiḥ pūjayitvā mahāmudrāṃ gurau dadati satsutaḥ [

evaṃ ṭokāyāmapi suśabdābhimānanāśā likhitavyaṃ mayā'rthaśaraṇatāmāśrityeti
atha yena yena prakāreṇa kulavidyāsuśabdābhimānakṣayo bhavati, tena tena prakāreṇārthaśaraṇatāmāśritya buddhānāṃ bodhi- sattvānāṃ dharmadeśanā deśabhāṣāntareṇa śabdaśāstrabhāṣāntareṇa mokṣārtham iti
mūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ dvādaśasāhasikāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ deśakālādhyeṣakamūlata ntralaghutantrasambandhoddeśaḥ tṛtīyaḥ
mo nāsti deśakādhyeṣakasādhanoddeśaḥ
idānīṃ mañjuśrī nirmita śonarendrasūrya rathadeśakādhyeṣakādisambandhādinā laghutantra- rājaṃ vitanomīti
iha mahāśrīmati kālacakamaṇḍalagṛhe pūrvadvā rāvasāne mahāmaṇi- avasanne bho ratnamaṇḍape mahāratnasiṃhāsanastho devāsuranāganirmita kāyaiḥ ṣaṇṇavatimahārājakulaprasūte- mahāratnamukuṭabaddheḥ koṭikoṭigrāmādhipatibhirnamaskṛtacaraṇāravindaḥ sarvatathāgataprajñāmūttiḥ sūryarathādhyeṣito yaśonarendraḥ sūryarathamidamavocat - sādhu sādhu sūryaratha yena tvaṃ brahma- ṛṣikulādīnāṃ sarvasattvānāṃ samyaksambuddhatvamārgalābhāya paramādibuddhatantrarājasadbhāvaṃ śrotuṃ mattaḥ samudyataḥ tena sādhu te
idaṃ yat paramādibuddhatantrarājasadbhāvaṃ laukikalokottara- siddhisādhakaṃ kālacakravajrayogaṃ tvayā pṛṣṭaṃ tatsarvamahaṃ deśayāmi saṅgītikārakatvena tvamapyekāgramanāḥ śṛṇu prāgvyākṛtatathāgatādhiṣṭhānabalena laghutantrarājasadbhāvaṃ prajñopā- yātmakaṃ yogaṃ śra dharāvṛttasaṅgītyā mahātantrarājāduddhāryamāṇam samyaksambuddha- sucandradeśakādhyeṣakasambandhena mayā yaśo rājñe iti
atha mañjuśrībhagavānnirmitakāyo yaśonarendro deśakā disaṃgrahavṛttaṃ prathamaṃ paramādibuddhāt tathāgatena vyākṛtamāha sarvajñaṃ jñānakāyaṃ dinakaravapuṣaṃ padmapatrāyatākṣaṃ baddhaṃ siṃhāsanasyaṃ suravaranamitaṃ mastakena praṇamya pṛcchedrājā sucandraḥ karakamalapuṭa sthāpayitvottamāṅge yogaṃ śrīkālacakre kaliyugasamaye muktihetornarāṇām māśritya '

iha tantrarāje deśakādīnāṃ saṃgrahārthamidamādivṛttaṃ bhagavatā saṅgītamiti
atra deśakādayaḥ deśakaḥ,sthānam, mahāparṣat, adhyeṣakaḥ, dharmadeśanā, prayojanaprayojanaprayojanamiti
eṣu deśakasaṃgrahastāvat sarvajñaṃ jñānakāyaṃ dinakaravapuṣaṃ padmapatrāyatākṣaṃ buddhamityebhistrayo- viśatyakṣarairdeśakasaṃgrahaḥ kṛto bhagavatā
siṃhāsanasthamityebhiḥ pañcākṣaraiḥ sthānasaṃgrahaḥ kṛtaḥ
suravaranamitamityebhiḥ saptākṣarairmahāparṣat saṃgrahaḥ kṛtaḥ
mastakena praṇamya pṛcchedrājā sucandraḥ karakamalapuṭaṃ sthāpayitvottamāṅga ityebhiraṣṭāviṃśatyakṣarairadhyeṣakasaṃgrahaḥ kṛtaḥ
yogaṃ śrīkālacakre kaliyugasamaye ityebhiścaturdaśākṣaraiḥ dharmadeśanāsaṃgrahaḥ kṛtaḥ
muktihetornarāṇāmityebhiḥ saptākṣaraiḥ prayojanaprayojanaprayojanasaṃgrahaḥ ityebhiryathānu- evaṃ- bhā krameṇa caturaśītyakṣarairdeśakasthānamahāparṣad adhyeṣakadharmadeśanāprayojanaprayojanaprayojanasaṃgrahaḥ mūlatantrarājāduddhṛtya mañjuśrībhagavatā tathāgatavyākṛteneti
dhara tathāgatena vyākṛta iti nanu sarvatantrarājeṣu evaṃ mayā śrutam ityādinā vajradharabhagavato 'vijahāra'- sthānanirdeśastathāgatenoktaḥ, kathamidaṃ tantrarājaṃ sarvajña ityādinā evaṃ mayā - ādi rahitaṃ buddhabhagavatā sandeśitaṃ bhavati
evaṃ mayā - ādi - vijahāra - sthāna nirdeśābhāvādidaṃ tantrarājaṃ buddhabhagavatā deśitaṃ na bhavatīha kasyacit saṃjñāvyañjanaśaraṇāśritasya vacanaṃ bhaviṣyati tasmāducyate iha yadvaktavyamanāgate ' dhvani bālajanairvyañjanasaṃjñā- śaraṇāśritaiḥ sarvatantrarājeṣu evaṃ mayā śrutam ityādinā vajradharabhagavato vijahāra- sthānanirdeśastathāgatenoktaḥ tanna, kasmāt dharmadeśakasyārthaśaraṇatāmāśritatvāt, saṅgītikārāṇāmapi nānādeśabhāṣāntareṇa saṅgītikaraṇāditi
iha yadi evaṃ mayā śrutam ityādinā saṃskṛtavacanena kaṇṭhatālvādiprayatnato janitena prādeśikena tathāgatasya dharmadeśanā, tadā caturaśītisahasradharma skandhānanekakālairapi tathāgato deśayituṃ na śaknoti saṅgītikārakaśca likhituṃ prādeśikaikasaṃskṛtavacanāditi
ihānantanirmāṇakā yābhāvāda- anantānantalokadhātuṣu anantānantasattvarutairyugapacca dharmadeśako na syāt, caturaśītisahasra- dharmaskandhadeśanābhāvāt sarvajño na bhavati ?
na caivam, ihāpramāṇo buddhaḥ, apramāṇo dharmaḥ, apramāṇaḥ saṅghaḥ ; atastathāgatavacanāt naikaḥ sarvajño deśakaḥ
naikā sarvajñabhāṣā yā sarva- sattvarutairarthapratipādikā; naiko vijahārasthānanirdeśaḥ
naikaḥ śrāvakasaṅgho'dhyeṣakaḥ tathāgata- syeti
iha sattvānāṃ nānādhimuktivaśādanekaḥ sarvajñaḥ, anekā sarvajñabhāṣā, anekaṃ vijahāra- sthānam, anekā adhyeṣakāḥ, anekā dharmadeśaneti

( ) evamādinā vajrapāṇeradhyeṣaṇā; evaṃ māyājāle'pi vajrapāṇiradhyeṣakaḥ prasiddho nāma- saṅgītyāmiti |
iha paramādibuddho adhyeṣakaḥ sattvānāṃ kleśanāśāya bhagavatā coditaḥ san— kāyavātcittayogena śāstuḥ pādāmbujadvayam | ratnapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya sapuṣpāñjalinā punaḥ ||
śirasā jānuyugmena bhūgatena tato bhūbhyāṃ samasthāpya dakṣiṇaṃ praṇamya ca | jānmaṇḍalam ||
lalāṭe karapuṭāṃ datvā'dhyeṣaṇā kurute nṛpaḥ | deśayitvākhilaṃ śāstā sarvatantraniruttaram ||
ādibuddhaṃ sadā sarva siddhisandohalakṣaṇam | yogaṃ śrīkālacakre'sminnālike lisamanvite ||
ekakṣaṇābhisambuddhaṃ catubindudharaṃ tattvaṃ bhinnaṃ catuḥkṣaṇavibheditam | ṣoḍaśabhedataḥ ||
śūnyaṃ jñānaṃ ca binduṃ ca varaṃ vajradharaṃ mahat | pañcākṣaraṃ mahāśūnyaṃ binduśūnyaṃ ṣaḍakṣaram ||
buddhadevāsurāneva bāhye dehe pareṣu ca | puruṣaṃ prakṛtiṣvevaṃ pañcaviṃśati maṃ param ||
dehe viśvasya mānaṃ yat trailokyotpatikāraṇam | bhukti devāsurādīnāṃ sarvametat yathā sphuṭam ||
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ lokadhātu- itvevamādyadhyeṣaṇā sucandrarājñaḥ paramādibuddhe |
tathā yogānuviddhe viśvarūpiṇī āha- śrīviśvarūpiṇī natvā pṛcchate vajrabhairavam tantraṃ yogānuviddhaṃ kiṃ vajrasattvaḥ paraṃ sukham ” ||
ityādyadhyeṣaṇā yogānuviddhe |
tathā lakṣābhidhāne vajravārāhī āha- praṇamya vajravārāhī herukaṃ tribhavātmakam | tantraṃ lakṣābhidhānaṃ kiṃ vajrasattvaḥ paraṃ sukham ||
ityādyadhyeṣaṇā lakṣābhidhāne |
evamanyeṣvapi tantrarājeṣu yoginā'dhyeṣaṇā jñātavyeti |
ato bhagavato'nantadharmadeśakatvāt borddharna vaktavyam - evaṃ mayā śrutam ityādinā sarvatantrarājeṣu vijahārasthānanirdeśa iti |
anena prādeśika saṃskṛtavacanena buddho'pi prādeśiko bhavati, sarvasattvarutasvabhāvinyā sarvajñabhāṣayā vinā |
ihāryaviṣaye śabdavādināṃ torthikānāṃ paṇḍitānāmabhimānaṃ dṛṣṭvā bālamatīnāṃ bauddhānāmabhiprāyaḥ, yathā brahmahariharādayaḥ saṃskṛtavaktāro brāhmaṇavaiṣṇavaśaivādīnāmiṣṭa- devatā, tathā'smadīyā iṣṭadevatā buddhabodhisattvāḥ saṃskṛtavaktāro bhavantīti |
iha na ca te anena prādeśikasaṃskṛtavacanena sarvasattvarutairdharmadaśakāḥ saṅgītikārakā bhavanti,
buddhabodhisattvāḥ sarvajñabhāṣayā vinā
ato devajātipratibaddhā prādeśikā buddhabodhi- sattvānāṃ na syāditi, nānāsattvarutadharmadeśakatvāt
iha mantranaye ekasaṃjñā na bhāvaḥ, ekasyāpi bhāvasyānekāḥ saṃjñāḥ saṃjñābahutvāt
na caikā saṃjñā pradhānā sthāt, sarvasaṃjñānātmakā' bhāvapratipādakatvāt
yathā strī - nārī - yuvatītyādīnāṃ naikā strīsaṃjñā pradhānā syāt, sarvāsāṃ stanakerāvatībhāvapratipādakatvāt; tathā ekāra- rahasya-padma-dharmodaya khadhātu- mahāsukhāvāsasiṃghā sana- bhaga-guhya-saṃjñānāṃ madhye naikā ekārasaṃjñā pradhānā, sarvāsāṃ sarvākāraśūnyatāpratipādakatvāt
tathā vaṃkāra- mahāsurata mahārāga sahaja-paramākṣara bindu-tattva-jñāna-viśuddhacitta-saṃjñānāṃ madhye na ekā vaṃkārasaṃjñā pradhānā, sarvāsāṃ mahāmudrā - sahajānandākṣarasukhapratipādakatvāditi
evamekāra-vaṃkārayoḥ sarvākāravaropetā śūnyatā, sarvadharmanirālambakaruṇā'bhinnabodhicitta - bhāvapratipādakatvāt
evaṃkāro vajrasattvo bodhicittaṃ kālacakraḥ ādibuddha: prajñopāyātmako yogaḥ jñeyajñānātmakaḥ advayaḥ anādinidhanaḥ śāntaḥ samājaḥ saṃvara eva- mādyanekasaṃjñābhiḥ prajñopāyātmako'dvayo yogo niranvayo yoginā'vagantavya iti
iha yat samājādau tantrarāje ekāro vaṃkārastantrādau bhagavatā niruktirūpeṇa nirdiṣṭaḥ, tad devānāṃ devarutena pācanāya saṅgītikāreṇāpi tantrādau likhitam; ato'syākṣaradvayasya samājādau paṭale ] deśakādhyeṣakasādhanoddeśaḥ tantrarāje luptirneṣyate
na cedakṣaradvayaṃ bhagavatā tālvādinā deśitam, na cāśrutvā saṅgītikāreṇa likhitamiti
ato'syākṣaradvayasya nītārtha ucyate- pūrvo'kāraḥ khadhāturguhyakamalam, tato visargaḥ sūryo rajaḥ, tato'kāro rāhuvijñāna sukhādhiṣṭhitam, tato'nusvāraḥ candraḥ śukram
anayorakārayoḥ khadhātu-rāhuvijñānayormadhye visarga: sūrya: rajaḥ itvamā 'padyate
tato visarge sūryarajasi utvamāpanne sati paramārthasatye guṇābhāvaḥ, guṇābhāvād yaṇādeśaḥ syāditi
para-akāreṇa rāhuvijñānena sahavatvam, anusvāraḥ candraśukeṇa saṃyogaḥ, ato vaṃkāraḥ
tathā pūrvo'kāraḥ khadhāturguhyakamalaṃ saptamyanto akāraḥ para-ikāreṇa saha guṇī bhavati, ata ekāraḥ
asminnekāre khadhātau ādhāre vaṃkāra ādheya visargākārānusvārasūrya rāhucandraraja ālayavijñānaśukrātmako madhye bāhye dehe pare cāvagantavya iti
iha mūlatantre bhagavānāha - ekāre madhyavaṃkāraḥ sarvabuddhasukhālayaḥ
khadhātau vajrasattvo'yaṃ kāyavākacittayogataḥ
kāyo bindvandu śukraṃ ca vāgvisargo rajo raviḥ
cittākārastvamī proktā ekhadhātau vyavasthitaḥ

vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ lokadhātu- ato'nantasaṃjñābhiḥ dharmadeśakatvād evaṃ mayā śrutam ityādinā deśitaṃ tantrarājaṃ tathāgatadeśitaṃ bhavati, rahasyetyādinā ' sarvajñādinā ca deśitaṃ tathāgatadeśitaṃ na bhavatīti bauddharna vaktavyam, svasiddhānte parasiddhānte doṣagrahaṇāt ṣaṣṭhī mūlāpattirbhavati
tasmāt tantrāntare pūrvāparasambandhaṃ jñātvā guṇadoṣāvagantavyāḥ (vyau )
anyathā'dṛṣṭadoṣagrahaṇād- vīcigamanaṃ bhavati duṣṭānāryāṇāṃ tathāgatahṛdayabāhyabhūtānāṃ viṣayendriyopabhogāsaktānāṃ mithyāpralāpināmiti
evamuktakrameṇa paramāgamayuktyā pūrvāparāviruddhaṃ sarvajñetyādinā paramādibuddhaṃ tantrarājaṃ bhagavatā sandeśitamiti

[lokadhātu- ataḥ sarvajño buddho bhagavān laukika lokottaradharmadeśako devāsuramanuṣyāṇāṃ śāstā paramakāruṇiko'kāraṇavatsalaḥ sarvāvaraṇanirmukta iti

nānyaḥ kaścit trisāha- sramahāsāhasra lokadhātuṣu anantānantasattvānāmanantānantarutakairyugapañca laukikalokottara- dharmaṃdeśakaḥ
kasmāt ?
prādeśikaikadevajātau sambhūtatvāditi, prādeśikaikasaṃskṛtavacanāta
na teṣāṃ sā sarvajñabhāṣā nānādhimuktikānāṃ sattvānāṃ svasvabhāṣāntareṇa laukikalokottara- dharmaṃ pratipādayantī (tī) ti
iha martyaloke pratyakṣaṃ dṛśyate saṃskṛtabhāṣayā tairdeśitā gītā siddhāntapurāṇadharmāḥ sarve prādeśikāḥ caturvedāśca
kutaḥ brahmakṣatriyavedādhyayanataḥ
iha brāhmaṇakṣatriyābhyāṃ vedādhyayanaṃ kartavyam, na hi viṭśūdrādibhiriti
tathā gotāsiddhāntapurāṇadharmā brāhma- ṇakṣatriyaviṭaśūdraiścaturvarṇaiḥ śrotavyā brāhmaṇamukhataḥ pravajyāgrahaṇañca na kaivartādibhiriti
ato dharmapratiṣedhavacanāntairdeśitā dharmāḥ prādeśikāḥ sarvasattvopakāriṇo na bhavantīti
iha martye yena kāraṇena prādeśika saṃskṛtabhāṣayā nānādhimuktikānāṃ sattvānāṃ nānārutairloki- kalo[kottaradharmadeśanāṃ kartumaśaktāḥ tena viṭaśūdrādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaṃ kurvanti
iha martye viṭśūdrādibhinikṛṣṭayonijātairvedādhyayanaṃ na kartavyam, pravrajyādaṇḍagrahaṇañceti
“ekaḥ śabdaḥ suprayuktaḥ svarge kāmadhuk” iti mithyāhaṅkārābhi- bhūtānāmabhiprāyaḥ, sarvajñabhāṣā'bhāvāt
iha martye loke yadi sarve manuṣyā vedagītā- siddhāntatarkaśāstravido bhavanti śūdrādayaḥ, tadā brāhmaṇānāṃ ko gauravaṃ kariṣyati; vidyādharmajñānasādhāraṇaparijñānāt, sarveṣāṃ gṛhavāsināṃ saṃsārabhogāsaktānāṃ viśeṣaguṇābhā- vāt
iti jñātvā duṣṭa - ṛṣibhirdravyalubdhaiḥ saṃskṛtabhāṣayā gītāsiddhāntapurāṇādayo dharmāḥ pustake likhitāḥ vedāśca mukhapāṭhenādhyayanīyā iti niyamaḥ kṛto bālajanānāṃ mahāmohajanaka iti asarvajñadeśanābhiprāyaḥ
iha pūrvakāle vedagītāsiddhāntapurāṇa- dharmā na pustake likhitāḥ santi yatīnāṃ mukhe tiṣṭhanti tataḥ pañcakaṣāyakālavaśāt ādeyaśca; nante
asarvajñadeśanā asarvajñādeśanā mma deśakādhyeṣakasādhanoddeśaḥ pustake likhitāḥ |, prajñāhīnatvād yatibhiriti
iha prādeśikī hariharādīnāṃ dharma- deśanā bauddharnānumodanīyā sarvasattvakṛpayā rahitā, saṃsāraduḥkhadāyikī mithyāhaṅkāra- kāriṇī jātivādābhimāninīti
iha dhātuke ye sarvajñena sarvajñabhāṣayā deśitā dharmā nānāsattvabhāṣāntareṇa saṅgī- tikārakaiḥ pustake likhitāḥ, vedādilaukikārtha' pratipādakāḥ, yānatrayārthapratipādakāḥ, lokasaṃvṛtisatyena paramārthasatyena deśitāḥ, sarvasattvānāṃ śravaṇāyādhyayanāya ca tadadhimu- ktikāḥ sattvāstān sarvajñadeśitān caturaśītisahasradharmaskandhān laukikalokottarārthaprati- pādakān śṛṇvanti paṭhanti vācayanti dhārayanti parebhyaśca vistareṇa saṃprakāśayanti prati- ṣṭhāpayanti, pūjayanti nānāpuṣpairnānāgandhairnānādhūpairnānācūrṇairnānāvastrairnānāghaṇṭābhirnānā- patākābhirnānācāmarairnānāchatrairnānāvitānairnānāmuktāhārairnānāratnairnānāpradīpairnānāratnā bha[b]raṇaiḥ pūjayitvā tebhyaḥ pañcāṅgapraṇāmaṃ kurvanti
evaṃ te sarvajñadeśitā dharmāḥ paropakāriṇaḥ adhimuktivaśāt paropakārāya na kasyacijjātyajātivaśāt te vihitāḥ pratiṣedhitāstathāgatenetyadyāpi nāntardhānaṃ gatāḥ
tathā tiṣṭhatu tāvadanyaviṣayāntaram
iha tathāgatābhisambuddhe āryaviṣaye bhagavati parinirvṛte sati saṅgītikārakairyānatrayaṃ pustake likhitaṃ tathāgataniyamena piṭakatrayaṃ magadhabhāṣayā, sindhubhāṣayā sūtrāntam; saṃskṛtabhāṣayā pāramitānayaṃ mantra- nayam; tantratantrāntaraṃ saṃskṛtabhāṣayā prākṛtabhāṣayā apabhraṃśabhāṣayā asaṃskṛta śava rādimlecchabhāṣayā ityevamādiḥ sarvajñadeśito dharmaḥ saṅgītikāra kailikhitaḥ
vo ṭaviṣaye yānatrayaṃ vo ṭabhāṣayā likhitam, cīne cīnabhāṣayā mahācīne mahā- cīnabhāṣayā, pārasikadeśe pārasikabhāṣayā ; śītānadyuttare campakaviṣayabhāṣayā vānara- viṣayabhāṣayā suvarṇākhyaviṣayabhāṣayā; tathā nīlanadyuttare ruhaya viṣayabhāṣayā surasmā' viṣayabhāṣayā evaṃ koṭikoṭigrāmātmakeṣu ṣaṇṇavativiṣayeṣu ṣaṇṇavati- viṣayabhāṣayā likhitam
evaṃ dvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu svargamartyapātāleṣu nānāsattvarutaiḥ saṅgīti- kārakairyānatrayaṃ likhitamiti
śrāvakaiḥ śrāvakayānam, pratyekaiḥ pratyekayānam, bodhisattvaḥ pāramitāmahāyānam mantramahāyānam, hetuphalātmakaṃ nānāsaṅgītikārakaiḥ sattvānāṃ vaineyārthamiti
anayā nānāsaṅgītikārakairnānāviṣayabhāṣayā likhitāgamayuktyā vicāryamāṇo buddho bhagavān sarvajñaḥ sarvajñabhāṣayā dharmadeśakaḥ, nānyo hariharādīti'
atha hariharādīnāṃ madhye ebhirguṇaiḥ paripūrṇaḥ kaścidasti, so'pyasmākaṃ vandanīyaḥ pūjanīyo mānanīya iti sarvathā- lauklikārthe pustake atra 'nānādaśa:' iti adhikaḥ pāṭhaḥ ; tiṣṭhantu surammā; 'pustakāni likhitāni' ityabhipra etya ' pustake likhitāḥ' iti prayogaḥ

[lokadhātu- ataḥ sarvajño buddho bhagavān sarvasattvakāruṇikaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvarutairdharmadeśaka:, tathā sucandro vajrapāṇirdaśabhūmīśvaro'dhyeṣakaḥ saṅgītikāraka paramādibuddhe prasiddhaḥ

tatra yo lokasaṃvṛtyā nirdiṣṭaḥ sa neyārthaḥ, yaḥ paramārthataḥ sa nītārthaḥ
etau dvāvarthau gurūpadeśato'vagantavyau śiṣyaiḥ

iha yasmin pūrṇakṣaṇe sarvatathāgatā abhisambuddhāstasmāt kṣaṇāt sarvadharmāṇāṃ notpādo na sthitirna bhaṅgaḥ, niranvayatvāt

paṭale ] deśakādisaṃgrahoddeśaḥ moho'moha ityevamādi pakṣaḥ, parasparāpekṣikatvāditi

ānanda-parama-virama- sahajabhedaiścaturvidham
tataḥ kāyānando vāgānandaścittānando jñānānandaḥ

tadeva tattvaṃ sahajakāya ityucyate
tato dharmakāyaḥ, tataḥ sambhoggakāyaḥ, tato nirmāṇakāyaḥ
evaṃ sahajavāk, sahajacittam, sahajajñānam; dharmavāk, dharmacittam, dharmajñānam; sambhogavāk, sambhogacittam, sambhogajñānam; nirmāṇavāk, nirmāṇacittam, nirmāṇajñānamiti cittādhimuktivaśāt sattvānāṃ pratibhāsate
ṣoḍaśākāraṃ tattvamiti, sa eva sahajakāyaḥ, śūnyatāvimokṣaviśuddho jñānavajraḥ sarvajñaḥ, prajñopāyātmako viśuddhayoga iti
sa eva dharmakāyo'nimittavimokṣaviśuddhaṃ cittavatraṃ jñānakāyaḥ prajñopāyātmako dharmayoga' ityuktaḥ
sa eva sambhogakāyaḥ, apraṇihitavimokṣaviśuddhaṃ vāgvajraṃ dinakaravapuḥ prajñopāyātmako mantrayoga ityuktaḥ
sa eva nirmāṇakāyo'nabhisaṃskāravimokṣaviśuddhaṃ kāyavajraṃ padmapatrāyatākṣaḥ prajñopāyātmakaḥ saṃsthānayoga ityuktaḥ
evametaṃ vajrayogaṃ caturvidhaṃ buddhaṃ pṛcched vajrapāṇiriti
śūnyatāvimokṣaviśuddho jñānavajraḥ prajñopāyātmakaḥ sahajakāyaḥ sarvajñatāprāptaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvadaśitatvāt
animittavimokṣaviśuddhaḥ cittavajraḥ prajñopāyātmako dharmakāyaḥ, mārgākārajñatāprāpto jñānakāyaḥ, paramākṣarasukhenāvasthitatvāt
apraṇihitavimokṣaviśuddho vāgvajraḥ prajñopāyātmakaḥ sambhogakāyo mārgajñatāprāptaḥ, dinakaravapuranantānantasattvarutairyu- gapallaukika lokottaradharmadeśakatvāditi

'dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ' ityadhikaḥ pāṭhaḥ Xxx x Homila paṭale ] maṇḍalābhiṣekādisaṃgrahoddeśaḥ (6) maṇḍalābhiṣekādisaṃgrahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ sūryarathādhyeṣitaḥ san mañjuśrībhagavān nirmitakāyo yaśo narendraḥ paramādi- buddhāt tathāgatavyākṛtaṃ sucandrādhyeṣaṇaṃ dvitīyavṛttenāha- śūnyaṃ jñānaṃ ca binduṃ varakuliśadharaṃ buddhadevāsurāṃśca bāhya dehe pare ca prakṛtiṣu puruṣaṃ pañcaviṃśātmakaṃ ca dehe viśvasya mānaṃ tribhuvanaracanāṃ bhuktide ( deṃ) vāsurāṇām etad vyākhyāhi samyak tridaśanaragurormaṇḍalaṃ cābhiṣekam || || ihādhyeṣaṇārthatvaṃ' pañcākṣaramahāśūnyaṃ binduśūnyaṃ ṣaḍakṣara - ṣaṭkulādīnāṃ saṃgrahārthaṃ bhagavatoktamiti, tadidaṃ vivṛṇomi śūnyamityādinā |

atra pratipad a, dvitīyā i, tṛtīyā ṝ, caturthī u, pañcamī la, nandā bhadrā jayā riktā pūrṇā ākāśavāyutejaudakapṛthivīdhātavo yathāsaṃkhyaṃ tamasa udghāṭana- (ne) prathamamṛdumātreti

madhye meroryadūrdhvaṃ bhramati dinaniśaṃ rāśicakra satāraṃ ṣaḍbhāge dvidvilakṣaṃ tribhuvanasakalaṃ kālayogāt prajātam || 11 ||
vāyvantād vāyusīmna: catvāri lakṣāṇi vāyorvāyvantaṃ pūrvādaparavāyuvalayāntaṃ yāvat |
evaṃ dakṣiṇāduttarāntaṃ yāvaditi |

iha ratnairyasminnāvāse ābhāḥ, sa āvāso ratnābhaḥ; tasmādasuranāga [ 46a ] lokāt pañcaviṃśatsahasrādadho yojanamānam; tasmāt ratnābhāt śarkarāmbho nigadito narakaḥ kathita ityarthaḥ
bālukāmbho nāma dvitīyaḥ paṅkāmbhastṛtīya iti śītanarakadvayam; tatastotradhūmranarakaścaturtho havirapi pañcama iti uṣṇanarakadvayam ; tato'dhastamaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ rauravo mahātamaḥ saptamaḥ khara-vāta- narakadvayaṃ vajrāciḥ sahitam
cakārādaṣṭamo vajrasūcyagrabhūmāgaḥ sadā prajvalita iti aṣṭau mahānarakāḥ pātāle

kinnaraṃ bhogabhūmau krauñcaṃ raudraṃ ca jambūnivasati manujaḥ saptamaṃ karmabhūmau // 16 //

kṣāro bhūmivalayabāhye, madyādayo bhūmyupari śailāḥ sapta - nīlābho mandarādriniṣadhamaṇikaro droṇa- lakṣayojanāyāmaṃ trilakṣaṃ vṛttena yasminnivasati manujastat karmabhūmau veditavyam, ṣaṭbhoga bhūmyāmiti

tadbāhye dvīparaulā iti

ato'ṣṭādaśabhāgalabdhaṃ ṣoḍaśasahasrebhyo mānaṃ sphuṭaṃ bhavatīti
tadbāhya pañcaviṃśat (ti) sahasra jambūdvīpaṃ viśālaṃ valayākāraṃ meroraṣṭadikṣu; tadbāhye lavaṇajalanidheraddha lakṣaṃ pramāṇaṃ bhavati sarvadikṣu
tadvadagnivalayasya vāyuvala- yasya tribhuvanadharaṇasyāntimasya pramāṇamiti
evaṃ merorbrahmasthānāt sarvadikṣu dvi-dvilakṣaṃ yojanamānaṃ bhavati; śarīre urorarddha pṛthvīvalayam; bāhurudakavalayamupabāhuragnivalayaṃ maṇibandhāt karāntaṃ vāyuvalayam
evaṃ lokadhātumānamiti
idānīṃ kālacakradeva nāgapīṭhopapīṭhādīnāṃ ' sthānamucyate brahmāṇḍe kālacakramityā- dinā - brahmāṇḍe kālacakra' jinavarasahitaṃ saṃstutaṃ devavṛndai- merau gīrvāṇacakra tvavanitalagataṃ pañcavarṇaṃ hyahīnām
śrīmeroḥ sarvadikṣu kṣitivalayagataṃ sarva pīṭhopapīṭhaṃ kṣetraṃ chandohamelāpakacitibhuvanaṃ vahnivāyvantasīmnaḥ ||20//
brahmāṇḍe bāhye śarīre, uṣṇīṣamaṇau kālacakraḥ tameva saṃstutaṃ jinavarasahitaṃ sarvabuddheḥ sahitaṃ samājame kalolībhūtaṃ devavṛndairbodhisattvabrahmādibhiḥ saṃstutamiti
merau gīrvāṇacakraṃ brahmādīnāmiti
avanitalagataṃ pañcavarṇaṃ [47b] hyahīnāṃ cakramiti
śrīmeroḥ sarvadikṣu kṣitivalayagataṃ sarvapīṭhopapīṭhamiti
pīṭhaṃ caturdikṣu vāyvagnyudaka- pṛthvīsvabhāvenāvasthitaṃ pūrvavideham, laghujambūdvīpam, uttarakuruḥ, aparagodānīyamiti
upapīṭhaṃ tatsvabhāvena caturdikṣu, āgneyanaiṛtya īśānavāyavyadikṣu
evaṃ kṣetropakṣetraṃ samu - drāddhi (ddhe )

melāpakopamelāpakamagnivalayārddhe
veśmopaveśmamagneraparārddha

aṣṭau mahāśmaśānāni vāyuvalayānte
evaṃ pīṭhādayo dvādaśabhūmayo veditavyā iti
śarīre ca dvādaśabhūmayo hasta- pādayordvādaśasandhaya iti
idānīṃ dikapālasthānamucyate- pūrve śakro'gniragnau yamadanavaruṇā yāmyadetyāpareṣu vāyuryakṣo haraścāniladhanadahareṣūrdhva bhāge tvadhaśca
brahmā viṣṇuḥ samastāḥ parijanasahitāḥ svasvadigrakṣapālā- stanmadhye kālacakro jinavarajanako'nāhato vajrakāyaḥ ||21||
pūrve śakra iti pūrve śakro merormūrdhni, pūrve diśi śakraḥ
agnāvagniḥ
yamo yāmye, danurdaitye, varuṇo'pare, vāyuranile, yakṣo dhanade, haro hare, ūrdhvabhāge brahmā, adho mahā jammūna mana vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- viṣṇuḥ
ete śakrādayaḥ samastāḥ parijanasahitāḥ svasvadigrakṣapālā iti
teṣāṃ madhye kālacakro jinānāṃ janako'nāhato vajrakāyo dikapālānāṃ prabhurityarthaḥ
tyādi - [48a] idānīṃ mahācakravattibhramaṇaṃ dvādaśabhūmikhaṇḍeṣūcyate mero:' pṛṣṭheṣvi- meroḥ pṛṣṭheṣu dikṣu bhramati bhuvitale durjayo dānavānāṃ yasmin dharmo vinaṣṭo vahati kaliyugaṃ tatra tatra prayāti
hatvā mlecchāṃśca yuddhe vicarati purataḥ sthāpayitvā svadharme kṛttretādvāparaṃ vai kaliyugamaparaṃ vartate kālayogāt || 22 ||
meroḥ pṛṣṭheṣu dikṣu bhramati bhuktile durjayo dānavānāṃ mlecchānāṃ yasmin bhūkhaṇḍe tathāgatadhamoṃ vinaṣṭasteṣāṃ viparyāsadharmo vartate, tasmin bhūmitale bhramati cakrī kaliyuge sa evādharmaṃ pravṛttiḥ, kaliyugaṃ vahutītyarthaḥ
tadevādharmātmakaṃ kaliyugam
yatra yatra mlecchadharmaṃ vahati khaṇḍe, tatra tatraiva khaṇḍaṃ prakarṣeṇa yāti prayātīti
hatvā mlecchāṃścakārād dānavādīṃśca yuddha e vicarati purataḥ sthāpayitvā svadharme tān mlecchādīn kṛttretādvāparaṃ vai kaliyugamaparaṃ vartate kālayogāt
atra yugaṃ kṛdayugā- dikamaparaṃ mahākṛyugādikaṃ na bhavatītyarthaḥ
etad yugaṃ kālayogād vartate
kālo dvādaśa- rāśicakram, tasya yogāt kālayogād vartate catuḥ sandhyābhedeneti
yasmin khaṇḍe sa cakrī praviśati balavān kṛdyugaṃ tatra yāti tretā pṛṣṭhe ca rājñaḥ kalirapi purato dvāparañca dvimadhye
viṃśatyekaṃ sahasraṃ rasaśatasahitaṃ varṣamānaṃ yugānām ekaikasya pramāṇaṃ yugaśaraguṇitaṃ mānavābde śataṃ yat || 23||
yasmin khaṇḍe sa cakkī praviśati balavān kṛyugaṃ tatra yāti; kṛyugaṃ nāma samyaksambuddhadharmaṃ yātītyarthaḥ
tretā pṛṣṭhe ca rājñastretā pṛṣṭhe rājño bhavati, dharmasyaikapādā- bhāvaḥ kṛdante tretānte dvipādābhāva:, kalirapi purato rājño dharmasya catuḥ pādābhāvaḥ kalpānte
dvāparaṃ ca dvimadhye iti
kalitretayormadhye dvāparaṃ dharmasya tripādābhāva iti
viśatyekaṃ sahasraṃ rasaśatasahitam
rasa iti ṣaṭṣaṭśatādhikaika viśatsahasraṃ varṣamānaṃ yugānāṃ caturṇāṃ mānaṃ tad bhavati
ekaikasya pramāṇaṃ [48b] yugaśaraguṇitamiti
yuga iti catvāraḥ, śara iti pañca taiḥ catuḥpañcāśadbhirguṇitaṃ yugaśaraguṇitamiti
mānavābde śataṃ yaditi
varṣaśataṃ guṇitaṃ catuḥśatādhikaṃ sahasrapañcakaṃ bhavati, pratyekayuga- mānaṃ tulyamiti
havinīta
paṭale lokadhātumāna saṃgrahauddeśaḥ idānīṃ manuṣyatanujādīnāṃ svasvaśvāsamānamucyate- sūkṣmocchvāsadhunāḍīdinayuga sahasra kaikaviṃśacchataiśca ṣaḍ bhirmānaṃ krameṇa tvaṇutanujanṛṇāṃ bhūtadevāsurāṇām
śakterbharturdinakaṃ vahati bhuktile śaktimānaṃ yugānāṃ khaṇḍe khaṇḍe ca cakrī vrajati śivapadaṃ dvādaśārkā dineke || 24 ||
sūkṣmocchvāsetyādi
sūkṣma iti manuṣyaśvāsaniḥśvāsasya ekasya ṣaṭśatādhika- ekaviṃśatsahasrabhūtasya ekāṃśaḥ sūkṣmaśvāsaḥ, tena śvāsaṣaṭkena tanujādīnāṃ kṛmikulā- dīnāmekaṃ pāṇī (ṇi) palam, ṣaṣṭibhiḥ pāṇipalaiḥ ghaṭīghaṭībhiḥ ṣaṣṭibhirdinamiti
dinaṃ nāma tanujādīnāṃ ṣaṭśatādhika ekaviṃśatsahasraśvāsaniḥśvāsasaṃkhyā, terucchvāsairgatanaṃ rāṇāṃ svadinaṃ bhūtapretānāṃ dhrubhiḥ svadinaṃ dhruriti
manuṣyaśvāsasya triśadguṇaḥ śvāsaḥ kṛṣṇa- pakṣo divā, śuklapakṣo rātriḥ pretānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ svadinamiti
nāḍībhirmanuṣyāṇāṃ ṣaṣṭayuttaratriśataśvāso devānāmekaśvāsaḥ
dinamati manuṣyāṇāṃ ṣaṭśatādhika-ekaviṃśat- sahasraśvāsāḥ, asurāṇāmekaśvāsaḥ
dhruriti manuṣyāṇāṃ varṣa:
śakterekaśvāsaḥ śakti- rityakaniṣṭhajātiḥ
yuga iti manuṣyāṇāṃ ṣaṭśatādhika ekavaśatsahasravarṣapiṇḍitaṃ bhartturekaśvāsaḥ
bharturiti naivasaṃjñāyatanopagajāteriti
sūkṣmocchvāsanāḍodina - [x] yugasahasraika [49a] viśacchateśca ṣaḍbhirmānaṃ krameṇa tvaṇutanujanṛṇāṃ bhūtadevāsurāṇāṃ śakterbharturdinaikam
evaṃ pūrvoktakrameṇa eṣāṃ tanujādīnāṃ svakīyasvakīyaśvāsaiḥ ṣaṭ- śatādhika - ekaviṃśatsahasrudinaṃ bhavati
eṣu māneṣu śaktimānamakaniṣṭhadevamānaṃ vahati bhuvitale cakriṇo yugānāṃ śakti- mānamiti
khaṇḍe khaṇḍe ca cakro vrajati śivapadam
pratyekakhaṇḍe buddhadharmaṃ pravattayitvā vrajati mahāsukhapadaṃ mahāsukhasthānamiti
dvādaśārkā dinaike
evaṃ pratyekaikakhaṇḍe pratye- kaikacakravartī dharmapravarttakaḥ
ekadine dvādaśa cakravattino bhavanti; aṣṭādaśāṣṭādaśaśataiḥ śaktiśvāsaiḥ manuṣyāṇāṃ varṣerekaiko vrajati
evaṃ dvādaśārkā dharmaṃdivākarāḥ puṇyajñānamārga- pravarttakā iti
bāhye varṣeṇa dvādaśarāśibhedena dvādaśārkā vrajantīti
idānīṃ sūkṣmādonāṃ śvāsabhedena pratyekadine kālabheda ucyate- pakṣā māsāḥ samastā ṛtuyugasamayā agnikālo'yane dve varṣaṃ rājan samastaṃ tvanudinaghaṭikālagnapāṇīpalāni

tantre'nekā hargaṇarāśiḥ, karaṇe'pi mandaśīghraṃ karmacatuṣṭayaṃ (iti) prapañca (:) kṛtam (kṛtaḥ), yena bālajanāviṣamaṃ dṛṣṭvā dūraṃ palāyanti, jyotiṣa (ṣaṃ) nāma na kurvanti

idānīṃ laghukaraṇaṃ tāvaducyate-
vahnau khe'bdhau vimiśraṃ prabhavamukhagataṃ mlecchavarṣaṃ prasiddham
ūnaṃ mlecchendravarṣaṃ karaphaṇiśaśinā śeṣamarkāhataṃ ca
miśraṃ caitrādimāsairadharayugahataṃ khāgnicandrairvibhaktaṃ
labdhaṃ mūrdhni praviṣṭaṃ bhavati narapate māsapiṇḍaṃ viśuddham || 27||
vaha nau khe'bdhau vimiśramiti
iha dhruvako'nityastantrarāje ṣaṣṭisaṃvatsarānte puna- dhruvakaraṇāditi
iha tathāgatakālāt ṣaḍvarṣaśatairmañjuśrīkālaḥ karaṇe dhruvaḥ
tasmā- daṣṭaśatavarṣaiḥ mlecchakālaḥ;
tasmāt mlecchakālāt dvayaśītyadhikaśatena hīno ajakalkī kālo yenājena ra laghukaraṇaṃ viśodhitam
sa eva kālaḥ karaṇe dhruvakaṃ bhavati mlecchavarṣā- diti
prabhavamukhagatamiti
prabhavo mukhamādiryeṣāṃ ṣaṣṭisaṃvatsarāṇāṃ te prabhavamukhāḥ, teṣu pratyekaṃ tanmiśraṃ tryadhikacatuḥśatavarṣaṃ rāśau mlecchavarṣaṃ prasiddhaṃ bhavati
ekavarṣamādi kṛtvā yāvat ṣaṣṭivarṣaṃ tāvad vimiśraṃ prabhavamukhagataṃ bhavati iti
tadeva varṣaṃ sarvakaraṇāntare prasiddhaṃ bhavatītyādityādivāravat
tena vimiśritaṃ tryadhikacatuḥśatavarṣarāśāviti
mlecchavarṣaṃ prasiddhaṃ mleccho madhumatī rahmaṇā va- tāro mlecchadharmadeśako mlecchānāṃ guruḥ svāmī
ūnaṃ mlecchendravarṣaṃ karaphaṇiśaśinā dvaya śītyadhikaśatenonam, karaphaṇi- śaśinonamiti, sambhalaviṣaye ajakalkīkālavarṣam, tadeva laghukaraṇe varṣapiṇḍamiti
śeṣamarkāhataṃ ca
tadeva varṣapiṇḍaṃ dvayaśītyadhikaśatenonāvaśeṣaṃ māsapiṇḍanimittamarkāhataṃ dvādaśaguṇitaṃ māsapiṇḍaṃ bhavati
miśraṃ caitrādimāsairiti
tadeva māsapiṇḍaṃ caitrādivarttamānamāsaimiśraṃ bhavati vartta- mānamāsārthamiti
adharayugahatamiti
tadeva māsapiṇḍamadha upari rāśau adho rāśau māsapiṇḍaṃ kṛtvā adhikamāsagrahaṇārthaṃ yugahataṃ caturguṇitaṃ bhavati
khāgnicandravibhaktamiti
atra sūryasya dvātriṃśatsāddha māsairadhikamāso'māvasyāṃ saṃkramaṇābhāvāt
tena bhogo na syāt, tasmāt rāśi caturguṇitā bhāgarāśirbhavati
bhāgarāśeścaturguṇite sati 'nimittābhāve naimittakasyābhāva' iti nyāyāt vibhajya rāśiścaturguṇī bhavati
tasmād paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ vibhajya rāśerbhāgarāśinā labdhaṃ phalaṃ bhavati
labdhaṃ mūni praviṣṭam
tadeva labdhamadhika- māsapiṇḍaṃ mūrdhni māsapiṇḍarāśau praviṣṭaṃ bhavati narapate māsapiṇḍaṃ viśuddhaṃ varttamānamāse triśattithigaṇitārthamiti
idānīṃ māsadhruvakamucyate-
māsāstristhānabhūtā kara śikhiguṇitā madhyarāśau sthitā ye
mūle ṣaḍbhāgalabdhaṃ bhavati ca tadṛṇaṃ madhyame śodhanīyam
ūrdhvo'dho netratraṃśat prakaṭayati dhanaṃ miśritaṃ ṣaṣṭibhāgai-
rlabdhaṃ mūrdhni praviṣṭaṃ bhavati munivaraiścheditaḥ śeṣavāraḥ ||28||
māsāstristhānabhūtāḥ kara śikhiguṇitā madhyarāśau sthitā ya iti
atra śuddha- māsāstristhāne kṛtvā madhyarāśau ye māsāḥ sthitāḥ te pratimāsavāraghaṭikārthaṃ karaśikhi - guṇitā dvātriṃśadguṇitā bhavanti
mūle ṣaḍbhāgalabdhamiti
mūlarāśau ṣaḍbhāgena labdhaṃ madhyame rāśau ṛṇaṃ bhavati madhyarāśau, ataḥ śodhanīyam, ṛṇatvāt
ūrdhvo'dho ' netratriṃśat prakaṭayati dhanaṃ miśritam
idaṃ dhruvakaṃ maṅgalādikam astamanakaraṇamāgatam, ata ādityādyudayakaraṇārthaṃ mūrdhni rāśau vāradvayaṃ dhanaṃ bhavati;
adho rāśau triṃśatghaṭikā
dhanaṃ bhavati
ato miśramūrddhādho yathāsaṃkhyaṃ netrañca triśañca netratriśaditi, ṣaṣṭibhāga- rlabdham, mūlaghaṭikārāśau ṣaṣṭibhāgena labdhaṃ vārapiṇḍaṃ bhavati
avaśeṣaṃ ghaṭikāpiṇḍaṃ vārabhoge' bhavati, mūdhni praviṣṭam
tadeva ṣaṣṭyā bhāgena labdhaṃ vārapiṇḍaṃ mūrdhni praviṣṭaṃ vārarāśau bhavati
munivaraiḥ saptabhiḥ chedito vibhaktaḥ san śeṣo vāra eva sa iti māsavake vāro mūrdhni sthātavyaḥ, vārādho ghaṭikāpiṇḍamiti
dvisthāne'rkendumiśraṃ hṛtamṛturaviṇā bhūtamiśraṃ samastaṃ deyaṃ tanmūni rāśau prathamakarahate piṇḍamaṣṭadvibhaktam
tristhāmadhye hateśāstvadharanavaguṇairbhāgalabdhaṃ dvimiśram ūnaṃ madhye ghaṭobhirhṛtamapi tu dhanaṃ mūrdhni rāśau dvihatvā || 29 ||
idānīṃ vārapadānyucyante- dvisthāne'rkendumiśramiti
tadeva māsapiṇḍamarkendumiśraṃ dvādaśottaraśatamiśram
arkenduriti karaṇāpekṣā na siddhāntāpekṣayeti dvisthāne bhavati
hṛtamṛturaviṇā
adhomāsapiṇḍaṃ hṛtaṃ ṣaḍviṃśatyadhikaśatena labdhaṃ mūni rāśau dhanaṃ bhavati
pratimāsaṃ piṇḍa- dvayārthaṃ mūni rāśau deyaṃ bhavati
prathamakarahate paścād deyaṃ bhavati
bhūtamiśraṃ samasta- miti
karaṇāpekṣā ṛturaviṇā bhāgalabdhaṃ siddhāntam
yataḥ ṣaḍviṃśatya- dhikamāsaśatena ekapiṇḍaṃ piṇḍasthāne'dhikaṃ bhavati, candracaraṇavaśāditi
piṇḍamaṣṭadvibha- vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- ktamiti
aṣṭaviṃśatibhirbhaktaṃ piṇḍaṃ bhavati; saptavārapadatvādaṣṭaviṃśatibhirbhāgaḥ syā- t, tena bhāgena labdhaṃ pratimāsaṃ vārapadaṃ bhavati
vāraghaṭikādhaḥ ṛturaviṇā bhāga- labdhāvaśeṣaṃ piṇḍāvayavaṃ bhavati, piṇḍādhaḥ sthāpanīyami i ti
idānīṃ sūryanakṣatra vakamucyate- tristhāmadhye hateśā iti
te śuddhamāsā tristhā ne) mūrdhni-madhye adhasi (adho) bhavanti
madhye ye māsāste īśairhatā ekādaśahatā iti karaṇāpekṣā na siddhāntāpekṣe kṣaye ti
adharanavaguṇairbhāgalabdhaṃ dvimiśramiti
adho rāśau navaguṇai reko- nacatvāriṃśadbhirbhāgalabdhaṃ dvimiśramiti karaṇāpekṣayā kṣepakaḥ
ūnaṃ madhye madhyarāśau hīnaṃ ṛṇamityarthaḥ
ghaṭobhirhṛtamapi tu dhanamiti
madhyarāśerghaṭībhiḥ ṣaṣṭibhirhataṃ labdhaṃ nakṣatrapiṇḍaṃ mūni rāśau dhanaṃ bhavati
ṣaṣṭibhāgāvaśeṣā nakṣatraghaṭikā bhavanti
tadeva dhanaṃ mūrdhni rāśi śau dvihatvā, yuktaṃ cakṣaṃ prabhakto bhavati nṛpa raveḥ śeṣa ṛkṣādibhogaḥ vāre vāraṃ pradeyaṃ dviguṇanṛpaghaṭī piṇḍake dve tvadhaśca
dve ca rudranāḍya : prakaṭayati dhanaṃ sūryabhoge pradeyam etanmāsaṃ dhruvaṃ syāt kathitamapi punarmāsi māsi pradeyam ||30||
yuktaṃ ca prabhaktaṃ saptā viṃśatibhiḥ prabhakto bhavati
nṛpāmantraṇam
raveḥ śeṣa ṛkṣādibhogaḥ
atra mūrdhni nakṣatrapiṇḍamadho nakṣatraghaṭikā piṇḍamiti
punaśśuddhādapya- śuddhamāsadha vakaṃ bālānāṃ pratibodhanārthaṃ racitam
vāre vāraṃ pradeyam, vārasthāne vāramekaṃ deyam
dviguṇanṛpaghaṭī
nṛpa iti ṣoḍaśa; dviguṇanṛpaghaṭī dvātriṃśad ghaṭī ghaṭikāsthāne dehā yā); piṇḍake dve tvadhaśceti ghaṭikādhaḥ piṇḍasthāne dve deye bhavataḥ
dve carkṣe nakṣatra- sthāne
rudranāḍyo nakṣatraghaṭikāsthāne
rudra iti ekādaśa nāḍyaḥ deyā iti
dhanaṃ prakaṭayati sūryabhoge pradeyaṃ nakṣatrādikam
tasmāt māsaghra vakaṃ syāt kathitamapi punarmāsi māsi pradeyam
pratyekamāsi pradeyamiti māsaghravakaṃ ka raṇāpekṣāvaśena bhagavatā anāgatameva vyākṛtamiti
idānīṃ māsa va tithyā digaṇitamucyate- deyā heyāśca deyāḥ punarapi tithayo vāranāḍīpadeṣu piṇḍe bhāge'bdhicandraḥ samaviṣamagate deyaheyau padārthoṃ śūnye śūnyaṃ viśuddhaṃ tridaśaśaśipade pañca netrārkayordik tithyākhyaikādaśe'gnau daśajaladhipade viṃśatiścaikahīnā || 31 ||
deyā heyāśca deyāḥ punarapi tithayo vāranāḍīpadeṣviti
iha pratimāsadhruvake śuklapratipadādyekatithimārabhya yāvat triṃśattamāmāvāsī tāvat tithayo deyā heyāḥ paṭale jyotirjñāna vidhimahoddeśaḥ punardeyā iti vārasthāne deyāḥ pratidinavārārtham; ghaṭikāsthāne heyāścandrakalāyā ekaghaṭī- hānita:, punardeyāḥ piṇḍasthāne candrapadānāṃ dhanaṛṇabhogapariśuddhita iti
piṇḍe bhāge'- bdhicandraḥ samaviṣamagate deyaheyau padārthāviti
piṇḍasthāne piṇḍādabdhicandreṇa caturdaśabhāgena samagatena padārtho dhanaṃ bhavati; viṣamagatena ṛṇaṃ padārtho bhavati
atra sama- bhāge dvābhyāṃ gataścaturbhirvāgata iti na gato'pi sama iti
viṣamabhāge ekena gataḥ tribhi - vagata iti
tadupari nāsti bhāgaḥ
saptapañcāśadūrdhvaṃ triśattithisahitāḥ saptā (pta) - viṃśatipiṇḍakā na bhavanti
ataścaturdaśabhāgaiścatvāro vibhaktā bhavantīti tena samaviṣama- bhāgena padārthā deyā heyā vā veditavyāḥ
padārtho nāma candracā (vā) rapadāni caturdaśa- sthāne'dha ūrdhve saptasaptapadavyāvṛttyā anulomavilomābhyāṃ pṛthivyādiguṇabhedabhinnāni
te ca padārthā deyā heyā bhavanti
śūnye śūnyaṃ viśuddhamiti
caturdaśabhāgāvaśeṣaṃ yadi śūnyaṃ tadā padārtho'pi śūnyaḥ, na deyo na heya iti nyāya: karaṇā (ṇe )
tridaśaśaśipade pañceti
trayodaśame (śe) pade avaśeṣe dṛṣṭe ekapade vā pañcaghaṭikā dhanaṃ vā ṛṇaṃ vā bhavati
evaṃ netrārkayodi ( )- giti dvipade adhike'vaśeṣe dvādaśe vā daśaghaṭikā deyā bhavanti, candrakalāvṛddhihānivaśā- diti
tithyākhya iti pañcadaśa; ekādaśe agnāviti tṛtīye bhavanti
daśapadajaladhīti caturthe pade bhāgāvaśeṣe dṛṣṭa viṃśatiścaikahīnā bhavanti
dvātriṃśat ( ti ) pañcarandhre ṛtuvasuni jinā: saptame pañcaviṃśat (tiḥ ) tasminnarkaprabhedaiḥ prakaṭitaravikā deyayā bhavanti
bhūtābhūteṣu vedāḥ śikhikaraśaśinaḥ pūrvabhāge'pare ca piṇḍe bhāge'bdhicandraḥ samaviṣamagate deyaheyāśca vārāḥ ||
dvāviṃśat (ti) pañcame randhra iti navame bhavanti
ṛturiti ṣaṭ (ḍ) vasunītyaṣṭame pade jināścaturviṃśatirbhavanti
saptame pade bhāgāvaśeṣe dṛṣṭe pañcaviṃśat ghaṭikā same deyā viṣame heyā bhavanti
tasminnarkaprabhedaiḥ prakaṭitaravikā deyayā bhavanti
tasmin tithibhoge'rkaprabhedena sūryapadāni bhavanti; sūryasya ṛṇaṃ tasmin ṛṇam, sūryasya dhanaṃ tasminneva dhanamiti sūryapramedaḥ
atra candrapadāni bhūtābhūteṣu vedāḥ śikhikaraśaśinaḥ pūrvabhāge'pare ceti
bhūtā iti pañca, punabhūteti tataḥ adhaḥ pañca, iṣuriti tato'dhaḥ pañca vedā iti tato'dhaścatvāri, śikhīti tato'dhaḥ trayaḥ; kara iti tato'dho dve, śaśina iti tato'dha ekapadam
evaṃ sapta- sthāneṣu padāni
tato'parasaptasthāneṣu vilomena ekādinā caturdaśasthāne pañca padāni yāvat
evaṃ candrapadānāṃ nyāso bhavati caturdaśasthāneṣviti
( ekādinā ); ka. ekodinā; ṅa. ekadināt
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- piṇḍe bhāge'bdhicandraiḥ samaviṣamagate deyaheyāśca vārā iti
atrāpi caturdaśa- bhāgāvaśeṣe dṛṣṭe samagate vārāḥ pañcādayo deyāḥ, viṣamagate heyā [ ] iti
caturdaśa- bhāgāvaśeṣe eke dṛṣṭe pañca ghaṭikā grāhyā; dvitīye dvitīyasthānasthaiḥ saha daśa; tṛtīye tṛtīyasthānasthaiḥ saha pañcā (ca) daśa; caturthe caturthasthānasthaiścaturbhiḥ saha ekonaviṃśatiḥ; pañcame pañcamasthānasthaistribhiḥ saha dvāviṃśat; ṣaṣṭhe ṣaṣṭhasthānasthābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ saha caturviṃśatiḥ saptame saptamasthānasthenaikena saha pañcaviṃśatiḥ tataścandracaraṇavṛddhirnivarttate
aṣṭame dṛṣṭe sati aṣṭapadāni pūrvaṃ saptapadaiḥ sahitāni
tyaktvā parāṇi ṣaṭpadānyabhuktānyeka- piṇḍaṃ kṛtvā caturviṃśatighaṭikā deyā heyā vā bhavanti
navame pañcapadānāṃ dvāviṃśad ghaṭikā; daśame catuṣpadānāmekonaviṃśatiḥ ekādaśe tripadānāṃ pañca'; dvādaśe dvipadānāṃ daśa; trayodaśe ekapadasya pañca; caturdaśame (śe) śūnyamiti siddhāntaniścayaḥ
saccāreṇāvaśeṣaṃ hatamṛturaviṇā bhāgalabdhaṃ ca tadvat tatraivārkaprabhedai ravipadaghaṭikā deyayā bhavanti
vedaistithyāhataṃ yat sphuṭamapi tu dhanaṃ tat tribhāgena miśram ṛtvṛkṣaṃ satripādaṃ tvṛṇamapi tu raveḥ śodhayed bhuktimadhye ||
saccāreṇāvaśeṣaṃ hatamṛturaviṇā bhāgalabdhaṃ ca tadvaditi
atra saccāra iti piṇḍādha(:)sthitānāṃ piṇḍāvayavānām, ṛturaviṇā bhāgalabdhāvaśeṣāṇāmiti
saccāra-
piṇḍe caturbhāgāvaśeṣaṃ cārapadaṃ yat tadupari sañcārapadaṃ piṇḍāvayavānāṃ śatapadaṃ bhavati
tatra grahaḥ praviṣṭaḥ
tasmāt tad grāhyaṃ deyayārtham
tena padenāvaśeṣaṃ ( ) piṇḍāvayavaṃ ( ), hataṃ ( ), tataḥ ṣaṭviṃśatyadhikaśatena bhāgalabdhaṃ ca tadvad deyam
sarvaṃ yathā pūrvacaraṇa- ghaṭikāpiṇḍaṃ tadvaditi
tatraiva cārkaprabhedaiḥ pūrvavad ravikā deyā heyā vā bhavantīti siddhāntaḥ
idānīṃ pratidinasūryabhoga ucyate- vedaistithyāhataṃ yat sphuṭamapi tu dhanaṃ tat tri bhāgena miśramiti
vedaiścaturbhistithi (bhi)cāhataṃ guṇitaṃ vedesthiyāhataṃ sphuṭaṃ dhanaṃ bhavati
sūryasya ghaṭikāsthāne tasyā guṇitaṃ tithyāstribhāgena miśraṃ dhanaṃ ghaṭikāsthāne deyamiti
ṛtvṛkṣaṃ satripādaṃ tvṛṇamapi tu raveḥ śodhayed bhaktimadhye iti
iha ravikāpadaviśuddhayarthaṃ sūryasya janmarāśiḥ śodhanīyaḥ, yena sūryasya pratyahaṃ vāraghaṭīpāṇī (ṇi) palādibhogo jñāyate, anyathā karaṇe śuddhirnāsti pratyahaṃ sūryasyeti
ato'śvinyādinakṣatrabhogāt ṣaṭnakṣatraṃ satripādamiti pañcacatvāriṃśad ghaṭikā iti meṣādirāśitrayaṃ śodhitaṃ bhavati
karkaṭe janmarāśirbhavati, sūryasya caraṇaśuddhita iti
ata ṛtvṛkṣaṃ satripādaṃ tvṛṇamapi tu raveḥ śodhayed paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ bhuktimadhye
bhuktirnakṣatrādibhogaḥ, tasmin bhuktimadhye nakṣatrasthāne nakṣatraṃ śodhayed, ghaṭikāsthāne ghaṭikāṃ śodhayediti
śiṣṭaṃ kāryaṃ yathāre rasayugaśaśino mandakārye padāni hatvā bhogena nāḍī : śaraguṇaśaśibhirbhāgalabdhaṃ sabhogam
deyaṃ heyaṃ ca sūrye tvayanagativaśānnānyathā śuddhirasti sūrye kālo raso'hiśca daśa haraharā rudradignāgaṣaṭ ca ||
bhavatīti ayanagativaśāt pratyahaṃ śuddhirnānyathā karaṇoktavidhineti
atra karaṇoktavidhirucyate- sūrye kālo rasa iti karkaṭe ṛṇaṃ prathamapakṣe kāla iti tisro ghaṭikāḥ
rasa iti ṣaṭ dvitīye karkaṭapakṣe
evaṃ siṃhe ahiścetyaṣṭa daśa (ca) iti
tathā kanyāyāṃ hara ekādaśa, punarhara ekādaśa, tatastulāyāṃ rudra ekādaśa, digiti daśa, ityevaṃ vṛścike nāga ityaṣṭa ṣaṭ ca ||34||
vahniḥ khañcāyanānte bhavati dhananṛṇāṃ (mṛṇaṃ ) cottare dakṣiṇe ca ṣaṇmāsaṃ pakṣabhedaiścarati dinakaro māsasaṃkrāntibhedāt
datvā sūrye tithīṃścāpyadhararasahatā vārabhogena miśrā ūnībhūtāḥ śaśāṅko bhavati narapate miśrasūryenduyogaḥ || 35 ||
pāṭha: - mūlasya bhoṭapāṭhānurodhena 'dhanamṛṇam' ityeva siddhaḥ vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- tato dhanuṣi vahniriti tisraḥ, khamiti śūnyamayanānte
evaṃ makarādī dhanamiti karaṇe, na siddhānte abhiprāyaḥ sarvatra
evaṃ ṣaḍa (0) māsaṃ pakṣabhedaiścarati dinakaro māsa- saṃkrāntibhedāditi
idānīṃ candrabhoga ucyate- datvā sūrye iti sūryabhāge tithīdatvā adhararasahatāstithī ' radhasi (dhaḥ) ṣaḍbhirhatāḥ
vārabhogena miśrā vārabhogaghaṭikādibhirmiśrā ūnībhūtā; ghaṭikāsthāne śaśāṅkabhogo bhavati
miśrasūryenduyoga iti sūryendubhogo miśro yogo bhavati

mīnagate sūrye kanyāgate ca viṣuvadvayaṃ bhavati, vasantārddhaṃ śaradarddhaṃ bhavatīti

idānīṃ dvādaśabhūmyāṃ dvādaśottarāyaṇāni dvādaśa dakṣiṇāyanāni sūrya rāśibhramaṇavaśe- nocyate- asmin vāmāyanaṃ syāditi
asminniti merordakṣiṇe bhūkhaṇḍe
kuto dakṣiṇe ?
laghujambūdvīpe tathāgatadharmadeśanāvaśāt
ato dakṣiṇe bhūkhaṇḍe vāmāyanam, uttarā- yana (ṇa) miti, makaragate ravau
evaṃ kumbhasūrye dvitīye bhūkhaṇḍe
mīne tṛtīyakhaṇḍe ayanamapi nṛṇāṃ meṣasūrye caturthe
paścime bhūkhaṇḍe caturtha iti
bhūtākhye pañcame'rke vṛṣasthe sati mithunagataravau ṣaṇmahau uttarāyanaṃ (ṇaṃ) bhavati
saptame meroruttare
mārttaṇḍe karkasthe bhavati harigate cāṣṭame bhūmikhaṇḍe kanyārandhre tulārke bhavati ca daśame vṛścike rudrakhaṇḍe
cāpasthe dvādaśe syādayanamapi nṛṇāṃ dvādaśāre ca bhūmyām evaṃ savyāyanaṃ syād ravigamanavaśāt karkaṭādau ca rāśau // 57
mārttaṇḍe karkaṭasthe vāmāyanaṃ bhavati harigate aṣṭame bhūkhaṇḍe bhavati
kanyāgate sūrye randhe navame bhūkhaṇḍe tulārke bhavati ca daśame bhūkhaṇḍe vṛścikasūrye rudra ekādaśame khaṇḍe re
cāpasthe dvādaśe khaṇḍe syādayanaṃ nṛṇāmapi sambhāvane, dvādaśāre ca bhūmyāṃ sthitānāṃ lokānāmuttarāyanaṃ (ṇaṃ) dvādaśarāśibhedeneti
evaṃ savyāyanaṃ syāt
evamukta- krameṇa dakṣiṇāyanaṃ ravigamanavaśāt kakaṃṭādau ca rāśau bhavati
asmin merordakṣiṇe laghujambūdvīpabhūkhaṇḍe dakṣiṇāyanaṃ karkaṭāditye bhavati
evaṃ dakṣiṇāvartte ca siṃhāditye dvitīye tṛtīye kanyāditye tulāditye caturthe meroḥ paścime dakṣiṇāyanaṃ bhavatīti
vṛści- kāditye pañcame dhanurāditye ṣaṣṭhe makarāditye saptame meroruttare kumbhāditye aṣṭame mīnā- ditye navame meṣāditye daśame vṛṣāditye ekādaśe mithunāditye dvādaśe bhūkhaṇḍe lokānāṃ dakṣiṇāyanaṃ dvādaśarāśibhedena bhavatīti
idānīṃ sūryasya bhūvalayādūrdhvaṃ krāntimānamucyate- ūrdhvaṃ ṣaṇṇāgasaṃkhyā tapanamapi ravaścāyane cottare'nte tasmāt kṣīṇaśca savye bhavati narapate rudrasaṃkhyāsahasram
ekāśītisahasraṃ śaraśatarahitaṃ madhyato golarekhā tasmād yadvadddvi pātaṃ khakhaśareṣūttare dakṣiṇe ca // 58
jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ ūrdhvaṃ ṣaṇṇāgasaṃkhyā iti
iha saptamabhūvalayād ūrdhvaṃ ṣaḍaśītisahasrayojana- saṃkhyā tapanamapi raveścāyane' cottare 'nte dakṣiṇāyanādau svasvakhaṇḍe karkaṭādau dakṣiṇāyanaṃ svasvasaṃkrāntidine bhavati dvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu yathākramamiti
tasmāt kṣīṇaśca savye bhavati narapate rudrasaṃkhyāsahasram
tasmāt dakṣiṇāyanasaṃkrāntidināt savye'yane uttarāyanaṃ (ṇaṃ) yāvat kṣīṇo bhavatyekādaśasahasraṃ pañcasaptatisahasraṃ tapanaṃ sūryasyordhvaṃ veditavyamiti ekāśītisahasraṃ sa (śa) rapañcaśatarahitaṃ madhyato gola rekhā bhavati, yojanamānam
tasyā golarekhāyā yadvad dvipātaṃ (yad vṛddhipātaṃ) bhavati sūryasya tat khakhaśareṣu iti pañcaśatādhikapañcasahasrāṇi yojanānāmiti, uttare rekhāyā dakṣiṇe ca svasvakhaṇḍe rāśibhogavaśeneti
idānīṃ sūryasya pratidinaṃ tiryakkrāntirdvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu savyottare ucyate- kṣārābdhi laṅghayitvā vrajati dinakaro dakṣiṇe yāvadagni kailāśa ( sa ) syottare ca vrajati himagiriṃ cottare cottarasthaḥ
bāṇāstithayāhatāśca vrajati digayane yojanānāṃ sahasraṃ sārddhākṣā hīndulabdhaṃ tyajati dinadine pañcabhūtaḥ samantāt //59
kṣārābdhiṃ laṅghayitvā vrajati dinakaro dakṣiṇe yāvadagnimiti
iha dvādaśāra- bhūmyāṃ pratye kaikakhaṇḍe rāśivaśāt kṣārābdhi laṅghayitvā dakṣiṇāyane sthitaḥ sūryo dakṣiṇāgnivalayādikaṃ yāvad vrajati samudrāntasīmna iti
tataḥ sīmno'gnivalayāt kailāśa ( sa ) syottare vrajati himagirim (:), uttare mārge uttarāyana (ṇa) stha: sūryo dakṣiṇāyanā- didinaṃ yāvat
bāṇāstithayāhatāśceti
bāṇa iti pañca, tithiriti pañcadaśa; tābhiḥ pañca daśabhirhatā bāṇāstithayāhatā iti pañcasaptatirbhavati'
taṃ ( i ) pañcasaptatisahasrasaṃkhyāṃ yojanānāṃ vrajati dinakaro digayane himādagni yāvada- gnerhimavantaṃ yāvat pañcasaptatisahasraṃ yojanānāṃ dakṣiṇāyanādidigvibhāgaḥ; taṃ dig- vibhāgaṃ ṣaḍmāsairyathākrameṇa vrajati uttarāyaṇe dakṣiṇāyane ceti
sārddhākṣā hīndulabdhaṃ tyajati dinadine iti
sārddhadvayaśītiśata bhāgalabdhaṃ pañcasaptatisahasrāt pratidinadig- vibhāgaṃ yojanānāṃ tyajati sūryaḥ
evamubhayāyanabhogena rāśicakrasya maṇḍaladināni pañcaṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayasaṃkhyā [kā] ni raverbhavanti
evaṃ dvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu tiryakkrāntiḥ sūryasya veditavyeti
idānīṃ pratyekarāśisthitasya sūryasya dvādaśakhaṇḍabhramaṇavaśād dvādaśamāsabheda
ucyate- rāśāvake sthito'rkaḥ sakalamṛtugaṇaṃ māsapakṣān karoti
khaṇḍe khaṇḍe ca māso bhavati bhuvitale dvādaśāre krameṇa
yanmānaṃ yatra khaṇḍe bhavati dinavaśāt saptame tanniśāyāṃ trailakṣādarddhamārge dinamapi purataḥ pṛṣṭhato'rkasya rātriḥ || 60 ||
rāśāveke sthito'rkaḥ sakalamṛta gaṇaṃ māsapakṣān karotīti
ihaikarāśī yatra kutracit meṣādike sthitaḥ sūryo dvādaśakhaṇḍe ṣaḍ ṛtūn dvādaśamāsān caturviṃśati pakṣān karoti ; rāśicakrabhramaṇavaśena;
iha meroḥ sarvadi gbhāge yo rāśicakra bhramaṇavaśād dakṣiṇāgni spṛśati, sa saptamakhaṇḍadikprāpto meruśṛṅga spṛśati himaparvato'ddhvaṃ vrajatīti nyāyāt sarvamṛtugaṇādikaṃ sakalaṃ pratyekaṃ rāśau sthitasyārkasya veditavyamiti
khaṇḍe khaṇḍe ca māso bhavati bhuktile dvādaśāre krameṇeti
iha dvādaśāre bhuvitale pratyekakhaṇḍe pratyekamāso bhavati ekarāśau sthitasya sūryasya yena prakāreṇa tathā kathyate- iha ekarāśinirdeśena sarvarāśayo veditavyā iti
atra laghujambūdvīpe merordakṣiṇe meṣādirāśayaḥ kila prasiddhāḥ, vasantādaya ṛtavaḥ, caitrādayo māsāḥ evaṃ pakṣāḥ sarve iti; tasmāt tasmin meṣarāśau sthito'rko yathā dvādaśakhaṇḍe sarvamṛtugaṇādikaṃ karoti tathocyate — iha merordakṣiṇakhaṇḍe meṣastho'rko vasanta ṛtudhaiśākhamāsaṃ karoti, agni- koṇārddha dvitīyakhaṇḍe jyeṣṭhamāsaṃ karoti; evamagnikoṇāt parārddhakhaṇḍe āṣāḍhaṃ karoti, tṛtīye evaṃ caturthe meroḥ pūrvakhaṇḍapūrvavidehe śrāvaṇamāsaṃ karoti, pañcame īśārddhe khaṇḍe bhādrapadaṃ karoti, īśakoṇe aparārddhe ṣaṣṭhe khaṇḍe āśvinaṃ karoti, meroruttare saptame khaṇḍe kārtikaṃ karoti, vāyavyakoṇārddha khaṇḍe aṣṭame mārgaśīrṣaṃ karoti, vāyavyāparārddhe koṇe khaṇḍe navame puṣyaṃ karoti, meroḥ paścime daśame khaṇḍe māghaṃ karoti, naiṛtyakoṇā- parārddha khaṇḍe phālgunaṃ karoti, naiṛtyakoṇāparārddha khaṇḍe caitraṃ karotīti
evaṃ māsadvayena khaṇḍadvaye ṛturbhavati;
pratyekakhaṇḍe śuklakṛṣṇapakṣabhedena pakṣadvayaṃ bhavati
evaṃ ṣaḍ ṛtavo dvādaśa māsāḥ caturviṃśati pakṣā dvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu cakrākārā bhramanto jñātavyā iti
asmin dakṣiṇakhaṇḍe yo māso dvādaśarāśivaśena bhavati saṃkrāntau so'parasaṃkrāntī dakṣiṇāvarttena naiṛtyakoṇe gacchati, naiṛtyāddha koṇastho'parakoṇārddhaṃ gacchati, apara- koṇārddhastho meroraparagodānyāṃ gacchati, aparagodānyāṃ sthito vāyavyārddhakoṇaṃ gacchati, vāyavyādarddhakoṇastho'parakoṇāddhaṃ gacchati, aparakoṇāddha stha uttarakuru vrajati, uttarakurustha īśānakoṇāddhaṃ gacchati, tatrastho'parakoṇārddhaṃ gacchati, apara- naiṛtyakoṇārddhaṃ ga. godānīṃ paṭale] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ koṇārddha sthaḥ pūrvavidehaṃ gacchati, pūrvavidehastho'gnikoṇārddhaṃ khaṇḍaṃ vrajati, pūrvāddha stho'para- koṇārddhaṃ vrajati, aparakoṇārddhastho dakṣiṇajambūdvīpaṃ vajrati iti dvādaśamāsānāṃ pratisaṃkrāntivaśena dvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu saṃcaraṇaṃ jñātavyamiti
yanmānaṃ yatra khaṇḍe bhavati dinavaśāt saptame tanniśāyāṃ bhavati
iha uttarāyaṇa- saṃkrāntī dakṣiṇakhaṇḍe divāmānaṃ kṣīṇaṃ rātrimānaṃ vṛddham, tadevottarakurau saptame bhūmikhaṇḍe ādityodayavaśena dakṣiṇe rātrivṛddhivaśena tanmānaṃ tvatra na bhavati; jambūdvīpe dakṣiṇodayavaśena saptame rātrihānivaśena tato dakṣiṇe bhavati
evaṃ sarvatra pratyekakhaṇḍe veditavyamiti
trailakṣādarddhamārge * dinamapi purataḥ pṛṣṭhato'rkasya rātririti
iha vṛttamānaṃ ṣaṭ- lakṣam; sūryasya gamanādarddha mārge trilakṣaṃ dinamiti
udayāstamanaṃ yāvadastamanādudayaṃ yāvat rātriḥ trilakṣañca
evamahorātraṃ veditavyamiti
idānīṃ dvādaśakhaṇḍasthānānmerusthitirucyate- sarveṣāñcottarastho bhavati narapate śailarājo janānāṃ pūrva sūryodayaḥ syād girisamamayanaṃ paścime cāstametat
kālāḥ sandhyāścatasra: praharadina niśāḥ sarvadā saṃkramanto merodikṣu bhramanti prakaṭaravimahau bheditā lagnabhedeḥ ||
sarveṣāñcottarastho bhavati narapate śailarājo nanānāmiti
iha dvādaśakhaṇḍe sthitānāṃ janānāṃ śailarājo meruruttare bhavati
pūrve sūryodayaḥ syāditi sarveṣāṃ meroḥ sarvakhaṇḍasthitānāñca yatra bhānurudayati sā pūrvadig bhavati; dakṣiṇe jambūdvīpasthānāṃ pūrvavidehaṃ pūrvadika; aparago dānyāṃ jambūdvīpaṃ pūrvadiguttarasthānāmaparagodānī pūrvadika; pūrvavidehasthānāmuttarakuruḥ pūrvadika; sūryabhramaṇodayavaśāditi
evaṃ koṇasthiteṣu aṣṭakhaṇḍeṣu jñātavyamiti
pañcapañcadaṇḍāntareṇa pratyekakhaṇḍe sūryodaya iti
girisamamayanamiti pratyekakhaṇḍe girisamaṃ merorabhimukhaṃ sūryagamanamuttare dakṣiṇe ca paścime cāstametat
evaṃ dvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu yathāpūrve udayastathā paścime'stametadveditavyamiti
kālāstrayaḥ - prabhāto madhyāhno vikāla iti
sandhyāścatasraḥ - arddharātra - sandhyā pūrvasandhyā madhyāhnasandhyā vikālasandhyā
praharā aṣṭau (ṣṭa ) - divāyāṃ catvāraḥ, rātrau catvāraḥ
dinaniśaṃ (śā) sarvadā saṃkramantaḥ
evaṃ kālādayo'horātraṃ sarvasmin kāle saṃkramantaḥ khaṇḍāt khaṇḍaṃ merodikṣu bhramanti
* - vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- prakaṭaravimahau dvādaśāramahyāmiti'
bheditā lagnabhederiti
lagnā dvādaśa teṣāṃ bhedairbheditāḥ kālādayo bhramanti
catuścaturlagnaiḥ kālo bhramati; pratyekapratyeka sandhyā trilagnairbhramati; pratyeka praharaḥ sādardhalagnena bhramati
evaṃ pratyekalagnaṃ pañcadaṇḍairbhramati; ardhapraharaḥ praharārddhena bhramati; praharāddharddhana ra muhūrtta bhramati; ṣaṣṭipāṇīpalairghaṭikā bhramati; pratyekapāṇīpalaṃ ṣaṭśvāsairbhramati
evamahorātraṃ śvāsabhedena lagnabhedena kālo bhramati gacchati dehināmiti
idānīṃ meroḥ sarvadikṣu sūrya bhramaṇamānamucyate- nāḍyabde ṣaṭsapādaṃ ca ( na ?) kramati dinakaro yojanānāṃ sahasraṃ ṣaṣṭhīnāmagnilakṣaṃ bhramati dinaniśaṃ pañcaśailaṃ sahasrama
golārddha khāgni nāḍīrvahati dinaniśaṃ hānivṛddhayośca pañca gole savyāva ( pa ) savyaṃ pratidinamapi tata śvāsayugmaṃ triliptam ||
nāḍyabdhe (bde ) ṣaṭ sapādaṃ kramati dinakaro yojanānāṃ sahasramiti
nāḍyabdhe- (bde) ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśataśvāsasaṃkhyākāle ṣaṭ sapādaṃ pañcāśadviśatādhikaṣaṭsahasra- miti kramati dinakaro yojanānāṃ rāśistho'yamākāśe'to rāśerayaṃ bhramaṇavego na sūryasya rāsyā (śyā)dhāre sthitasyeti atra rāśicakraṃ savyāvartena bhramati, graharāśiṣvava ( pa ) savyena caranti
yathā rāśicakraṃ bhramati tathā rāhurapi saṃcarati paścimābhimukho meroḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kurvan
grahāḥ pūrvamukhā merora (roḥ) pradakṣiṇāṃ kurvanto rāśicakra saṃkramaṇaṃ kurvanti
evaṃ paramārthayuktyā rāhuḥ puṇyavān sūryādayo' duṣṭāḥ pāpagrahā ucyante iti
ṣaṣṭhīnāmagnilakṣamiti ṣaṣṭhīnāṃ nāḍīnāmadhve (bde) kāle ṣaṭśatādhikairṛviśat- sahasraśvāsasaṃkhyākāle
agnilakṣaṃ trilakṣam, pañcaśailaṃ sahasramiti pañcasaptatisahasra- saṃkhyā yojanānāṃ bhramati; dinaniśamahorātramiti
golārddha khāgni nāḍīvhati dinaniśamitīha viśu (ṣu) vasaṃkrāntidine pūrvoktagola rekhāyāṃ khāgniḥ triṃśannāḍī raho vahati, triśannāḍī rātriṃ vahati, sūryaḥ samamahorātramityarthaḥ
hānivṛddhayāśrayaṃ ( hānivṛdhayośca ) pañca gole savyāva ( pa ) savyamiti
tasmād golārddhāt savyāva ( pa ) - savyamāsatrayeṇa savye rātrervṛddhiḥ pañcanāḍyaḥ, ava ( pa ) savye māsatrayeṇa dinavṛddhi: pañcanāḍyaḥ
atra rātrivṛddhayā divāhānidivāvṛddhyā rātrehaniḥ jñātavyeti
pratidinamapi ataḥ anenādhāreṇātra mūle parivartanaṃ na vihitam
paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddaśaḥ tat śvāsayugmaṃ triliptamiti
atra savyāva ( pa ) savye trimāsānāṃ pratidinamahorātre vṛddhirhānirvā śvāsadvayaṃ trīṇi pāṇīpalāni
idaṃ mānaṃ kailāśa ( sa ) khaṇḍe, nāryaviṣayādike
āryaviṣaye ca pratyahorātraliptā dvayaṃ ( pāṇīpalāni ) vṛddhirvā hānirvā veditavyā; māsatrayeṇa madhyaviśru ( ṣu)vāt triṃśada- horātratulyamānān nāḍītrayaṃ hānirvā vṛddhirvā ayanavaśād veditavyeti
idānīṃ kailāśa ( sa ) khaṇḍabhedena dvādaśalagnānāmudayakāla ucyate- liptā syānmeṣalagne gagananavakaraṃ khartunetraṃ vṛṣe syāt yugme'pyākāśakhabde śararasaghaṭikāḥ syuḥ kulīre ca siṃhe
kanyāyāṃ sārddhaṣaṭkaṃ bhavati khalu tulādyutkrameṇaiva sarvaṃ ṣaḍa lagnarastametaduditamapi tathā'rkaṃnakṣatrabhedaiḥ || 63 ||
liptā syānmeṣalagne iti
iha meṣalagne prathamodayādaparalagnodayaṃ yāvat kālo bhavati
gagananavakaraṃ liptāpāṇīpalapiṇḍaṃ navatyadhikaśatadvayam, ataḥ ṣaṣṭibhāgena ghaṭikācatuṣṭayaṃ pañcāśat pāṇīpalāni meṣalagnodayakāla iti kailāśa ( sa ) viṣaye niyamaḥ
khartunetraṃ vṛṣe syāditi
evaṃ vṛṣalagnodayakālaḥ ṣaṣṭya ttaraśatadvayaṃ pāṇīpala- piṇḍaṃ catasro nāḍyo viśatipāṇīpalānyudayakālaḥ
yugme'pyākāśakhabde yugme'pi mithune udayakālaḥ śatadvayaṃ liptāpiṇḍaṃ nāḍyastisraḥ pāṇīpalāni viṃśatiriti
sa (śa) rarasaghaṭikāḥ syuriti śara (:) pañcaghaṭikā kulīre karka lagne udayakālaḥ
tataḥ siṃhe rasa iti ṣaṭpaṭikālagnodaye kāla iti kanyāyāṃ sārddhaṣaṭkaṃ kanyālagnodayakālaḥ ṣaṭ ghaṭikā triṃśat pāṇīpalāni sādarddhaṣaṭkaṃ bhavatīti
tulādyutkrameṇaiva sarvam
ataḥ ṣaḍlagna kālāvadheraparaṣaḍlagne tulādiṣūtkrameṇa
evamanena vidhinā sarvaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ bhavatīti niyamaḥ
atra lagnodayakāle tulāyāṃ ṣaṭ ghāṭikā triṃśat pāṇīpalāni vṛścike ṣaḍ, dhanuṣi pañca, makare tisro nāḍyaḥ, pāṇīpalāni viṃśatiriti, kumbhe catasro nāḍyaḥ, viśati pāṇīpalāni; mīne ghaṭikāścatasraḥ, pāṇīpalāni pañcāśaditi kailāsaviṣaye niyamaḥ
ṣaḍlagnairastametaduditamiti, tathā hi-yasmāt ṣaḍlagnairuditamapi tasmāt astametad bhavatīti
arkasya nakṣatrabhederiti sārddhatrayodaśanakṣatrānte astametadudayastathā sārddhatrayodaśanakṣatrānte ahorātrabhedena ṣaḍlagnaiḥ sārddhatrayodaśanakṣatrairdivākālo bhavati; ṣaḍlagnaiḥ sārddhatrayodaśanakṣatra rātrikālo bhavati; lagnodayakālavaśena divārātri ( hīnādhiko (T) bhavati, sūryasañcāravaśāditi

* bhoṭānusāram |
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- pañcānām |
etanmāsatra yeṇābdadinaṛṇaghaṭī, aśītyuttarasahasraghaṭī, bhakti reṣāṃ trayāṇā- miti siddhāntakaṭa (T) kṣavacanāt mañjuśriyā gaditam [75 a] |
māse bhuktirguroḥ syāt prakaṭitaghaṭikā rudrapādāsasaṃkhyā triṃśat pāṇīpalāni sphuṭayugaghaṭikā sūryaputrasya bhuktiḥ |
ekībhūtā dinākhyā triguṇitamapi tat sārddhamāsaḥ sarātraḥ bharbhuktau dinānāṃ pratidivasamṛṇaṃ ṣaṭgrahāṇāṃ ca bhuktiḥ || 75 ||
māse bhaktirguroḥ syādityādi siddhāntavṛttamidaṃ kaṭākṣārthamuktaṃ lokarūḍhikṣayā- thaṃm |
atra ye tripakṣāste ketudayabhogasambandhinaḥ pañcagrahāṇāṃ mūlasiddhānte uktāḥ, ato na te bṛhaspatiśaninorbhoge deyā iti' |
idānīṃ rāhubhogāt ketubhuktirucyate- māsaikaṃ mandakarmaṇyapi ca dhanavibhornānubhuktaṃ grahairyat tasmād vai sārddhasaptatrigaṇitaghaṭikā bhuktiratraiva māse |
rāhormāsasya bhuktiḥ saraviśaśipadaṃ cārddha nāḍīvihīnaṃ tisro'rkasyāyanāḍyo śaśimadanadine pañca candrasya nāḍyaḥ || 76 |
māsaikamityādi |
iha prāṅmandakārye sādarddhaṣaṭtriṃśanmāsāvaśeṣaṃ yanmāsaṃ grahairnānubhuktaṃ tadeva māsamaṣṭaguṇāhataṃ tataḥ ṣaṭhyaṃśenāṣṭaghaṭikā grāhyā, tāsu sārdhasapta- ghaṭikā rāhormāsaparibhogaḥ |
ta traiveti sūryamāsapramāṇe rāhormāsasya bhuktiḥ |
saraviśaśipadamiti raveḥ sattvarajastamāsi tripadāni, śaśino gandharasarūpasparśaśabdaguṇāḥ pañcapadāni etānyaṣṭapadāni rāho sārddhasaptaghaṭikā rūpāṇi śeṣārddhaghaṭo nistamorūpā iti, tayā hīnaṃ cādarddhanāḍāvihonam |
tisyo rkasyāyanāḍye makarasaṃkrāntau prathamapakṣe tisro nāḍyaḥ sattvarajastamātmikā dhanabhogaḥ |
evaṃ karkaṭaprathamapakṣe ṛṇaparibhoga iti |
śaśimadanadine iti prathamadine prathamapade piṇḍasthāne caturdaśa bhāgāvaśeṣe trayodaśame vā pañca candrasya nāḍyo bhavanti pañcaguṇātmikā iti |
no bhuktā yārddhanāḍī ṝtudinasamaye sā kalāhīnacandrā sā sūryasyādhikā syāt khalu yugaga ṇitā sūryamāse dinaṃ tat |
rāhormāsasya bhogānnayanaviga ṇitāt pakṣabhogena bhaktāt labdhāḥ ketośca nāḍyaḥ pratidinasamaye māsabhogācca rāhoḥ || 77 |
paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ no bhuktā yādarddhanāḍī, rāhuṇā na bhuktā, ṛtudinasamaye catuḥṣaṣṭhidine, sā kalāhīnacandrā, candrasya naṣṭakaletyarthaḥ |
sā sūryasyādhikā syāt, sūryasya dvināḍī bhūtvā adhikā pratidinayugaguṇitā 'vedaistithyāhatam' ityādinā; yanmāsamadhyadinaṃ taṃ tadevādhikaṃ bhavati |
sūryamāse saṃkrāntimāse dinamekamadhikaṃ tadeva madhyamāṅgaṃ veditavyamiti |
rāhormāsasya bhogānnayanaviguṇitāditi rāhoḥ' ṛtubhogāt ' pañcadaśaghaṭikātaḥ pakṣabhogena bhaktāt, pādona caturbhirbhakāllabdhāḥ ketośca nāḍyaḥ catasraḥ pratidinasamaye; punarmāsabhogāt rāhoḥ |
hatvā saptārddhaliptāṃ khalu guṇaga uṇitā ketuḥ sūryeṇa sārddhaṃ vicarati purataḥ śodhayet sūryabhoge pṛṣṭhataḥ śīghracāre |
sūryabhogāt krameṇa cāre sāddha dviliptāṃ carati dinadine ṣaṭa triṃśadbhiḥ su māsaiścarati saghaṭikā lokatithyāhatāśca || 78 ||
hatvā saptārddhaliptāṃ saptalikā strayaḥ śvāsāḥ |
etāstriguṇairguṇitāḥ sārddhadvāviṃśallikā bhavantīti karaṇavivakṣā svarūpataḥ |
sārdhaistribhirguṇairyā yatra sūryasya pratyahaṃ catasro nāḍyaḥ ṣaḍviṃśatipāṇīpalāni bhuktiriti, śodhayet sūryabhoge karaṇavivakṣāyāḥ |
asau sūryabhogaḥ pratyahaṃ catasro ghaṭikā viṃśatipāṇīpalāni siddhānte ṣaṭpāṇīpalānyadhikānīti, tasmin sūryabhoge śodhayet, śodhitāvaśeṣaṃ caraṇaṃ bhavati |
ketuḥ sūryeṇa sādhaṃ vicarati purataḥ śīghracāre krameṇa, pṛṣṭhato vakracāre* utkrameṇa caraṇaṃ ca raṇaṃ carati |
cāre sārddhaṃ dviliptāṃ carati dinadine sūryabho- go t krameṇotkrameṇa vā |
ṣaṭtriṃśadbhiḥ su māsaiścarati saghaṭikā lokatithyāhataṃ ca tribhiḥ pañcadaśāhataṃ pañcacatvāriṃśaditi |
sārddhaṃ māsaṃ dvināḍī pratidinamudayaḥ śīghravakre'grapṛṣṭhe sārddhaṃ māsaṃ hi yāvad bhavati tadudayo dṛśyate martyaloke |
bhūyaḥ śrīkālacakre praviśati sa yadā varṣabhoge'pyadṛśyo jñāte tasyodayāṃśe tadudayamapi jñāyate'nantakālam || 79 ||
sārdhaṃ māsaṃ dvināḍī pratidinamudayaḥ, trivarṣāvasāne liptābhogān tyajya pratyahaṃ pakṣatrayaṃ yāvad dvināḍībhogaḥ śīghre agre vakre pṛṣṭhe |
evaṃ sāddhaṃ māsaṃ yāvat tasyodayaḥ, kintu māsamekaṃ nidhūmaḥ tato'ntimapakṣamekaṃ sadhūmaḥ tena sattvānāṃ prakaṭo dhūmaketuḥ |
gamanāgamanena māsamekaṃ bhavati, dhūmatyāgena nakṣatravat pratibhāti |
bhūyaḥ śrīkālacakra e trivarṣabhoge praviśati sa yadā varṣabhoge tadā'dṛśyo bhavati |
jñāte tasyo- dayāṃśe, evaṃ tasya ketorudayāṃśe jñāte sati, tadudayamapi yajjñāyate'nantakālamiti |
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- idānīṃ kālacakratrivarṣadināni pañcaviṃśat tattvaguṇitāni ayanadinahīnāni navasahitāni grahāṇāṃ maṇḍalānyucyante- varṣāhaḥ pañcaguṇyaṃ punariṣunihataṃ cāyanāhaḥ prahīṇaṃ raktandhremi bhavanti svagatiguṇavaśānmaṇḍalāni grahāṇām |
bhaume śailāhiṣaṭa kaṃ bhavati dinagaṇaṃ śailarandhrādināgaṃ saumye dantāgnivedaṃ bhavati suragurorbhārgave'gojinākṣī || 80 ||
varṣāha rityādi |
varṣāho'śītyuttaramekaṃ sahasram ta deva pañcaguṇyaṃ punariṣu- riti pañcabhinihataṃ guṇitamityarthaḥ |
evaṃ khaṇḍaguṇitaṃ pañcaviṃśadguṇitaṃ bhavati |
ayanāho- śītyuttaraśatam, tena hīnam, ekāyane grahāṇāṃ bhuktivaśādekāyanadinānyūnī bhavanti;
randhaimaśro bhavati, randhramiti nava, ṣatriguṇāstamiśrāṇi dinamaṇḍalāni grahāṇāṃ svasvagativaśāt pañcānāṃ bhavati nti, ekapiṇḍata ekonatriṃśadadhikāṣṭaśatottaraṣaṭsahasrā- yutadvayam, tato bhaume śailāhiṣaṭkamiti saptāśītyuttaraṣaṭśataṃ dinagaṇaṃ bhavati
śailarandhrādināgaṃ saptanavatyadhikaṃ saptaśatāṣṭasahasraṃ saumye bhavati
dantāgnivedaṃ dvātriṃśa- dadhikatriśatacatuḥsahasraṃ suragurorbhavati
bhārgave'gojinākṣī saptacatvāriṃśadadhika- dviśatadvisahasraṃ bhavati
ṣaṭṣaṭśailākṣaraikaṃ bhavati dinagaṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ sūryaṃ sūno śailaveṃkañca bhaume khalu bhavati dhanaṃ candraputre śataghnam
hīnaṃ vaha nayarkaśaile daśaga ṇitadinaṃ bhārgave'bdhyaṣṭahīnaṃ śūnyākāśarta netraṃ khakaraphaṇiyutaṃ mantriputro ṛṇaṃ syāt || 81 ||
bhaume sārddhā navārddhādhikanṛpati budhe sūryasū kṣaṃ gurau syāt ṣaṭ śukre doṣamaṃ kā vṛṇamiti sakala śodhayed bhuktimadhye
paścācchuddha śca teṣāṃ bhavati narapate janmanakṣatracārāt svāṅge nīcasya śuddhirbhavati gativaśāduccakasyārkamadhye || 82 ||
ṣaṭṣaṭśelāsva (mba) raikam, ṣaṭṣaṣṭhyadhikaṃ saptaśatāyutamekaṃ bhavati dinagaṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ sūryasūnoriti
śailaveṃkañca bhauma ityādi pūrvoktamiti ṭīkāyāṃ vṛttadvayaṃ yāvaditi
idānīṃ grahāṇāṃ janmaśuddhayarthaṃ nakṣatrasamuccayamucyate- ṣaṇmāsairmāsabhuktaṃ guṇitamapi bhavet kālacakrābdamāse- rbhāge labdhaṃ ṛṇaṃ syādapi raviśaśinā nānubhuktārddhanāḍī

paṭale] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ ketorbhayo'dhikaṃ yat pratidinasamaye guṇyamindordinaiśca ṣaṣṭyā bhāgena nāḍī pratidinasamaye śīghravakrodaye ca || 83 ||
ṣaṇmāsairmāsabhuktamiti tadupari śuddhiḥ siddhānte, kintu sattvāśayavaśena karaṇā- pekṣā iti
ketūdaye punastripakṣadinaiścārapadaṃ guṇitaṃ sārddhadvādaśādhikaśataliptāpiṇḍaṃ bhavati
tadeva pratidine udayāt ketorbhavati iti kāryaṃ veditavyam
yārddhanāḍī ṛkṣa- ṛṇasthāne labdheti mātṛmodakavākyaṃ sattvānāṃ pācanāyeti
idānīṃ grahāṇāṃ cārabalamucyate- saumyo mantrī ca śīghre prabhavati balavān bhaumaketū ca vakre mandaḥ śukraśca mande prakṛtiguṇavaśāt śeṣacāre'balāśca
śīghre pūrvā mukhāḥ syuḥ punaraparamukhā vakracāre praviṣṭā mande savyānanāḥ syuḥ svagatiguṇavaśānnirgame cottarāsyāḥ ||84 ||
sauma ityādi
saumyo budhaḥ, mantrī bṛhaspatiḥ śīghraṃ śīghracāre uditaḥ san pūrvārdhe prabhavati balavāniti
bhaumaśca ketuśca bhaumaketū vakre arddhamaṇḍale parityakte utkramabhogāt balavān bhavati
mandaḥ śaniḥ śukraśca mandakrameṇāparārdhe balavāniti
prakṛtiguṇavaśād vānta (dhātu) guṇa' vaśāditi
śeṣacāre cāratraye abalāśca bhaumādaya iti
śīghre pūrvā mukhāḥ syuḥ, sarvagrahāḥ śīghracāre pūrvārdhe pūrvā mukhāḥ syuḥ ; punaste te aparamukhāḥ paścimamukhāḥ vakracāre praviṣṭāḥ santaḥ, mande krameṇāparārdhe savyānabhā- (nanā) bhavanti
svagatiguṇavaśānnirgame cotkrameṇāparārdhe uttaramukhā bhavantoti
etat svarodaya bhūmibalārthaṃ vākyam
sūryacandrapadānyucyante- māsaistriṃśaddinā ye jalanidhinihatāḥ khākṣicandraṃ yadīndo rāśīnāṃ dvādaśānāṃ yugaguṇitapadaṃ cāṣṭavedaṃ veśca, tanmadhye hānivṛddhī tvayanagativaśādutkrameṇa krameṇa cakrārddhārddha samastā vasuyugagaṇanā veditavyā narendra ||85||
mārseviśaddinā ye jalanidhinihatā, triṃśaddināścaturbhiguṇitāste, khākṣi- candraṃ yadindoḥ, viṃśatyadhikaśataṃ padaṃ bhavati, candrasya saptadināvadheḥ kalāvṛddhayarthaṃ vakṣyamāṇe vaktavyam
rāśīnāṃ dvādaśānāṃ caturbhiguṇitānāṃ cāṣṭavedaṃ raveḥ padaṃ ṣaṇmāsāvadherbhavati, tadeva vakṣyamāṇe vaktavyam
atra saṃgrahamātreṇaiṣāṃ padānāṃ niyamaḥ
tanmadhye hānivṛddhī tvayanagativaśādutkrameṇa [kumeṇa ] cakrārddhārddha samastā vasuyugagaṇanā, aṣṭacatvāriṃśad gaṇanā veditavyāḥ karaṇavivakṣāyāmiti
