paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ candramaṇḍale rāhupraveśalakṣaṇamucyate parvaccheda ityādi - parvacchede ca rāhoḥ praviśati śaśino maṇḍale maṇḍalaṃ ca sarvagrāso viśuddho bhavati śaśivaśānmaṇḍalaṃ ṣaṣṭināḍhyaḥ
ardhagrāso'rddhaśuddhe bhavati śaśivaśānnāḍikā yāvadasti chedo grāso ravīndodinaniśisamaye kṛṣṇaśukle ca pūrve || 86 ||
[lokadhātu- parvacchede, pūrṇāvasāne, amāvāsyāvasāne vā, rāhoḥ praviśati maṇḍale śaśino maṇḍalam ; maṇḍalaśabdenātra ṣaṣṭighaṭikā, ekanakṣatraparibhogaḥ, sa ca dvādaśarāśyātmakaḥ, ṣoḍaśakalātmako veditavyaḥ, sarvagrāsavaśāditi, sarvagrāso viśuddho bhavati
yadi rāhubhogena candrabhogaḥ pariśuddhastadā sarvagrāsaḥ ṣaṣṭināḍikāparyantam
ardhagrāso'rddha bhoge śuddhe sati śaśicaraṇavaśānmaṇḍalaṃ ṣaṣṭināḍyoścandrasya ṣoḍaśakalātmakam
evaṃ śaśi- caraṇavaśānnāḍikā yāvadasti rāhubhoge praviṣṭā tāvadekakalāyāścaturthāṃśaṃ yāvad graha- samāgamo jñeya iti'
etat siddhānte rāho va janādikaṃ vistareṇa bāhyajñānārthaṃ veditavyam
asmin tantre laghuhetuto mañjuśriyā na prakāśitam
atra yadadhyātmopayogyaṃ tadevoktaṃ saṃkṣepata iti
evaṃ grahayuddhādikaṃ samastaṃ siddhānte jñātavyam
atra tantre siddhāntāpekṣā jyotiṣaviṣaye grahayuddhādike; adhyātmani grahayuddhavivakṣā nāstīti bhagavato niyamaḥ
na bāhye grahaṇe jāte sati sattvānāṃ śukragrahaṇaṃ bhavatyadhyātmani; ravigrahaṇād rajograhaṇaṃ bhavatīti niyamaḥ sarvatra nāsti
tasmād bauddharbāhyaparijñānārthaṃ brahmasūryayamanakaromaka- siddhāntaṃ (T: ) jñātavyamiti ( jñātavyā iti ) bhagavato niyamaḥ
idānīṃ sarvagrahāṇāṃ caturyugānte śūnyacaraṇapraveśa ucyate mānuṣyāṇāmityādi- mānuṣyāṇāṃ śatābdaṃ guṇitamapi bhaved varṣabhuktyā grahāṇāṃ dvisthaṃ digbhāgalabdhaṃ bhavati ca tad ṛṇaṃ śodhayenmūni rāśau
viśatyekaṃ hi lakṣaṃ ṣaḍayutamapi ca ṣaṭvarṣasaṃkhyā yugāddhe bhūyo rāśidvayena prabhavati niyataṃ varṣasaṃkhyā caturṇām ||87||
mānuṣyāṇāṃ śatābdaṃ yat tadguṇitaṃ varṣabhuktyā grahāṇāmiti kālacakreṇa saha grahāṇāṃ sārddhadinena varṣaśatabhuktiḥ, tridinairdviśatam, triṃśaddinairdvisahasram ṣaṣṭyuttaratriṃśaddinaiḥ caturviṃśativarṣasahasra bhuktirbhavati
tayā varṣaśataṃ guṇitaṃ dvisthaṃ digbhāgandham, daśabhāgena labdham, mūni rāśau ṛṇaṃ śodhanīyaṃ bhavati; avaśiṣṭaṃ viśatyekaṃ hi lakṣaṃ ṣaḍayutamiti ‍ iti nāsti
paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ ekaviṃśatilakṣaṃ ṣaḍayutamapi yad varṣasaṃkhyā yugā bhavati; yugārddhāntāt pañca padāni - kṛd- yugasya catvāri padāni tretāyāḥ padamekamiti, yugārddhamākāśavāyutejaudakapṛthvīmaṇḍalā- tmakam, yathākramaṃ bharturvāmanāḍyāṃ' prāṇasañcāra iti
bhūyo rāśidvayena punadvatīyarāśinā pañcapadātmakena
tatra tretāyāḥ padadvayam, dvāparasya padadvayam, kalerekapadam, pṛthivyaptejo- vāyuśūnyātmakam; yathānu krameṇa dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ bhartuḥ prāṇasañcāraḥ pañcamaṇḍalātmaka iti
evamubhayanāḍī maṇḍalavarṣa rāśidvayena varṣasaṃkhyā caturṇāṃ yugānāṃ dvayayutādhika- iti; atra pratyekayugānām
śūnyaṃ śūnyaṃ khanāgāḥ karamuniśaśinaḥ kṛyugasya pramāṇaṃ tretāyāṃ khaṃ khaśūnyaṃ rasanavadinakṛd varṣasaṃkhyā prasiddhā
śūnyaṃ śūnyaṃ khavedaṃ rasabhujagamiti dvāpare'bdāni samyak
śūnyākāśaṃ khanetraṃ guṇajalanidhayo varṣasaṃkhyā kalau syāt || 88||
śūnyaṃ śūnyaṃ khanāgāḥ karamuniśaśina iti varṣāṇāṃ kṛdayugasya pramāṇam, caturmaṇḍalapadopabhogata iti |
tretāyāḥ khaṃ khaśūnyaṃ rasavadinakṛditi varṣa saṃkhyā prasiddheti |
śūnyaṃ śūnyaṃ khavedaṃ rasabhujagamiti catuḥṣaṣṭisahasrādhikāṣṭalakṣāṇi dvāpare'bdāni samyag bhavantīti |
śūnyākāśaṃ khanetraṃ guṇajalanidhaya iti varṣa saṃkhyā kalau syāditi |
evaṃ tretāyāḥ tripādāḥ, dvāpare dvau, kalau tvekapadam ; tridvayekamaṇḍalātmakaḥ, pratyekamaṇḍalaṃ kalau varṣamāneneti |
etairvarṣairyugānte grahagaṇacaraṇaṃ tiṣṭhate rāśiśūnye aśvinyādyaṃ ca bhūyaḥ prabhava iti tathā caitramāsādikaṃ ca |
vāro yoga stithi karaṇamapi tathā cādhikaṃ tatra kāle devānāṃ dānavānāṃ kṣititalanilaye raudrayuddhaṃ bhaviṣyati || 89 ||
etairvarṣeṣu gānte grahagaṇacaraṇaṃ tiṣṭhate rāśiśūnye iti |
etairvarṣaipūrvokta- yugānte caturyugānte |
grahagaṇetyādi subodham |
revatyante (rāśiśūnye tiṣṭhati |)
aśvinyādyaṃ ca nakṣatraṃ bhūyo grahāṇāṃ bhuktirbhavati |
prabhava iti yathā bhavati, caitramāsādikaṃ ca tathā bhavati |
vāro yogastithirve karaṇamapi tathā cādhikaṃ tatra kāle |
ādityavāraḥ, viṣkambhayogaḥ, pratipat śuklapakṣe tithiḥ, karaṇaṃ vavamityādikam; tasmin kāle |
devānāṃ na likhitam api tu vyākhyānaṃ prastutam |

paṭale ] svarodaya yantra vidhiniyamamahodda eśaḥ bhadrā saumye dvitīyo nṛpati (mriyate ) yadi maghāyoga evātra vāre aśleṣākṣī (kṣaṃ) jayāyāṃ yadi bhavati gururyāti mṛtyuṃ tṛtīyaḥ |
riktātithyāṃ dhaniṣṭhā yadi bhavati bhṛguryāti mṛtyuṃ caturthaḥ pūrṇā mandena sārddhaṃ prabhavati bharaṇī pañcamo yāti mṛtyum ||
|| evaṃ bhadrā saumye yadi maghānakṣatraṃ bhavati, tadā dvitīya ikārasvaranāma ' ( T ) striyati (te) |
evaṃ jayāyāṃ bṛhaspatau yadi aśleṣā bhavati, tadā tṛtīya ṛkāra- svaranāmā mriyate |
evaṃ riktāyāṃ śukro yadi bhavati dhaniṣṭhā, tadā caturtha ukārasvara- nāmā mriyate ; tathā pūrṇā śanau yadi bharaṇī [pra]bhavati, tadā pañcama lṛkārasvara- nāmā mṛtyuṃ yāti |
evaṃ svarāṇā muditānāmapi nakṣatrakā (vā) ra vaśāt mṛtyuyogo bhavati |
sūrye nandodayānāṃ vraṇamapi ca bhavet kleśa evāturāṇāṃ saumye (some) bhadrodayānāṃ prabhavati niyataṃ yuddhe roge ca tadvat |
satyaṃ pañcodayānāṃ bhavati bahuphalaṃ mṛtyu yogavihīnaṃ jñātavyaṃ deśayātrā vivāhe ||
|| rogayuddhe'pyaśubhāśubhaphalaṃ tathā sūryavāre nando' 'dayānāṃ vraṇaṃ raṇe bhavati, āturāṇāṃ kleśo bhavati |
evaṃ somavāre bhadrodayānāṃ jñeyaṃ satyaṃ pañcodayānāṃ' ākārādīnāṃ svasvatithāvuditānāṃ bahuphalaṃ mṛtyuyogavihīnaṃ bhavatīti , tadeva naimittikena jñātavyam, roge yuddha cāśubhaśubhaphalaṃ ' deśayātrāyāṃ vivāhakāle ceti |
evaṃ pakṣe nakṣatravārāḥ svaragaṇa tithayaḥ pañcatattvaprabhedaiḥ māse'yane'bde'pyudaya iha bhavet ṣaṣṭisaṃvatsare ca |

atrāśubhāḥ ādityo anurādhayā, somaḥ kṛttikayā, bhaumaḥ śatabhiṣayā, budho'- śvinyā, gururmṛgaśīrṣayā ', bhṛguḥ rohiṇyā, śanirhastena - ete mṛtyuyogāḥ
dagdhatithīrvaja- yitvā sarvavāraiḥ sarvāḥ tithayaḥ śuddhā viśiṣṭabhogaṃ vivarjya
atra dagdhatithayaḥ- ādityena dvādaśī dagdhā, somenaikādaśī' dagdhā
dagdhā maṅgalena daśamī, budhena tṛtīyā, bṛhaspatinā ṣaṣṭhī, śukreṇa dvitīyā, śaniścareṇa saptamīti
etāḥ tithayaḥ śubhakārye varjanīyāḥ, aśubhakārye sarvāḥ krūrā grahatithyādayo grāhyā iti
atra sarve yogāḥ praśastāḥ vyatīpātaṃ parighaṃ vaidhṛtiṃ varjayitvā
saumyakārye tathā viṣkambhe trighaṭikā varjanīyāḥ
pañcaśūle ca varjayet; ṣaṭ gaṇḍe ca, atigaṇḍe ca, nava vyāghāte varje ca ( varjayet ) iti yogāśuddhiḥ
evaṃ śuddhavāre tithau ceti lokavyavahāreṇa vārādipariśuddhiḥ, paramārthataḥ sattvānāṃ pūrvakarmāpārjitaṃ śubhāśubhaphalaṃ bhavatīti tathāgataniyamaḥ
idā [82b]nīṃ tārādibalaṃ lokavyavahāreṇocyate kṛtvā ṛkṣa (tvṛkṣa) - mityādi- kṛtvā tvṛkṣaṃ tribhāgaṃ navanavanava kairjanmanakṣatramādau mṛtyurevāturāṇām
krūrāścandreṇa sārdhaṃ viṣamapadagatā aśvinyādya strināḍyāmahirapi racito revatī yāvadeva pāpenduṃ janmaṛkṣaṃ bhavati yadi nṛṇāmekanāḍyāṃ vināśaḥ || 112 ||
| | | | END_OF_PDF_PAGE_177 vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- kṛtvā tvṛkṣaṃ' tribhāgamiti etat saptaviṃśati nakṣatrāṇi tribhāgāni navanavanavakairiti teṣu navake[ṣu ] pratyekanavakeṣu (navake) janmanakṣatraṃ rogiṇo yodhasya saṃgrāme ādau prathamaṃ navanavakād bhavanti ; tata ekāntaritaṃ nakṣatraṃ viṣamapadam; tasmin viṣamapade yadi krūrā ādityamaṅgalaśaninā lagne (kālāgna ) yaścandreṇa sārddhaṃ bhavanti, tadā mṛtyurevāturāṇāṃ saṃgrāme yodhasya vā
samapade jaya iti tārābalaniyamaḥ ' | idānīṃ phaṇicakramucyate aśvinyetyādi- aśvinyādyaiḥ saptaviṃśatinakṣatraistrināḍyāmahirapi racito revatī nakṣatraṃ yāvadeva pāpenduṃ pāpagrahaśca induśca pāpendu'; rogiṇo yodhasya vā yajjanmanakṣatraṃ yadi bhavati nṛṇāmekanāḍyām, tadā vināśo matyurbhavatīti trināḍī phaṇicakraniyamaḥ
kṛttikādi- naikāntarita ' trināḍyā tadvadādrādi ' ' nāḍi (tri) nakṣatrāntarite trināḍyāṃ candre gate yaḥ prathamaṃ yuddhabhūmi viśati sa vijayī bhavati nāḍībāhyeṣu trinakṣatre candre sthite yaḥ paścāt praviśati, sa vijayī bhavatīti tṛtīya phaṇicakraniyamaḥ

vimalaprabhāyāṃ idānīṃ pratītyasamutpādamu ( u ) cyate- puṣye māse tvavidyā makaragataravau [ lokadhātu- kumbhasūrye ca māghe saṃskāro mīnasūrye bhavati narapate phālgune meṣasūrye
caitravaiśākhajyeṣṭhe vijñānaṃ nāmarūpaṃ vṛṣamithunagate netrādyaṃ karkaṭe'rke bhavati hariravau sparśanaṃ vedanā ca ||
|| āṣāḍhe śrāvaṇe yoṣidapi tulagate māgha (bhādra) māse'śvine ca tṛṣṇopādānameva prabhavati ca bhavaḥ kārtike vṛścike ca

|| puṣye māghe'bhisandhau makaragataravau tatra vāre tvavidyā tasmānmṛtyudvitīye bhavati narapate jātirevaṃ tṛtīye
evaṃ sarvaṃ bhavādyaṃ kramagatiguṇitaṃ dvādaśāṅgāni yāvat tasmādvahrayabdhibāṇaṃ maraṇa (makara) gativaśāt ṣaṣṭhamāsaṃ (ṣaḍaṅga) kadācit ||

vijñānaṃ tadddvitīye prabhavati divase nāmarūpaṃ tṛtīye evaṃ māsadvayāṅgaṃ bhavati ravivaśāt sṛṣṭisaṃhārayogāt ||
|| atrāvidyāṃśa viṣamamapi bhavecchobhanaṃ sarvakārye saṃskārādya samaṃ yat tvaśubhamapi yadā sekayātrāvivāhe
evaṃ pakṣaprabhedaiḥ śaśigamanavaśād dvādaśāṅgāni yāni śukle kṛṣṇe ca pakṣe prathamatithivaśāt sṛṣṭisaṃhārayogāt ||

paṭale ] svarodayayantra vidhiniyamamahoddeśaḥ [a] ' puṣyetyādi
puṣye māse tu puṣyanirgame māghapraveśadine avidyā
atra dvādaśāraḥ [raṃ ] rāśicakraṃ makarādikam
tatra thapari [ prathame āre] makara iti dine gataravau kumbhasūrye ca māghe, cakārādatraiva māghānte phālguṇa (na) praveśadine saṃskāraḥ, evaṃ phālguṇā (nā) nte monacaitrādidine vijñānam, tathā caitrānte vaiśākhādidine meṣe nāmarūpam, evaṃ vaiśākhānte jyeṣṭhādivṛṣabhe dine ṣaḍāyatanam, tathā jyeṣṭhānte āṣāḍhādimithunasaṃkrānti - dine sparśaḥ
evamāṣāḍhānte śrāvaṇādikarkaṭa saṃkrāntidine vedanā
evaṃ śrāvaṇānte bhādra- padādisiṃhasaṃkrāntidine tṛṣṇā, tathā bhādrapadānte'śvinyādikanyāsaṃkrāntidine upādānam, evamaśvinyante kāttikāditulāsaṃkrāntidine bhavaḥ, tathā kārtikānte mārgaśīrṣādivṛścika- saṃkrāntidine jātiḥ, tathā mārgaśīrṣānte puṣyādidhanusaṃkrāntidine jarāmara [b]ṇamiti dvādaśāreṣu yathāsaṃkhyaṃ jñeyāni dvādaśāṅgāni
trisūryabhedaḥ ' candrabhedaḥ sarvatra prasiddhaḥ, puṣyapratipadādinā
ubhayagativaśāditi saṃkrāntivaśāt pakṣatithivaśāditi dvādaśāṅgāni
puṣye māghe'bhisandhau makaragataravau tatra vāre tvavidyā tasmād dinā [d] dvitīye jña dine jarāmaṇam tṛtīya vāre jātiḥ, caturthe bhavaḥ, pañcame upādānam
vedanā, aṣṭame sparśaḥ, navame ṣaḍāyatanam, daśame nāmarūpam, ekadaśame vijñānam, dvādaśame saṃskāra iti
yathā tṛtīye vāre tathā trayodaśame jātiḥ, caturdaśame bhavaḥ, pañcadaśame vāre upādānam
kadācit ṣoḍaśame vāre tṛṣṇeti sūryagatibhedenāṅgāni kvacit saṃkrāntipakṣe ṣoḍaśavāro bhavatīti dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ makarasamaṃ lagnavaśāditi
evaṃ māghaphālgunya- bhisandhau kumbhagataravau tatra vāre saṃskāraḥ, dvitīye vijñānam, tṛtīye nāmarūpam, caturthe ṣaḍāyatanam, pañcame sparśa, ṣaṣṭhe vedanā, saptame tṛṣṇā, aṣṭame upā- dānam, navame bhavaḥ, daśame jātiḥ, ekādaśame jarāmaraṇam, dvādaśame'vidyā iti vāma- nāḍī kumbhaviṣayalagnavaśena sṛṣṭikrameṇa
atra yathā tṛtīye tathā trayodaśame nāmarūpam, pañcadaśame sparśaḥ, kvacit ṣoḍaśame vedaneti
evaṃ sūryacandravāraḥ sarvatrasamaviṣamabhedena jñeya ubhayapakṣe saṃkrāntau vārabhedeneti
vimalaprabhāryā [lokadhātu- atrāvidyāṃśaṃ viṣamaṃ sarvasaumyakāryeṣu śobhanaṃ avidyā, vijñānam, ṣaḍāyatanam, vedanopādānajātiriti viṣamāṃśaṃ saṃskārādyaṃ samaṃ yat saumyakārye'śubhaṃ saṃskāro nāmarūpa- sparśa - tṛṣṇā-bhava jarāmaraṇamiti samaṃ viṣamaṃ krūrakarmaṇi praśasyate
evaṃ śuklapakṣe puṣye māse pratipadi avidyā dvitīyādyāṃ (yāṃ) saṃskāraḥ ṣaṣṭi- (sṛṣṭi) 'bhedena kṛṣṇapadi [ prati ] padi avidyā, dvitīyāyāṃ jarāmaraṇamityupasaṃhārakrameṇa (dvāda) śa[ ti [ thi] ṣu dvādaśāṅgāni yathā puṣye māse śuklakṛṣṇapakṣabhedenā- vidyātya (dya ) ṅgāni tathā māghe śuklakṛṣṇapakṣabhedena saṃskārādīni sṛṣṭisaṃhārabhedenāva- gantīti (ntavyānīti), evaṃ phālgunādike śuklakṛṣṇapakṣeṣu vijñāne yāni (vijñānādīni ) pratyekaṃ dvādaśāṅgāni bhavanti
atraiva yathā tyai ( laukika vyavahāro jyotiṣajñāne'bhijñārahito jñeya iti pratītyasamutpādaniyamaḥ tena vistaro nokta iti
idānīṃ bhūmibalamucyate- sūryārau ketumandau ripunidhanakarau saṃsthitaḥ savyapṛṣṭhe vāme'gre saumyarūpaṃ grahagaṇasakalaṃ yuddhabhūmyāṃ tathaiva
yoginyo viṣṭirudrāśca pavanasahitāḥ saukhyadāḥ savyapṛṣṭhe vāme'gre mṛtyurūpā samaviṣamagatā dve bale ghātayanti || 119 ||
sūryetyādi
atra bhūmivala (ya) maṣṭadiśātmakaṃ jñātvā rāśicakre grahana (kṣa) tra (cakra) bhūmyāmaṣṭadiśāsu yatra sūryārau ketumandau ripunidhanakarau saṃsthitāḥ sarve savye dakṣiṇapṛṣṭhe vā śatrūṇāmabhimukho vāmataḥ sthitā maraṇaṃ kurvantīti
vāme'gre saumyarūpaṃ grahagaṇasakalaṃ yuddhabhūmyāṃ tathaiva ripunidhanakaraṃ (:) candro budhaḥ śukro bṛhaspati- riti saumyarūpaṃ grahagaṇaṃ śatrūṇāṃ dakṣiṇe pṛṣṭhataḥ sthitaṃ ripunidhanakara iti niyamaḥ
evaṃ vakṣa (kṣya) māṇe yo [ gha] [ ga] 'ginyo viṣṭirudrāśca pavanasahitāḥ saukhyadāḥ savyapṛṣṭhe ripūṇāṃ vāme'gre sthitā nidhanakarā bhavantīti
samaviṣamagatā arddhakūrā ripūṇāṃ savye pṛṣṭhe arddhakurā na bhavanti; arddhasaumyā vāme'gre bhavanti, arddha- saumyā na bhavanti; tadā dve bale' ghātayanti, ubhayavalānāṃ maraṇaṃ bhavatīti niyamaḥ
paṭale ] svarodayayantravidhiniyama mahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ brahmāṇyādiyoginībalamucyate- brāhmī raudrī kumārī khagapatigamanā śūkarī vajrahastā cāmuṇḍā caiva lakṣmīrbhavati vasutithāvāditithyāṃ navamyām
śakre yakṣe'gnikoṇe danuyamavaruṇe mārute ceśabhūmau kurvantyatrodayaṃ tāḥ punarapi tithiṣu dvayaṣṭabhedaivibhinnāḥ ||120||
brāhmītyādi
atra śuklapakṣe kṛṣṇapakṣe vā pratipadi brahmāṇī śakre, dvitīyāyāṃ pa (ya) kṣe' raudrī, tṛtīyāyāmagnikoṇe kaumārī, cartuthyāṃ naiṛtyai vaiṣṇavī, pañcamyāṃ yāmyāṃ vārāhī, ṣaṣṭhyāṃ vāruṇe (jye) aindrī, saptamyāṃ vāyavyau (vye) cāmuṇḍā, aṣṭamyām aiśānye mahālakṣmīriti vasutithau aṣṭatithī, ādi pratipadādau navamyāmiti
punarnavamyāṃ brāhmī śaka e, daśabhyāṃ raudrī pa (ya) kṣe, ekādaśyāṃ kaumāryagnau dvādaśyāṃ vaiṣṇavī naiṛtye, trayodaśyāṃ vārāhī yāmye, catuddaśyāṃ aindrī vāruṇye, pañcadaśyāṃ pūrṇimāyāmamāvasyāyāñca arddhatithau cāmuṇḍā arddhatithau mahālakṣmīriti nyāyaḥ
evamāsvaṣṭadiśāsūdayaṃ kurvanti, tāḥ yoginyaḥ
punarapi pratipadādi ' tithiṣu pūrvārddha aṣṭabhedāḥ, aparārddha aṣṭabhedāḥ, evaṃ dvayaṣṭabhedeṣūdayanti tāsu tithisu jñeyāḥ, tāsāṃ svasvatithau prathamāṣṭamāṃśo bhogaḥ ' tithyā- dhipatyāḥ, paścāt yathoktakrameṇa saptabhogāḥ saptānāmiti nyāyaḥ
evamaparāddhe'pi tithau jñeyamiti yoginīcakrodayaniyamaḥ
viṣṭīnāṃ śuklapakṣe'pyudaya iha bhavet śakrayāmyābdhikoṇe kṛṣṇe pakṣe ca bhūyaḥ śikhidanupavanaḥ ceśakoṇe krameṇa

vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- dvau dvau garbhāntarāle skhalitamapi bhujairmudritaṃ vā samantāt pṛṣṭhe ṣaṭkāṇḍadhārā ubhayabhujasamā cāgrato yantravāhāḥ
kūpastambhainibaddheścaladanilapaṭairacālitaṃ cānilena tatrārūḍhaṃ svasainyaṃ vrajati jalanidhī toyadurgakṣayārtham || 135 ||
atra bāhye caturbhujāsu pratyekagarbhe pūrvāpare bhuje pada puṭa dvaye kāye vāmadakṣiṇe [ ṣaṇṇāṃ madhye ] āsu catasṛsu bhujāsu stambhakāṣṭheṣu cchidrabhujācchidrebhyo nirgateṣu bhujā- vistāratribhāgikeṣu hastatrayaphalakena ubhayakoṭicchidriteṣu na sarvagarbhān anyo'nyaṃ niyantrayet garbhakoṇacatuṣṭaye
śeṣaprakāśasthāneṣu kītivālān vāhyediti, ūrdhve mudritaṃ vā pūrvāparaṃ samantāt kītivālapravāhasthānāni varjayitvā
sarvayantrasya pṛṣṭha ṣaṭkāṇḍadhārāḥ ; evaṃ dvādaśa vi ( tri) śatiḥ śataparyantāḥ, ubhayabhujā samāścāgratāḥ ; teṣāṃ kāṇḍadhārāṇāṃ yantravāhāḥ sahasraparyantā bhavanti yantrānurūpata iti
atha vā tān akulaṃ jñātvā kūpastambhainibaddha ścaladanilapaṭervāta paṭaiścālayantaṃ vātena tad yantram, tatrārūḍhaṃ svasainyaṃ vrajati jalanidhau toyadurgakṣayārtham, dvīpāntaragrahaṇārthamiti nyāyo duṣṭānāṃ damanārthamiti jalayantralakṣaṇam
idānīṃ giridurgakṣayārthamagnitailamokṣaṇāya vātayantramucyate- eke pīṭhesbdhiko caladanilapaṭa : sadhvajastambhabandhaḥ pakṣe pṛṣṭhe manuṣyai skhalitamapi mahau rajjunā mūrdhni gacchat
vātenoddhūyamāno vrajati nabhasi vai śailadurgasya mūrdhni tasmāt muktāgnitela dahati ripubalaṃ sarvadurgaṃ samantāt || 136 ||
eka ityādi
atra caturhaste caturasre phalake pīṭhe madhye trihastastambho vistāra- vṛttena dvādaśāṅgulaḥ vāmadakṣiṇapṛṣṭho'grakoṇeṣu lohakīlaṃ sakaṭakam ; teṣu kaṭakeṣu rajjubhirmūni stambhaṃ niyantrayet
yantrabhāgadvayaṃ urdhvādhaḥ paryantaṃ dṛḍhavastre rācchādayet
atra koṇe dhvajāḥ pañcahastame kavistāraḥ, adhovāmadakṣiṇakoṇa* kaṭake rajjutrayaṃ hastadvayaṃ yāvat; tatra ekaṃ samāhāreṇa giridurgādūrdhvamānatulyam, tena rajjunā pṛṣṭhe manuṣyaiḥ skhalitaṃ mahau rajjunā mūrdhni gacchatā t tad yantraṃ vātenodbhūyamānaṃ vrajati nabhasi vai śailadurgaṃkṣayārtham
tasya mūrdhni tatra yantre'gnitailaṃ puruṣamekaṃ agnisahitaṃ praveśayet, tena nareṇa tasmād vātayantrāt muktāgnitelaṃ dahati ripubalaṃ sarvadurgaṃ samantāditi niścitaṃ giridurgabhaṅgani yamaḥ

paṭale ] svarodayayantra vidhiniyama mahoddeśaḥ cakraṃ sārddhadvihastaṃ bhavati ca kaṇayaścoddhvato'nyassayaṣṭiḥ yaṣṭayagre tīkṣṇakhaḍgaṃ bhramati laghutayā bhrāmite yantramūle || 137 ||
katūrī yantra pratyakṣadarśanenāvagantavyam, vṛttenāpi sphuṭamuktamiti
rajjukyā vātayantraṃ vrajati hi gagane svapradeśāt krameṇa durgāveṃ śatrudūtastatigatiniyatāṃ rajjukīṃ mānayitvā
bhūmau mānaṃ tathā vai kathitamapi bhavet śṛṅgabhede ripūṇāṃ śṛṅgaṃ dagdhvā samantād ripubhavanatale maṇḍale'gni pradadyāt || 138 ||
evaṃ śṛṅgabhedo'pi vātāyantramapi prakaṭitaṃ sphuṭaṃ vṛttenāpi nāstīti
cakrāṇāṃ mūrdhni bhāge racitamapi mahāmandiraṃ lauhakāṣṭha- vṛttākāraṃ samantāt pihitamapi phalaiścabhirmāhiṣairvā
mānuṣyaiścālyamānaṃ vrajati samamahau khānikāṃ yāvadetat tat khāni pūrayitvā praviśati sahasā koṭṭabhittau pratolyām || 139 ||
tathā vajrapañjare'pi vivakṣā nāstīti
idānīṃ saṃgrāme saguḍa gajabhedāya nārācayantramucyate- pīṭhe kīladvaye vai saguṇamapi dhanurniścalaṃ yantrayitvā pṛṣṭhe dvau lauhakīlau suracitakaṇaye'pyaṅgulokārddhacakrāḥ
aṅgulya guṇena pracurasamaśarā tīkṣṇanārācakānāṃ muktāḥ saṃgrāmakāle saguḍagajatanurbhedayitvā prayānti || 140 ||
ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ dīrghato nārācayā naṃ siṃhaṃ kṛtvā tasyādha urdhva savyavāmabhāge caturasraṣaḍaṅgulalauhadaṇḍātre mudritacchidre lauhakīlakaiḥ dvisandhau madhyamāyāṃ saguṇamapi dhanuniścalaṃ yantrayitvā punastasya pṛṣṭhabhāge savyetare caturasrau dvau lauhakīlakau lauhadaṇḍāgre kīlacchidra kaṇayāgradvayaṃ praveśayet
kaṇayamadhye'ṅa gulikāḥ ṣaṭ pañca catuḥ - triḥ - dve vā
adho lauhaikadaṇḍikā kaniṣṭhāṅa gulipramāṇā dīrgheṇa tryaṅa gulā adhaḥ kīlakaṭake vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- sacale praveśayet
ūrdhve guṇān aṅa gulyagre niyantrayet
tatrāṅgulyagra pratyekasandhau nārācaguṇā:, tena pracuraguṇasamaśarā bāhyanārācatīkṣṇamuktāḥ saṃgrāmakāle saguḍagaja- tanuṃ bheda yitvā prayāntīti nārācayantraniyamaḥ
idānīṃ rājamandirarakṣaṇāya pātālakhaḍgayantramucyate dvau stambhāviti - dvau stambhau bhūmigarbhe dṛḍhamapi nihitau rajjubhirveṣṭyamānau rajjvormadhye'simuṣṭiviṃśati savalayāvattatānekavartte
tanmadhye khaḍgamuṣṭiḥ prabhavati niyatā cālitā kīlitā ca mudrāvarttena sārdhaṃ pihitamasivaraṃ stambhitaṃ cāgrabhāge || 141 ||
atra dvau stambhau caturasrau dvādaśaṅa gulamānau, vistareṇa caturhastau hastadvayaṃ khānikā'dhobhūminihitau, hastadvayaṃ khānikāmadhyocchrito
tatra khānika- sthānādūrdhvaṃ sārddhahastān tyaktvā adho-vāma-dakṣiṇa-stambhacchidra argalān praveśayet, ūrdhve ṣaḍaṅa gulaṃ tyaktvā rajjubhirveṣṭayet calanārthaṃ valayasya
tayo rajjvormadhye aśi- muṣṭi: valayamadhye praviṣṭā
prathamaṃ valayamanekāvartairvaṃttitaṃ valayamadhye asimuṣṭi: praviṣṭā
kīlitācārya bhāge, tathā cāgrataḥ stambhadvaye vilomenāvattitā khaḍgāgragata- mudrayāgrabhāge stambhitam, khaḍgāgramudrāgre rajjunā stambhitaḥ khaḍgamuṣṭyagra kaṇayabhāge ṣaḍaṅa- gulamānaṃ kaṇayam
tasyordhva chidrarekhā bhavati ca phalake nirgamaścālitasya khaḍgodrdhva deya ekastvayasakṛtalaghuścandramā madhyabhāge
pādo yasyendumūrdhni prapatati sahasā cchidyate so'sinā ca koṭṭe dvārāvasāne narapatibhavane yantrameva prahāri || 142 ||
tataḥ khāniphalakairūd rdhvaṃ cādvayet khaḍganirgamaṃ yena cālitasya bhavati ayasalaghukṛtam
candrameti guhyabhāṣā, madhyeti prapañcaḥ
atra khaḍgamuṣṭyagre valayopariphalakaṃ deyam; tasmin phalake yasya pādaḥ patati cchidyate so'sineti svarūpaṃ jñātavyam; khaḍgayantraniyama iti khaḍgayantralakṣaṇam
evamanekayantrāṇi koṭṭa- dvāra vināśe saṃgrāmakāle narapatibhavane sadā mūladvāraṃ mārgaṃ varjayitvā yantrameva prahārīti pātālakhaḍgayantraniyamaḥ
idānīṃ kṣatrayuddhe syandanalakṣaṇamucyate- akṣoddhvaṃ mūlapīṭhaṃ bhavati ravibhujaiścakramānaṃ navāṃśa koṇe stambhārgalābhirviṣamasamaratho sārddhamānena pīṭhāt
lohe kāraṇa svarodaya yantravidhiniyamamahoddeśa : bhāgāddhe yodhabhūmiviṣamasamadhurā vājināṃ vā gajānām tasmāccihnārddhabhūmāvupari viracitādarśahārā dhvajādyāḥ || 143 ||
akṣoddhvaṃ ityādi
atra rathārthaṃ prathamamakṣo dṛḍhaṃ kāryam, aśvānāṃ rathasya cakramānaṃ sārddhaṃ hastadvayam akṣaścaturaṅgulacikit sādhikaḥ, tenaiva mānena catu vṛttam; tato'kṣa ūrdhve pīṭham, ajha dikṣu madhye cakramānaṃ navāṃśaṃ navakoṣṭhātmakamiti; tadeva ravibha jairdvādaśabhujaiḥ ṣaḍaṅgulavistāraiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ pūrvāparaiḥ, ṣaḍbhirdakṣiṇottaraiḥ pīṭhaṃ bhavati
akṣaścaturdaśāṅgulavistāraḥ; sa tribhāgaṃ kṛtvā, madhyabhāgaṃ parityajya dvayordvayoḥ sthāne cchidrayet; tayoḥ cchidrayoścakrāraṃ praveśyākṣeṇa sārddhaṃ kīlayet; cakranesyāṃ lauhakaraṇayaṃ niścalamādhāracchi- dravalaye praviṣṭaṃ sacakraṃ bhavati
tatastasya pīṭhasya catuḥ koṇeṣu catuḥstambhāḥ pīṭhabhujavistārā dīrghatvena dviguṇāḥ
te cārgalābhiścaturdikṣubhinnā adha ūrdhvabhinnā samarathaḥ samāśveścālitaḥ viṣamaratho viṣamāśveścālitaḥ, dvau catuḥ ṣaṭ samāśvāḥ, tripañca sapta-viṣamāśvā iti viṣamarathaḥ sārddhaṃ mānena pīṭhāditi ūrdhvabhāgā [] yodhabhūmiriti ūrdhve stambhānāṃ bhāgamekaṃ pīṭhapramāṇaṃ tyaktvā, arddhabhāge pīṭhopari yodha- bhūmiḥ pīṭhādarddhamānā bhavati, viṣamasamadhurā vājināṃ vai gajānāmu- cchrayeṇa bhavati, vistāre'ṣṭāṅgulā, dīrghatvena pīṭhād dviguṇā, argalābhiḥ sārddhaṃ kīlitā; tataḥ pīṭhabhāgādha ūrdhvaṃ cihnāddha bhabhirbhavati
caturbhiraṅgulo pari, tasyāṃ bhūmyāṃ viracitā ādarśahārā dhvajādyāḥ
ādiśabdena cāmaravitānaghaṇṭādaya iti yodhabhūmicihna ṃ bhūmyormadhye caturdikṣu prakāśo yuddhārthamiti
sārddhakadvitri bhāgāḥ śaśikaraśikhino mūlapīṭhārgalānāṃ vṛttā sārddhaṃ kabhāgaiḥ kamalarathamahī nandibhedaiḥ samā syāt
sādhāraṃ cakramānaṃ turagagajavaśāt sārddha hastaṃ dvidhā ca devānāṃ padmajātiḥ surasutavihito nandijātirnarāṇām || 144||
evaṃ dvau dvau vibhāgā iti mūlapīṭhasyādha eko vibhāgo yodhabhūmyām, ardhavirāgaḥ prakāśe cihnāvetau dvau vibhāgāviti
śaśikaraśikhina iti stambhā rgalānāṃ mūlapīṭhopari caturaṅa gulānām eko vibhāgo dhurāsthāne; tasmāt stambhānāṃ dvau vibhāgau caturaṅa gulānāṃ yodhā yuddhasthāne; tatsthānāṃ tribhāgā upari caturaṅgulānām, tadupari cihnabhūmirdhvajādīnāmiti
vṛttā sārddhakabhāgaiḥ pīṭhā- kaścit sādhikaḥ)

vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- u paripūrvoktāḥ sārddhabhāge bhūmivṛttāḥ kamalarathasya bhavati; nandibhedairiti nandirathabhede caturasrā mahī bhavati
evamuktakrameṇa sādhāraṃ cakramānaṃ turagarathasya sārddhahastaṃ bhavati, turagarathasya mānāt dvidhāmānaṃ gajarathasya bhavati, cakrapīṭhārgala- cihnādīnāmiti niyamaḥ
atra devānāṃ padmajātirvṛttaratho bhavati
surasutavihita iti arjunasya vitānaṃ nandijātiścaturasraḥ, narāṇāmanyathāpi caturasro ratho vihitaḥ kṣatriyāṇāmiti
aṣṭāmbhodhyakṣa cakre strividha iha mahāsyandano'rdhaḥ samaśca divyo'rkasyaikacakro viṣamahaya rathaścāsurāṇāṃ vināśaḥ
yuddha vevattikaḥ syād viṣamasamamahāsyandanaḥ kṣatriyāṇāṃ bhagno sammukho vai samaviṣamaraṇe pṛṣṭhabhaṅgī samaḥ syāt || 145 ||
aṣṭāmbhodhyakṣacakrestrividha iha mahāsyandano'rdhaḥ samaśceti
ihaśca - turakṣairaṣṭacakrarmahāratho avaivarttī saṃgrāme bhavati;
adha ūrdhve akṣābhyāṃ catuścakre : arddharatho bhavatīti
* [ tasmāt aṣṭa iti cakram; ambhodhiriti caturrakṣā:; evaṃ trividha iti catuścakram, tathā dvi, tathā akṣāḥ | syandanasyandanārdhasamaḥ tulyaḥ | divyo'rkarathaika- cakra iti ekena cakreṇa divyaratho bhavati, tathā arke bhavati | viṣama ityādi sugamaḥ |]**

idānīṃ devānāṃ vimāne vā nṛpāṇāṃ yātrācalagṛhalakṣaṇamucyate- ṣaṭpañcābdhyagnibhāgaiḥ prabhavati ca samā cchidritā stambhakoṭi- lādūrdhvāddha bhāgo bhavati puravaśāccāmarāṇāṃ vimāne

; eṣāṃ catuḥ koṭicchidritāḥ samā bhavanti - ekā cchidrakoṭiḥ ṣaṭ hastavibhāge dvitīyā pañcahasta- vibhāge; tatra tṛtīyā caturhastavibhāge bhavati, [caturthī' trihastavibhāge ], adha ūrdhve hastaikaikaṃ varjayitvā cchidrakoṭirabhimukhā bhavati
mūla bhāse bhūmyāṃ trayodaśa- hasto bhavati mūla bhāsā dūdha ardhabhāgo bhūmyāṃ saptārddhahasto bhavati; pratyeka- puravaśāt purabhūmyāṃ bhavati, catuśca rā lopari bhūmirbhavati gaṇavimāne
evaṃ bhūmau nṛpāṇāṃ trividhapuravaśād deśayātroccha veṣu prathamapure stambhabāhyacchidre bāhya- nirgatārgalopari phalakaiḥ sarvatra bhrāmarī trīṇi hastā bhrāmarī dvitīyapure ekahastād - bhrāmarī sarvadikṣi ti devavimānaprasthāna gṛhalakṣaṇanigamaḥ
idānīṃ ca vasantotsavāya cakradolālakṣaṇamucyate- cakro stambhamūni trividhapuravaśād dolakāḥ sarvadikṣu dvaṣṭāvaṣṭī tato'rddhādigatuyugabhujādhāra evaṃ tribhūmau
ūrdhve'dho bhrāmyamāne bhujadhṛtakaṇaye yoṣitāmāsanāni antarbāhya niviṣṭānyadha uparigatānyaṣṭadikcālitāni || 147 ||
cakrordhvaṃ ityādi
atra pūrvo ratha ca daṣṭacakrāṇi mūlapīṭhañca tadupari hastirathastambhāyātmana ścatvāraḥ stambhāḥ, vimānaca trividhapurātmakā dolā bhavanti, catuścaturgalopari dvayaṣṭāvaṣṭau tato dvāviṃśati prathamapure caturdikṣu ṣoḍaśa da dolakā bhavanti, dvitīye aṣṭau tṛtīye catvāraḥ
dikūdaśabhujairargalo- pari sthitaiḥ savyāva pavasavyaiḥ pūrvāparasthai: dolakā prathamapure; dvitīye ṛtubhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ bhavati, tṛtīye catubhiriti dolakānāṃ bhujā ārā stribhamyāmiti dvayordvayobhujayorekaikakaṇayo cchidrādhāre praviṣṭā
ekaikakaṇaye catuścaturāsanāni yoṣitāṃ bhavanti, antarbāhya niviṣṭāni adha uparigatānyaṣṭadikcālitānīti cakradolālakṣaṇa- niyamaḥ
idānīṃ vāṭikādisiñcanārthaṃ jalayantralakṣaṇamucyate ūrdhvaṃ ityādi- ūrdhvādho vakramānaṃ bhavati ca nalikākārayantraṃ samantāt nālāgre kumbhamukhaṃ śu ṣiramapi yadā nāḍirandhrapramāṇam
toyākṛṣṭi karoti skhalitamapi jalaṃ mokṣitaṃ kumbharandhrā udyāne vāṭikāyāṃ vrajati samamahau nīyate yatra tatra || 148 ||

Phyed de Ses pa ni 295 19 25 152 vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- ūrdhva'dho vāmadakṣiṇe yantraṃ ca kranālākārau, madhye sacchidraṃ madhyaṃ caturhastaṃ vā mūlayantram, tasya nālāgre kumbhamukhaṃ grīvā nālāgre praveśa mānā adhaḥ kumbhe cchidraṃ nālirandhrapramāṇaṃ yantrapṛṣṭhanālāgre aparanalikā jalamadhye praviṣṭā karttavyā; tataḥ kumbhacchidraṃ jalagati nālikācchidram agrato vā nālikācchidraṃ mudrayet; tato mūla- yantracchidreṇa jalaṃ praveśayet yāvajjalapūrṇaṃ yantraṃ bhavati
tato [ b] jalamadhyabindu- (cchidraṃ) kumbhacchidraṃ vā nālikācchidraṃ yaugapadyena kumbhaṃ toyākṛṣṭa śarataḥ karoti, tadeva jalamudyāne vāṭikāyāṃ vrajati samabhūmyāṃ noyate yatra tatra vrajatīti jalaśe kayantra- lakṣaṇaniyamaḥ

tanmadhye kiñcidatra sphuṭamiha viṣaye deśitaṃ te mayādya svasthāne rakṣaṇārthaṃ kuru sakalamidaṃ dveṣalobhairna sūryaḥ || 149 ||
iha duṣṭānāṃ sādhanārthaṃ pūrvoktamādibuddhe yat tribhuvanaguruṇā śākyasiṃhena sucandrasya vajrapāṇinirmitakāyasya sarvasvarodayayantralakṣaṇam, tanmadhye, tasya svarodaya- yantrādhyāyasya madhye, kiñcidatra sphuṭamiha viṣaye sambhalākhye deśitam, te sūryarathasya mayā mañjuśriyā yaśonarendreṇa, adya svasthāne rakṣaṇārthaṃ kuru sakalamidaṃ dveṣalobhairna sūrya iti sūryarathasya niyamo yathā tathānyeṣāmapi kālacakraparijñānāni nāma yamapi niyamo bhagavataḥ

kaumāre yauvane'nyā prabhavati vipulaikā ca samyak tathaikā
vṛddhatve'pyekaśāntā bhavana (ti) nidhanatā cāntimā garbhajānām
matsyākārastu mātsye bhavati katidinaṃ kūrmabhāvaśca kaumyeṃ vārāhye tasya sūryāyatanamapi bhavennārasiṃhe prasūtiḥ ||7||
iha guhyābje raktamadhye patitamapi yadā bodhibījamiti śukraṃ sajīvamālaya- vijñānasahitaṃ guhyābje patitaṃ yadā tadā daśāvasthālakṣaṇaṃ bhavati
teṣu garbhamadhye matsya kūrmava rāhāvasthāstisro bhavanti; tathaikā prasavanakāle narasiṃhāvasthā; bālakāle- 'pi ca dve, dantotthānād vāmanāvasthā, dantotthānād dantapātaṃ yāvat paraśurāmāvasthā
kaumāre yauvane'nyā iti dantapātāt ṣoḍaśavarṣāvadheḥ prathamavipulā rāmāvasthā, tathā aparā vipulā ṣoḍaśavarṣāt palitotpatti yāvat kṛṣṇāvasthā, palitotpatteḥ maraṇa- divasaṃ yāvad buddhāvasthā, maraṇadine sarvabhūtasyai kalolībhūtatvāccharīre kalkyavasthā bhavati
vṛddhatve'pyekaśāntā bhavana (ti) nidhanatā cāntimā garbhajānāmiti daśāvasthā- niyamaḥ
adhyātmani cittavajradhara viṣṇorda śāvasthākṛtyamucyate matsyetyādinā - iha mātsye bhāve matsyākāro bhavati bodhicittavajro ra viṣṇuḥ katidinaṃ ṛtu- dinaṃ yāvat; tataḥ kau bhāve kūrmabhāvo bhavati cakārādaparaṛtudinaṃ yāvat; vārāhye bhāve bodhicittaviṣṇoḥ sūryāyatanaṃ dvādaśāyatanodbhavo bhavati, tṛtīyaṛtudinaṃ yāvat; evaṃ saptamāsājjātadinaṃ yāvat nārasiṃhe bhāve prasūtirbhavati, karābhyāṃ yoni- vidāraṇāt
vāmanye bālabhāvo dvinṛpatidaśanānyudbhavaṃ yānti rāmye bhūyo rāmye patanti sphuṭamapi kapaṭaṃ kārayet saiva kārye
bauddhe śānti karoti vratamapi niyamaṃ yāti mṛtyuṃ ca kālkye śukre majjāsthināḍī bhavati rajasi vai carmaraktaṃ ca māṃsam ||8||
paṭale ] kāyavākcittotpatti caturārya satya nirṇaya-mahoddeśaḥ raktaṃ bījaṃ prarohatyamṛtarasagataṃ garbhamadhyekamāse paścād bījāṅkurasya suhṛdi daśavidhā nāḍikātyantasūkṣmā
nābhau cāṣṭāṣṭakānyāḥ karacaraṇamukheṣūrdhvataḥ sarpyamāṇā māse pūrṇe dvitīye karacaraṇamukhoddeśanaṃ kiñcidatra || 9 ||
vāmanye bhāve bālabhāvo bhavati pūrvoktaḥ, dvinṛpati dvātriddaśanānyudbhavaṃ yānti rāmye, paraśurāmye bhāve' patanti'
sphuṭamapi kapaṭaṃ kārayediti sa eva bodhicitta - viṣṇuḥ kārye bhāve māyāṃ kārayet, bauddha bhāve śānti karoti
sattvānāṃ vratamapi niyamaṃ yāti mṛtyuṃ ca kālkye
kālkye bhāve pūrvoktavidhinā maraṇaṃ yātīti daśāva- tāraḥ
iha vākye yanmatsyādikaṃ purāṇaṃ racitaṃ duṣṭabrahmaṛṣibhistad bālānāṃ vañcanāya narakāvāptihetukamiti vakṣyamāṇāyāṃ paramākṣarajñānasiddhau vistareṇa vaktavyam
mātṛgarbhasthayoḥ śukrarajasovikārāḥ garbhādhānamārabhya prasūtiparyaṃntā ucyante śukra ityādinā- iha śarīre ṣaṭ koṣā jātakasya bhavanti - śukre majjā bhavati, asthīni bhavanti, nāṭyaśca snāyavo bhavanti, rajasi camaṃraktaṃ ca bhavati, māṃsaṃ ca bhavati, ekasmād raktād raktabījaprarohanā ( ṇā) diti
atra garbhe māsamekaṃ yāvad raktaṃ bījaṃ prarohati pañcāmṛtarasagatam, paścād bojāṅkurasyeti, tato māsādūrdhvaṃ bodhicittasyāṅkurībhūtasya hṛdayamadhyasthāne daśavidhā nāḍyaḥ prāṇādivāyvādhārā bhavanti; tāścātyantasūkṣmā bālapramāṇā; nābhau nābhi- pradeśe'ṣṭāṣṭākāścatuḥṣaṣṭidaṇḍavāhinyo bhavanti; atisūkṣmā anuktā'pi dvādaśarāśi- saṃkrāntivaśāccatuḥṣaṣṭīnāmādhārabhūtā dvādaśa tā: nāḍyo bhavanti; tathātyanta - sūkṣmāstāḥ sarvāḥ pūrvoktā nābhināḍhyaḥ karacaraṇamukhasthānakaraṇāya ūrdhvataḥ sarpyamāṇāḥ karacaraṇamukhasthāneṣu tataḥ sarpyamāṇaprabhāvāt
dvitīye māse pūrṇe sati mukhakaracaraṇoddeśanaṃ kiñcidatra iha prāṅ matsyāvasthādine gate sa ti kūrmāvasthāyāṃ mukhakaracaraṇa'- sthāneṣu pañca sphoṭakā bhavanti
hastau pādau ca kaṇṭho bhavati samaśiraḥ pūrṇamāse tṛtīye sūkṣmā māse caturthe karacaraṇamukhe nāḍikā kaṇṭhadeśe
hastau pādau ca kaṇṭho bhavati samaśiraḥ kūrmaḥ, aparā sūkṣmanāḍyaḥ karacaraṇamukhe kaṇṭhadeśe karacaraṇānāṃ ṣaṭsandhiṣu mukhakaṇṭhe sahajasambhoganāḍyo'tisūkṣmāścaturthe māse pūrṇe sati bhavantīti kūrmāvasthā
pañcame māse vārāhye bhāve māṃsānyāśritya cāsthīni kharasahutabhu giti ṣaṭyuttaratriśatānyasthīni sandhayaśca tāvat yaḥ ṣaṣṭhe māse ca cakārāt vārāhye bhāve māṃsaṃ rudhiraṃ bhavati, vedanā bhavati, sukhaduḥkhaṃ ca vetti, abhijñā- balena pūrvanivāsānusmṛti ca, svacittena vairāgyaṃ karoti, bhavaduḥkhaṃ nindayati, buddhamārga praśaṃsayati
bhrūkeśā randhraromāṇyapi munivare nāḍikāḥ sāvaśeṣā māsādūrdhvaṃ hi jātā: pratidinasamaye śūnyaśūnyākṣisaṃkhyā
marmāṇyasthīni majjā bhavati ca rasanā mūtragūthe'ṣṭame ca randhrākhye ghoraduḥkhaṃ prasavanasamaye yoninā pīḍitasya || 11 ||
tato keśā romarandhrāṇi cakṣurādīni
api ca vārāhye bhāve munivare saptame māse pūrṇe sati sampūrṇānyāyatanāni bhavanti
iha garbhamāsādūrdhvaṃ pratidinasamaye śūnya- śūnyākṣisaṃkhyā dviśatasaṃkhyā: pratyahaṃ nāḍikā bhavanti
tataḥ ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatamarmāṇi asthīni, tanmadhye majjā bhavati rasanā jihvā jihva endriyotpādāya pūrvacakṣu- rādayo bhavanti kaṭhināḥ
tato rasanotpādakālādiha mūtragathe cāṣṭame māse bhavataḥ
vārāhye bhāve tato randhrākhye navame māse pūrṇe sati kvacid daśame ekādaśe dvādaśe vā vārāhyabhāvāvasāne ghoraduḥkhaṃ bhavati prasavanakāle yoninā pīḍitasya nārasiṃhe bhāve garbhajātakasyeti garbhotpattiniyamaḥ
caturāryasatyeṣu saṃsāriṇāṃ duḥkhasatyamāha garbha ityādi- garbhe garbhasthaduḥkhaṃ prasavanasamaye bālabhāve'pi duḥkhaṃ kaumāre yauvane śrā (strā) ' dhanavibhavahataṃ kleśaduḥkhaṃ mahad yat
vṛddhatve mṛtyuduḥkhaṃ punarapi bhayadaṃ ṣaḍgatau rivādyaṃ duḥkhād gṛhṇāti duḥkhaṃ sakalajagadidaṃ māhitaṃ māyayā ca ||12||
iha saṃsāriṇāṃ prathamaṃ tāvat garbhe sthitānāṃ garbhastha duḥkha kumbhīpākasadṛśaṃ bhavati prasavanasamaye mudgarayantra pīḍitavat, bālabhāve'pi viṣṭhābhakṣaṇa śūkaravat, kaumāre yauvane ca śrī 'viyogaduḥkhaṃ dhanavibhavahataṃ duḥkham, tadabhāvāt
vṛddhatve'pi jarāmaraṇa- du:kham, punarapi maraṇānte ṣaḍgatau bhayadaṃ rauravādyam
āditaḥ pretatiryaggatau raudraṃ duḥkhaṃ bhavati pāpavaśād daśā kuśala parityāgāt
evaṃ duḥkhād gṛhṇāti duḥkhaṃ sakala- vaṭale ] kāyavāścittotpatti-caturāryasatya nirṇaya-mahoddeśaḥ jagadidaṃ mohitaṃ māyayā ca
iha saṃsāre māyayā mohitamahaṅkāramamakārātmikayā naraka- pretatiryaggatau duḥkhaṃ gṛhṇātīti
mārgasatyamāha saṃsāra ityādi- saṃsāre mānuṣatvaṃ kvacidapi tu bhaved dharmabuddhiḥ kadācit tasmād buddhe'nurāgo bhavati śubhavaśādādiyāne pravṛttiḥ
tasmād śrīvajrayāne kvacidakhilamatirvarttate bhāvanāyāṃ tasyāṃ buddhatvamiṣṭaṃ paramasukhapade'ho praveśo'tikaṣṭa : ( kaṣṭam ) ||13||
[89b] iha ṣaṭ gatyātmake saṃsāre mānuṣatva kvacidapi tu bhavet, tato dharmabuddhiḥ kadācid bhavati narāṇāṃ tīrthikadānādidharmeṣu
tasmād buddhe'nurāgaḥ kadācit śubhavaśād bhavati, dānādyanurāgo'pi
tato'pi śubhavaśādādiyāne pravṛttirbhavati śrāvakapratyeka- buddhāne
tasmāt śrīvajrayāne pravṛttiḥ, vajramabhedyamacchedyaṃ mahaditi
tadeva yānaṃ mantra- nayaṃ pāramitānayaṃ phalahetvātmakam, ekalolībhūtam tasmin pravṛttirvajrayāne pravṛttiḥ
tataḥ kvacidakhilamatirvattate bhāvanāyām, kasyāñcit ' sālambanaśūnyatānirālambana- karuṇātmikāyāṃ puṇyajñānavaśena, tasyāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ buddhatvamiṣṭaṃ bhavati
evaṃ paramasukha- pade'ho praveśo'tikaṣṭaḥ (ṣṭam) dūrād dūrataraḥ saṃsāriṇāṃ saṃsārasukhābhilāṣiṇāmiti
samudayasatyamāha garbhaṃ ityādinā - garbhe saṃśuddhakāyaḥ prasavanasamayād dantabhāvo'rtha ( bhāve ca ) ra dharmaḥ
dantānāṃ vai prapātaḥ prabhavati nṛpa sambhogakāyo jinasya |
tasmānnirmāṇakāyaḥ prakaṭitaniyato mṛtyusīmno narāṇāṃ garbhe bāhaye caturdhā bhavati punaraso jñānavijñānabhedāt || 14 ||
iha prathamaṃ lokasaṃvṛtyā viśuddhakāyādikaṃ garbhajātasya śarīre saṃdṛśyate;
paścād tad vai dharmeṇa buddhasya bhagavataḥ suviśuddhakāyādikaṃ yogibhirveditavyam |
atra mātṛgarbhe yāvat tiṣṭhati tāvat pañcābhijño saṃśuddha kāyo bhavati śarīriṇaḥ |
tataḥ prasavanasamayamārabhya yāvad dantotthānaṃ tāvad dharmakāyo bhavati |
tato dantotthānād yāvad dantaprapāto bhavati tāvat sambhogakāyo jinasya bodhicittavajrasya |
nṛpetyāmantraṇam |
tasmād dantapātā- nirmāṇakāyaḥ prakaṭito niyato mṛtyusomno narāṇāṃ garbhajānāmiti |
garbhe bāhye caturdhā bhavati, punarasau kāyasamūhaḥ, iha jñānabhedāt garbhe ṣaḍvijñānabhedāt bāhye, jñānavijñāna- bhedāditi |
[90a] vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ adhyātma- vijñānaṃ candrasūryāvapi kamalagatāḥ śuddhakāyaḥ sa garbhe dhātuskandhodbhavo yo bhavati daśavidho dharmakāyo jinasya |
tasmāt sambhogakāyo bhavati guṇavaśācchotraśabdādijāte tasmāt keśādijāte prasavanasamayaścātra nirmāṇakāḥ || 15 ||
atra garbhe tāvad vijñānamālayavijñānaṃ sat saukhyaṃ jñānamiti |
candrasūryau śukra- rajasī api kamalagatā mātṛkamalagatāstrayaḥ ekībhūtā garbhaniṣpattaye;
śuddhakāyaḥ sa garbhe bhagavata ucyate, mātrāpitrā layavijñānaikasukhāvasthātaḥ |
dhātuskandhānāmudbhavo yo daśavidhaḥ, sa dharmakāyo jinasya bodhicittavajrasya |
ato'dhyātmaśrotrādīnāmi- ndriyāṇāṃ śabdādīnāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ jāte sati guṇavaśād divasavaśāt sambhogakāyo bhavati |
tasmāt keśādijāte āditaḥ lomaviṭmūtraniṣpattiḥ |
prasavanasamayo yoneru- tpādo nirmāṇakāya' ucyate garbhe |
jāte śvāsodbhavo yaḥ bhavati nirguṇaḥ śuddhakāyaḥ sa eva tasmāt dantodbhavo yo'sphuṭamapi vacanaṃ dharmakāyastathaiva |
tasmāt pāto dvijānāṃ prasavati nṛpa sambhogakāyo jinasya dantebhyaḥ mṛtyusīmne bhavati jinapaterbāhyanirmāṇakāya: || 16 ||
tato bāhye kāyacatuṣṭayaṃ jāte sati śvāsasya prathama udbhavo yo madhyanāḍyāṃ bhavati, sa nirguṇo vāgvajraviṣaye saṃśuddhakāyaḥ, sa eva sapādaṣaṭpañcāśat śvāsasamūhaṃ yāvat tāvat śuddhakāyo madhyamāpravāhataḥ |
tasmāt prāṇasañcāravaśād dantodbhavo yo'sphuṭamapi vacanaṃ yat tat saṃjñālakṣaṇam, sa vāgvajraviṣaye dharmakāyaḥ |
tasmāt pāto dvijānāṃ dantānāṃ pāto bhavati yāvat sambhogakāyo'ṣṭavarṣaparyantaṃ parisphuṭavacanāt |
dantebhyaḥ patitebhyo mṛtyuparyantaṃ nirmāṇakāyo bāhye bhavati |
iha garbhe kāya [90b]bhedena caturvidhaḥ kāyabhedaḥ |
bāhye prāṇa[saṃ] cārabhedena caturdhā kāyabhedo jñānaṃ vijñānaṃ ca dehe (antaḥ) bāhye'pi kāyaprāṇayoḥ sahāyaḥ |
atra kāyabhedaḥ śukrasambandhī, vāgbhedo raktasambandhī;
evaṃ vyāpyavyāpakasambandhena cittabhedena caturvidhaḥ kāyabhedaḥ |
jāgradādilakṣaṇena jñānabhede- nāpi caturvidhaḥ kāyabhedaḥ, ānandādinā caturvidhasvabhāveneti dehe bāhya sahajādikāyo - tpādaniyamaḥ |
kāyavākcittajñānotpattimāha garbhaṃ ityādinā - garbhe śrīkāyavajraṃ prathamamiha bhaved vāksvarūpaṃ prasūte cittaṃ dantodbhave vai punarapi patanādudbhave jñānavajram |
jñānaṃ vijñānamiśraṃ raviśaśisahitaṃ jñānavajrādisarvaṃ garbhe rūḍhaṃ krameṇa prabhavati balavat kāyavajrādinā ca || 17||
paṭale ] kāyavā cittotpatti- caturāryasatyanirṇaya-mahoddeśaḥ iha mātṛgarbhe jñānaṃ sahajānandaṃ vijñānamiśritamālayavijñānam, raviśaśisahitaṃ rajaḥ śukrābhyāṃ sahitaṃ jñānavajrādisarvaṃ sahajānandādikaṃ bhavati |
garbhe rūḍhaṃ krameṇa prabhavati balavat kāyavajādinā ca |
atra prathamaṃ' garbhe śrīkāyavajraṃ bhavati prasūtyavadhiṃ yāvat, kāyāvayavaniṣpattirityarthaḥ |
tataḥ prasūte vāgvajraṃ prāṇasaṃcāraḥ prāṇotpattiḥ |
tata- ścitta |
saṃjñāgrāhakaṃ dvātriṃśaddantobhave sati bhavati |
vai ityekāntaniścitamityarthaḥ |
punarapi dantapatanāt dvātriṃśaddantodbhave sati strīṇāṃ dvādaśavarṣāvadhi jñānavajra śukracyu- tyavasthālakṣaṇaṃ sahajānandalakṣaṇaṃ narāṇāṃ ṣoḍaśavarṣāvadherveditavyamiti kāyavākcitta- jñānaniṣpattikathanam |
ādhārādheyasambandhamāha pṛthvītyādi - pṛthvīgarbho hutāśo bhavati ca dharaṇīmārutau śūnyagarbho vāyorbhūmeśca garbho bhavati hi salilaṃ niḥsvabhāvaḥ svabhāvam |
bhūmerādhāra ambu jvalanamapi jalasyānilaḥ pāvakasya vāyoḥ śūnyaṃ narendra prabhavati hi tathādhāra ādheyabhūtaḥ || 18 ||
iha pṛthvyā garbho hutāśo'gnirbhavati, na tatkulī, pṛthvīguṇasvabhāvābhāvāt |
ākāśagarbhe' śunyamadhye tiṣṭhato dharaṇīmārutau na' tatkulajātau, ākāśaguṇābhāvāt |
evaṃ vāyuga bhūmeśca garbha salilaṃ bhavati;
tadeva vāyugarbhe niḥsvabhāvānurūpe- ṇāvasthitam ' ; yatra yatra vāyuḥ saṃsarati, tatra tatra tena sārddhaṃ vrajati |
yad bhūmimadhye vyava- sthitaṃ tat svabhāvaṃ dravarūpaṃ khanyamānaṃ dṛśyate bhūmyamiti |
atra punaḥ sarveṣāṃ pṛthvyādīnā- mākāśamādhāraḥ, pṛthivyādayo bhūtā ādheyāḥ |
atra yathākramamādhārādheyau bhavataḥ |
bhūme - rādhāramambu bhūmimaṇḍalasya bāhya yathā jalamaṇḍalamādharaḥ tathā śarīre'pi |
jvalanamapya- gnimaṇḍalaṃ jalasyeti jalamaṇḍalasyādhāraḥ |
anila iti vāyumaṇḍalam |
pāvakasyāgni- maṇḍalasya vāyuriti vāyumaṇḍalassya śūnyamākāśamādhāraḥ |
evaṃ sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ pṛthivyā- dīnāṃ śūnyamādhāro bhavati |
|
samūhādhārādheyamāha skandha ityādi- skandhādhāro hi bhauto bhavati varatanau bhūtavṛndasya nāḍyo nāḍīnāṃ prāṇavāyurbhavati daśavidhaścetanā prāṇavāyoḥ |
taccittaṃ dvisvabhāvaṃ bhavati guṇavaśāt sasvabhāvāsvabhāvaṃ bharttādhārastayośca tribhuvananamito'nāhataḥ sarvago yaḥ || 19 ||
iha śarīre pañcaskandhānāmādhāro hi bhautaṃ to pṛthivyādibhūtasamūhaṃ ho bhavati |
tatkasya hetoḥ ?
iha pṛthivyādiprakṛterabhāvāt pratyayo nāstyavidyādikaḥ |
avidyāsaṃskāravijñānādipratyayābhāve rūpādiskandhābhāvaḥ |
yataścatubhūtātmakaṃ rūpam, ṣaḍdhātvātmako mahāpuruṣapuṅga la:, ataḥ skandhādhāro hi bhautaṃ to bhavati |
varatanau bhūtavṛndasya nāḍyo dvāsaptatisahasrasaṃkhyā bhavantyādhārāstāsāṃ nāḍīnāmapi prāṇavāyurā- dhāraḥ, saṃskārakāraṇāt |
sa ca daśavidho vakṣyamāṇo vaktavyaḥ |
cetanā prāṇa- vāyorādhāraḥ;
sā cetanā tadeva dvisvabhāvaṃ cittaṃ bhavati, guṇavaśāditi sattvarajovaśāt sasvabhāvaṃ' jāgratsvapnalakṣaṇam, tamoguṇavasānniḥsvabhāvaṃ susu ptalakṣaṇaṃ nirindriyaṃ bhavati |
tayośca sasvabhāvāsvabhāvayorbharttādhāraḥ sukhaduḥkhāvasthādhāvī dhārī saṃsāri- ṇām |
sa ca sukhaduḥkhābhyāṃ rahitastribhuvananamito yaḥ so'nāhataḥ sarvago bhavati sukhaduḥkhāntakṛnniṣṭha iti |
gandhotpattirdharaṇyāmapi śikhini rasasyodake rūpadhātoḥ vāyo sparśasya śānte tvapi bhavati ravasyāmbare dharmadhātoḥ |
śūnyaṃ gṛhṇāti śabdaṃ khalu jinajanako dharmadhātuṃ ca vāyuḥ gandhaṃ vahniśca rūpaṃ rasamapi salilaṃ sparśameva dharitrī || 20 ||
dhātūnāṃ janyajanakasambandhamāha gandha ityādinā - iha bāhye vā'dhyātmani gandhotpattidhaṃraṇyāṃ bhavati mukhyataḥ rasādīnāṃ punargauṇata iti |
api sambhāvane, śikhinyagnidhātau rasasya ṣaṭprakārasyāgneḥ paripākahetuto rasasyotpattirbhavati, yatastasmādudake rūpadhātoḥ |
ihākurādīnāṃ prarohādikaṃ nānāvarṇaṃ nānāsaṃsthānamudakād bhavati mukhyataḥ, gauṇataḥ punaḥ rasādīnāmapyutpattiḥ |
evaṃ vāyau vāyudhātau sparśasyotpattirbhavati;
śānte jñānadhātau ravasyeti śabdotpādaḥ |
ambare pañcātmakarasadhātau śūtake dharmadhātorbodhicittadhātorutpattirbhavati sukhāvasthāyā iti |
idānīṃ grāhyagrāhakasambandha ucyate- iha parakulāliṅganataḥ kāryasiddhirbhavati, svakulāliṅganena svātmani kriyā-
virodhāt
ataḥ parakulāliṅganaṃ śarīrotpattikāraṇaṃ bhavati

khalu jinajanaka iti niścitaṃ jñāna- dhātudbhūtaṃ manaindriyaṃ gṛhṇāti, dharmadhātumākāśadhātū dbhūtaviṣayam
vāyuriti janya- janakābhedyatvena vāyubhūtaṃ ghrāṇendriyaṃ bhūmidhātubhūtaṃ gandhaviṣayaṃ gṛhṇāti
va hni- śceti agnidhātudbhūtaṃ cakṣurindriyaṃ gṛhṇāti toyadhātujanitaṃ rūpaviṣayam
vahnidhātu- janitaṃ rasaviṣayaṃ jaladhātudbhūtaṃ rasanendriyaṃ gṛhṇāti
dharitrī pṛthvīdhātudbhūtaṃ kāyendriyaṃ gṛhṇāti vāyudhātṛdbhūtaṃ sparśaviṣayamiti viṣayaviṣayiṇāṃ grāhyagrāhakalakṣaṇaniyamaḥ
lokasaṃvṛtyā rūpādīnāṃ skandhānāṃ pratyekadhātau siddhimāha siddhamityādi - siddhaṃ rūpaṃ dharaṇyāṃ tribhuvananilaye codake siddhasaṃjñā vahnau vai vedanā ca prabhavati pavane siddhasaṃskāra eva
vijñānaṃ jñānamiśra sagaganakuliśe siddhamevātra kāye dhātuskandhādisarvaṃ bhavati ca rajasā bodhicittādiyogāt // 21 //
iha dhātuskandhānāmaikyaṃ siddhamucyate
atra dharaṇyāṃ mukhyato rūpaṃ siddham, saṃjñādayo gauṇataḥ siddhāḥ
tribhuvananilaye traidhātukaviṣaye, na nirvāṇadharme bhavatīti
cakārādudake saṃjñā siddhā bhavati
iha saṃjñānāmasvaradharmacandraḥ, śukraṃ toyaprakṛtiḥ, toyaprakṛtitvāt saṃjñā siddhā bhavati, udaka iti niścayaḥ
vahnau vai vedanā ceti iha vahniprakṛtiḥ sūryo rajaḥ, kāli: sūryarajaḥ kālisvabhāvā vedanā
ato vahnau vedanā siddhā bhavati mukhyataḥ, śeṣā gauṇataḥ prabhavanti
pavane siddhaḥ saṃskāraskandhaḥ, evamatra vāyuḥ kāyavākcitta- saṃcālakaḥ saṃskārajanakaḥ, tena vāyuprakṛtiḥ saṃskāraḥ siddhaḥ
evaṃ vijñānaṃ jñānamiśraṃ jñānasahitam
yathākramaṃ sagaganakuliśe ākāśe vijñānaṃ siddhaṃ śrotravijñānādilakṣaṇam
kuliśe jñānadhāto jñānaṃ siddhaṃ bodhicittacyavanānte sukhavakṣaṇātmakam

cakṣustoyasvabhāvaṃ tribhuvananilaye vāyubhāvaśca kāyaḥ evaṃ śrotrādisarvaṃ bhavati guṇavaśāt jñānavijñānayogāt // 22 //

ṣaḍdhātuvaśena mātsaryādayaḥ (dīn ) ṣaṭ cittavikārānāha mātsaryamityādi - mātsaryaṃ jñānadhātau bhavati varatano cāmbare dveṣavatraṃ (cittam) īrṣyācitaṃ hi vāyau prakaṭitamanale rāgacittaṃ tathaiva
toye śrīmānavajraṃ (cittaṃ ) prakṛtiguṇavaśānmohavatraṃ (cittaṃ ) dharaṇyāṃ vāhastau pādapāyū marudanalajalakṣmāsu sarve babhūvuḥ // 23 //

iha vāgindriyaṃ vāyudhātorbhavati, pāṇondriyaṃ tejodhātoḥ pādendriyaṃ toyadhātoḥ, pṛthvīdhātoḥ pāyvindriyam, anuktatvāt, ākāśadhātoḥ guhyendriyam

rajaḥ śukravaśād dhātuvikāraḥ, samudayasatyaṃ kāya vidhai keneti bhagavatoktam |
iti śrīmūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ kāyavākcittotpattiduḥkha- māgaṃ samudaya nirodhasatya nirṇayamahoddeśaḥ prathamaḥ || 1
samudayasatyādimahoddeśaḥ idānīmādhārādheya samudāya ucyate guhyetyādinā - guhyoṣṇīṣe ca nābhau sahajajinatanuniḥ svabhāvasvabhāvā hṛccakre dharmakāyo bhavati hi nṛpa sambhogacakre jinasya |
bindau nirmāṇakāyo bhavati guṇavaśāccādhidaivakrameṇa hṛccakra kaṇṭhacakra śirasi ca kamalaṃ dharmasambhogaśuddham || 27
iha śarīre ṣaṭ cakrāṇyādhārabhūtāni catvāraḥ kāyā ādheyāḥ; teṣu sahajakāya- strikamaleṣu svabhāvāsvabhāvabhe dena |
ato guhyakamale uṣṇoṣakamale nābhikamale viśuddhakāyo'dhidevatā niḥsvabhāvā, akalpanāsvabhāvā, pratisenopamā |
hṛdaye dharmakāyaḥ, kaṇṭhābje sambhogakāyo'dhidevatā kāyabhedana |
bindāviti śiraso'bje śuddhacakre nirmāṇa- kāyo'dhidevatā |
kāyabhedena nābhau nirmāṇacakre nirmāṇakāyo'dhidevatā iti kāyabhede niyamaḥ |
guṇavaśāditi cakrāṇāṃ guṇaḥ; jñānākāśabhūmiguṇaḥ sahajakāyādhārāṇām; vāyu- guṇo dharmakāyādhārasya; tejoguṇaḥ sambhogakāyādhārasya; udakaguṇo nirmāṇakāyādhārasyeti ādhārādheyabhāvaḥ |
uktakrameṇa hṛccakra kaṇṭhacakra śirasi ca kamalaṃ dharmasambhogaśuddha miti |
nābhau kaṇṭhe ca guhya e śirasi ca hṛdaye tadvaduṣṇīṣamadhye māturbhartuḥ krameṇa trividhamapi bhavet kāyavākcittavajram |
cakra ṃ ratnaṃ khapadma ṃ jalajamasivaraṃ ṣaṭkulaṃ vajrayuktaṃ tānyūrdhvādhastrināḍyastrividhapathagatāścandrasūryāgnibhedaiḥ || 28
paṭale ] samudayasatyādimahoddeśaḥ kāyādhārabhedena ṣaṭkulānyucyante nābhāvityādinā - iha śarīre ṣaḍdhātuvaśena ṣaṭcakreṣu ṣaṭkulāni bhavanti yathāsaṃkhyam - mātuḥ kulāni trīṇi, bhartuḥ pituḥ kulāni ca |
nābhau pṛthivīcakre cakrakulaṃ bhavati, kaṇṭhe agni- padmaratnakulaṃ bhavati, guhyaṃ jñānakamale katikākulaṃ bhavati, māturyathāsaṃkhyaṃ kāyo ya vāścittañca |
evaṃ śirasi toyakamale kamalakulaṃ bhavati; hṛdaye vāyukamale khaṅgakulaṃ bhavati |
tadvaduṣṇīṣamadhye ākāśadhātukamale vajrakulaṃ bhavati |
bhartuḥ kāyavākcittañca yathākramaṃ rajaḥ śukrayoḥ kulaṃ bhavati |
pṛthivyādidhātūtpannānāṃ vikārāṇāṃ yathānukrameṇeti |
vāyunāḍībhedena tānyevāha tānītyādi - iha prāṇāpānatrināḍikābhedataḥ trināḍīsaṃjñāla kṣaṇāni bhavanti - viḍnāḍī cakrakulamapāne, sūryanāḍī ratnakulaṃ prāṇe, śukranāḍī kartikākulam apāne iti mātustri- kulāni kāyavākcittāni tathā candranāḍī prāṇe kamalakulam, apāne mūtranāḍī khaḍga- kulam, prāṇe madhye rāhunāḍī vajrakulamiti bhartuḥ kāyavākcittānīti ṣaṭkulaniyamaḥ |
kāye bhāvapraveśa-dhātuvarṇā ucyante kāya ityādinā - kāye bhāvapraveśaḥ khamiva samaraso bhāvamadhye ca kāyo jñātavyo yogayuktaiḥ prakṛtiguṇavaśād dhātuvarṇādibhedaiḥ |
pītaḥ kṛṣṇaśca varṇaṃstvaruṇa iti sito bhūmivātāgnitoye jñānākāśe ca nīlo bhavati ca haritaḥ kāyabhāvaprabhedaiḥ || 29
iha śarīre grāhagrāhakabhedo dhātūnāṃ parasparaṃ bhavati |
tatra yo grāhako dhātuḥ sāmagrīvaśāt sa kāyasaṃjño bhavati; yo grāhyaḥ sāmagrīvaśāt sa bhāvasaṃjño bhavati |
tena grāhyagrāhakayoḥ parasparamekatvaṃ samarasatvam, ataḥ kāye bhāvapraveśaḥ |
khamiva samaraso bhāvamadhye ca kāyo, jñātavyo yogayuktaiḥ prakṛtiguṇavaśād dhātuvarṇādibhedairiti |
atra prakṛtayaḥ pṛthivyādidhātavaḥ kāyendriyādīnāṃ gandhādi - viṣayāṇāṃ caturṇāṃ bhavanti |
atra kāyabhedena kāyendriyam, gandhaviṣayam, pāyvindriyam, ālāpaḥ pṛthivīkāyasvabhāvataḥ pītavarṇāḥ bhāvavaśāt, vāyuprakṛtitaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇāḥ |
evaṃ ghrāṇendriyam, sparśaḥ, vāgindriyam, viṭsrāvo vāyuprakṛtayaḥ kāyabhedena kṛṣṇavarṇāḥ; pṛthivī bhāvabhedena pītā: cakṣurindriyam, rasaḥ, pāṇīndriyam, gatistejaḥ prakṛtayaḥ kāyavaśād raktavarṇā bhavanti, bhāvavaśādudakaprakṛtayaḥ sitā bhavanti |
evaṃ jihvandriyam, varṇo rūpam (varṇarūpam), pādendriyam, ādānamudakaprakṛtayaḥ kāya- bhedena sitavarṇā bhavanti, agniprakṛtibhāvavaśād raktavarṇā bhava nti |
bhūmivātā- gnitoye bhūprakṛtau vātaprakṛtau agniprakṛtau toyaprakṛtau varṇo veditavyaḥ |
evaṃ śrotrendriyam, gharmadhātuviṣayam, bhagendriyam, śukracyavanam, ete ākāśaprakṛtayaḥ kāyabhedena haritavarṇāḥ, vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ adhyātma- jñānaprakṛtibhāvabhedena nīlāḥ |
tathā manendriyam (mana indriyam ), śabdaḥ, divyendriyam, mūtrasrāvaḥ, ete jñānadhātuprakṛtayaḥ kāla ya bhedena nīlavarṇāḥ ākāśaprakṛtayo bhāvabhedena haritāḥ |
ato jñānākāśe ca nīlo bhavati ca haritaḥ kāyabhāvaprabhedairiti kāya bhāva- prakṛtivarṇaniyamaḥ |
prajñopāyāliṅganamasthimāṃsādidhātūnāmāha prajñetyādi— prajñopāyo'sthimāṃsaṃ sasalilarudhiraṃ pāvako mūtrameva vāto viṭśūnyaśukra bhavati varatanau śrīrajaścittameva |
śrotrācchandādayo'nye svajinakulavaśād vāksvarūpādayaśca evaṃ devyaḥ sabuddhā viṣayaviṣayiṇo maṇḍale veditavyāḥ ||30
iha śarīre prajñā asthidhātuḥ, upāyo māṃsadhātuḥ, tayoḥ parasparaṃ saṃyogaḥ pṛthivī- saṃskārayoḥ, yat kaṭhinatatvaṃ tat pṛthivīdhāturiti jñāpakāt prajñopāyāsthimāṃsamiti |
evaṃ prajñopāyaḥ sasalilarudhiramudakadhātunā sārddhaṃ vedanāskandhaḥ |
tathā pāvaka iti uṣṇatā mūtramiti saṃjñā, anayo prajñopāyataḥ |
nanu śukradhātuḥ saṃjñāskandhaḥ, kathaṃ mūtra- mityucyate ?
iha śarīre yā saṃjñā sā jātamātrasya bālasya mūtrasrāvataḥ yanmahāsukhajñānaṃ tat ṣoḍaśavarṣāvadherbhavati śukracyavanadharmataḥ |
ato mūtraṃ saṃjñādharmaḥ, śukraṃ jñānadharma iti viśeṣāt mūtraṃ saṃjñāskandha iti |
evaṃ* vāto viṭ prajñopāyavāyudhātu rūpaskandha iti |
śūnyamiti rajodhātuḥ, śukramiti vijñānadhātuḥ prajñopāya iti |
bhavati varatanau śrīrajo dharmadhātuviṣayaṃ ra bodhicittameva |
evaṃ śrotrāt śabdādayo'nye iti |
śabdaḥ prajñā śrotra upāya:, gandhaṃ prajñā ghrāṇopāyaḥ (ghrāṇa upāya :), rūpaṃ prajñā cakṣurupāyaḥ, rasaḥ prajñā jihvopāyaḥ, sparśaḥ prajñā kāya upāyaḥ, dharmadhātuḥ prajñā ma upāyaḥ, viṭsrāvaḥ prajñā guda upāyaḥ, gati prajñā pāda upāyaḥ, ādānaṃ prajñā kara upāyaḥ, ālāpaḥ prajñā vāgindriyamupāyaḥ, mūtrakriyā prajñā bhaga upāyaḥ, liṅgaṃ vā śukracyavanaprajñā śaṅkhinīndriya- mupāya iti |
25 3 svajinakulavaśāt vāksvarūpādayaśca karmendriyādyā iti |
evamuktakrameṇa devyaḥ pṛthivyādayaḥ sabuddhā vairocanādibhiḥ sārddha viṣayaviṣayiṇo maṇḍale vakṣyamāṇe vaktavyā iti parasparaṃ prajñopāyāliṅganavidhiniyamaḥ |
idānīṃ candrasūryaśukarajaḥ kṛtyamucyate nāḍītyādinā- nāḍīnāṃ ṣaṭsahasraṃ dinakaraguṇitaṃ garbhamadhye ca kṛtvā varṣe pūrṇe ca vṛddhi tyajati śaśadharo'rkaśca vṛddhi karoti |
paṭale ] samudayasatyādimahoddeśaḥ varṣeṣu dvādaśeṣu tyajati dinakaro dhātuvṛddhi svadehe
nāḍīcchedaṃ prasūte bhavati dinadinaṃ yugmasaṃkhyākrameṇa || 31 ||
iha śarīre garbhamāsādekādūrdhvaṃ pratyahaṃ nāḍīśatadvayaṃ bhavati;

| nāḍīnāṃ ṣaṭsahasraṃ dinakaraguṇitamiti dvādaśaguṇitaṃ garbhamadhye ca[vṛddhi] ' katvā

iha bāhye saptavārāḥ |
aṣṭamī kulikā bhuktiḥ śarīre te hṛtkamaladaleṣvaṣṭaṣvaṣṭau bhavanti — pūrvadale ādityaḥ āgneyyāṃ somaḥ, dakṣiṇe maṅgalaḥ, nairṛ tye budhaḥ, vāruṇye paṭale ] samudaya satyādimahoddeśaḥ bṛhaspatiḥ, vāyavye śukraḥ, kubere śani:, īśāne kulikābhoge ketuḥ , apara bhogārdhe rāhuriti prakaṭitaghaṭikā nābhipadma e ca ṣaṣṭiḥ |
dala' catasraḥ śūnyaghaṭikādalāni śeṣāṇi ṣaṣṭidalāni ṣaṣṭimaṇḍalavāhinyaḥ dalāni ṣaṣṭighaṭikā iti pratyekavārasya ṣaṣṭisaṃkhyā |

vedā iti caturthaghaṭikātmakaḥ caturthadale |

śaśina iti ekaghaṭikātmakaṃ padaṃ saptamaṃ saptame dale |
evaṃ vilomenāṣṭame ekaghaṭakātmaka padam , navame dvighaṭikātmakam, daśame trighaṭikātmakam, ekādaśame (śe) caturghaṭikātmakam, dvādaśame (śe) pañcaghaṭikātmakam, trayodaśe caturdaśe'pyevaṃ jñeyamiti |
candrapadāni manu saṃkhyakāni bhavanti |
nṛpa ityā- mantraṇam |

aśvinī dvitīyāyām, bharaṇī tṛtīyāyām, kṛttikā caturthyām; tato daṇḍanakṣatraṃ sa ( rā ) lākā sapta saptadalasaṃjñā nāḍya iti |
tata uttarasa (śa) lākāsu saptasu vāmāvarttena prathamasa ( rā ) lākāyāṃ rohiṇī, dvitīyāyāṃ mṛga- śirā, tṛtīyāyā mārdrā, caturthyāṃ punarvasuḥ, pañcamyāṃ puṣyaḥ, ṣaṭhyāmaśleṣā, saptamyāṃ maghā, tata evamanyāpi veditavyā iti |
ṛkṣāṇāṃ nābhipadyeṣu punarapi ghaṭikāḥ ṣaṣṭi vedi- tavyeti |
nāḍyaḥ pāṇīpalāni ṣaṣṭiḥ, ṣaṣṭīnāṃ ṣaṭtriṃśa[t śa] tāni sphuṭasakalatanau hastapādādisandhāviti vakṣyamāṇe vaktavyo vistareṇeti vārādiniyamaḥ []|

40 ||
paṭale ]
samudaya satyādimahoddeśaḥ 179 iha bāhye sūryasya meṣādiviṣamalagne śūnyamaṇḍalādibhogaḥ, vṛṣabhādisamalagne pṛthi- vīmaṇḍalādibho[100b] gaḥ
tathādhyātmani śūnyādyaṃ pañca tatvamiti śūnyaṃ vāyuragni- stoyaṃ pṛthivīmaṇḍalaṃ pravahati niyatamuttare vāmanāsāpuṭe
kṣmādi ( dī ) ti pṛthivyaptejovā- yuśūnyamaṇḍalaṃ savyanāsāpuṭe vahati
eṣu vāma ( apasavya ) savyeṣu daśamaṇḍaleṣu yathāsaṃkhyaṃ vāme śūnyamaṇlaṃ rāhuradhidevatā pravāhakāle; dakṣiṇe kālāgniḥ vāme vāyumaṇḍale candraḥ, dakṣiṇe sūrya:, vāme vahnimaṇḍale budhaḥ, dakṣiṇe maṅgalaḥ; vāme toyamaṇḍale śukraḥ, dakṣiṇe bṛhaspatiḥ; vāme pṛthivīmaṇḍale ketuḥ, dakṣiṇe mandaḥ ; evaṃ khādiṣu maṇḍaleṣvadhipatayo daśa kṣetriṇo bhavantīti
evaṃ ṣaṇmāsaṃ candra īśo bhavati, meṣamithunasihatulādhanuḥ, kumbharāśau vāmanāḍyāṃ candraḥ śukradhāturośa uttare; dakṣiṇe vṛṣabhakarkaṭakanyāvṛścika- makaramīna rāśī; arko rajodhāturīśo bhavati
narapata ityāmantraṇamiti maṇḍalādhipati- niyamaḥ
idānīṃ prāṇādayo vāyava ucyante tasyetyādinā- tasyordhvaṃ hṛtpradeśe vasudalakamale saṃsthitaṃ nāḍicakra' prāṇādyaṃ vāyuvṛndaṃ raviśaśiśikhino vāmasavye ca madhye
vāme nāḍī śaśāṅko vahati khalu sitā dakṣiṇe raktasūryā madhye kālāgnirūpā pravahati viṣuve hīnaniḥśvāsaṣaṣṭiḥ || 41
5 10 15 iha nābhicakre garbhe caturdalātmake, tadbāhne aṣṭadalātmake, tadbāhaye dvādaśa dalātmake, tadbāhaye catuḥṣaṣṭidalātmake
dalaśabdena nāḍyaḥ śvāsadharmiṇyo gṛhyante
tasya nābhicakrasyordhvaṃ sārddhadvādaśāṅgulādūrdhvaṃ hṛtpradeśe saṃsthitam, nāḍīcakramaṣṭāram aṣṭanāḍyātmakam
teṣu prāṇādyaṃ vāyuvṛndaṃ vasudalakamale śaśi - raviśikhinaḥ
śikhoti rāhuḥ
vāme candranāḍī śaśāṅko vahati sitavarṇā, dakṣiṇe rakta- varṇā sūryasya nāḍī va [10]a]hati, aṣṭasu daleṣu madhye kālāgnirūpā nāḍī rāhunāḍī pravahati, viṣuve lagnodayābhisandhī sapādaṣaṭpañcāśat śvāsāni ( i ) ti
asminnaṣṭāre hṛdabje praharasaṃkrāntināḍyo veditavyāḥ
nāsārandhre pañcamaṇḍalavāhinyo'mī bhavanti
nāḍyā nāḍyāṃ prāṇasañcāraḥ saptaviṃśatiśataiḥ śvāsapraśvāsairbhavati sārddhasaptadaṇḍairiti
prāṇo'pānaḥ samānaḥ kamalavasudale mārutaścāpyudāno vyāno nāgaśca kūrmo'tha kṛkarapavano devadatto dhanañjaḥ
ityevaṃ nāḍi (ḍī) cakre daśavidhapavanāḥ saṃsthitāḥ karmabhedaiḥ śaṅkhinyantaṃ tviḍādyaṃ svahṛdayakamalaṃ nābhicakraṃ samastam || 42
maṇḍaḥ; 25 T 180 vimalaprabhāyāṃ vyadale'lambuṣānāḍyām; devadatta uttaradale iḍānāḍyām; dhanañjaya īśadale kuhānā- ḍyām; suṣumnāyāṃ prāṇaḥ, diksaṃkhyā saṃ ( śaṃ) khinīyā sravati bodhicittaṃ tasyāmapāno'dhi- devatā
ityevaṃ nāḍi (ḍo) cakra e daśavidhapavanāḥ saṃsthitāḥ karmabhedaiḥ
eṣāṃ prāṇādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ vā dvāsaptatināḍīsahasreṣu vyāptiḥ kintvaṣṭasthāneṣu janmāṣṭānāmiti
saṃ (śaṃ)- khinyantaṃ tviḍādyaṃ svahṛdayakamalaṃ nābhicakraṃ samastam, janmasthānaṃ samānādīnāmiti cakraniyamaḥ
idānīṃ prāṇādikṛtyamucyate prāṇa ityādinā - prāṇaḥ prāṇaṃ karotyarkaśaśipathagatastvannapānaṃ samastam āpāno netyadhastāt sakalasamarasaṃ neti kāye samānaḥ
kāye spandatyudāno mukhakaracaraṇairgītanāṭayaṃ karoti vyāno vyādhiṃ karoti prakṛtiguṇavaśād gātrabhaṅgaṃ tathaiva || 43
iha śarīre prāṇaḥ prāṇaṃ balaṃ karoti arkaśaśipathagataḥ san; arkapathaḥ piṅgalā, rasanā vā; śaśipatha iḍā, lalanā vā; tasyāḥ pathagata iti
tu nipātanārtham
annapānaṃ samastam, yat rasaṃ bhakṣitamudakādipītam, tat sakalamapānavāyuradho netītyāgamaḥ
nayati sakalaṃ samarasaṃ tulyarasaṃ yāvat kāye samāno nāma vāyuḥ, saptadhātuṣu samarasaṃ karotī- tyarthaḥ
kāye spandatyudāno nāma vāyurmukhakaracaraṇairgītanāṭyaṃ karoti
atra śarīre sa evodāno mukhena gītādikaṃ hāsyālāpādikaṃ karoti, karacaraṇairnāṭyaṃ karoti, gamanādikaṃ ceti
vyāno vyādhi karoti prakṛtiguṇavaśāditi udakaprakṛtivaśād gātrabhaṅgaṃ tathaiva karoti yathā vyādhim
nāgo'pyudgārameva sphuṭakaracaraṇāt saṃkucan kūrmavāyuḥ krodhaṃ kṣobhaṃ samastaṃ sa kṛkarapavano jṛmbhikāṃ devadattaḥ
kāyaṃ pañcatvagantuṃ (gataṃ tyajati (na) narapate vāyureko dhanañjaḥ evaṃ prāṇādisarve prakṛtigataguṇān vāyavo na tyajanti || 44
nāgo'pyudgāraṃ karotyeva sphuṭakaracaraṇāt saṃkucan kūrmavāyuḥ sa iti krodhaṃ kṣobha samastaṃ kṛkaro vāyuḥ karoti; jṛmbhikāṃ devadattaḥ karoti; kāyaṃ pañcatvaṃ gataṃ ghanañjayo vāyunaṃ tyajati
narapate ityāmantraṇam
vāyureko dhanañjaya iti
evamukta- krameṇa vāyavaḥ prāṇādayaḥ sarve prakṛtigataguṇān vāyavo na tyajanti, ākāśā- diprakṛtiguṇāniti prāṇādikṛtyaniyamaḥ
paṭale ] samudaya satyādimahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ nāḍīsaṃjñā tīrthikasaṃjñābhirucyate piṅgatyādinā - piṅgāḥ sūkṣmāstviḍādyāstrividhapathagatā rohiṇī hastijihvā pūṣā ṣaṣṭhī jayā'gāpi ca vasu navame'lambuṣā śrīkuhe ca
diksaṃkhyā śaṅkhinī yā sravati narapate bodhicittaṃ sukhānte etat śrīnāḍicakraṃ bhavati bahuvidha sandhibhedairanekaiḥ || 45
piṅgāḥ sūkṣmāstviādyāstisro nāḍya iḍādayaḥ
trividhapathagatā iti candrapatha- gatā iḍā, sūryapathagatā piṅgalā, rāhupathagatā suṣumnā iti rohiṇī caturthī, hastijihvā pañcamī, pūṣā ṣaṣṭhī, jayā'geti saptamī, api vasu aṣṭamī, alambuṣā navamī, kuhā dik- saṃkhyā śaṅkhinī yā daśamī śaṅkhinī bodhicittaṃ travati sukhānte
etat śrīnāḍicakraṃ ' bhavati bahuvidhaṃ sandhibhedairanekairiti
atra śarīre sandhibhedāḥ ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatasaṃkhyāstaiḥ sandhibhedaiḥ ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatanāḍīcakrabhedā bhavantīti
prāṇādivāyuprakṛtyabhedā apyevaṃ jñeyā iti nāḍīcakraniyamaḥ
indre'gnau yāmyadaitye savaruṇapavane yakṣarudre ca madhye eṣu sthāneṣu śaktibhraṃmati dinaniśaṃ tatsvarūpaṃ bhajantī
tasyordhvaṃ kaṇṭhacakre grahagaṇasahitaṃ saṃsthitaṃ carkṣacakraṃ brahmāṇḍe śrīlalāṭe svaraparikarita: ( ) śvetabinduṃ sravan vai ||46
idānīṃ tasminneva nāḍīcakre indrādyadhiṣṭhānamucyate indra ityādinā - iha indradale prāṇaśaktirindrasvabhāvaṃ bhajati praharamekaṃ yāvat; agnidale agni- svabhāvam, yāmyadale yamasvabhāvam, naiṛtyadale nairṛtyasvabhāvam, vāruṇyadale varuṇasva- bhāvam, vāyavyadale vāyusvabhāvam, dhanadadale dhanadasvabhāvam, īśadale rudrasvabhāvam
evameṣvaṣṭadaleṣu tatsvabhāvaṃ bhaja ntī bhramati prāṇaśaktiriti indrādisvabhāva- niyamaḥ
idānīṃ kaṇṭhacakramucyate- iha hṛtkamalasyordhvaṃ sārddhadvādaśāṅgalānte kaṇṭhacakre prahagaṇasahitaṃ saṃsthitaṃ cakṣaṃ- cakram
catvāri daṇḍanakṣatrāṇi, aṣṭāviṃśati pradhānanakṣatrāṇi; ebhirdvātriṃśaddalakam
caturdaṇḍanakṣatrāṇi tyaktvā prāṇasyāṣṭāviṃśaddalakeṣvahorātreṇa saṃkramaṇaṃ ṣaṣṭidaṇḍebhyo bhāgena labdhaṃ pratyekadale sañcāra iti
brahmāṇḍe śrīlalāṭe svaraparikaritaṃ śvetabinduṃ sravan vai
tasyāpi kaṇṭhakamalasyordhvaṃ sārddhadvādaśāṅgulānte lalāṭakamale ṣoḍaśadale daladvayaṃ tyaktvā caturdaśadaleṣu prāṇasyāhorātreṇa sañcāraḥ; ṣaṣṭidaṇḍebhyaścaturdaśabhāgalabdhena pratyekadalatyāgaḥ
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ adhyātma- binduvisargau kaṇikāyāṃ veditavyo candrapadavāheti
punarnābhicakre catuḥṣaṣṭināḍyo bhīmā- disaṃjñābhiḥ hṛdaye aṣṭa iḍādisaṃjñābhi: ; kaṇṭhe'śvinyādisaṃjñācatuḥ sandhyāsaṃjñābhiḥ; lalāṭa- nāḍyāṃ pratipadādisaṃjñābhiḥ ; uṣṇīṣe catuḥ sandhyābhedena catuḥ tattvasaṃjñābhiḥ ; ityeṣu kaṇṭha- lalāṭakamaladaleṣvantarbhūtamaṣṭadalaṃ cakraṃ veditavyam
evaṃ nābhau hṛdaye kaṇṭhe lalāṭe aṣṭāra- cakraṃ pṛthivyaptejovāyusvabhāvam; uṣṇīṣe caturdalaṃ śūnyasvabhāvam; nābhau caturdalaṃ jñāna- svabhāvam; guhye dvātriṃśaddalakam; bāhya ṣoḍaśadaleṣu svaranāḍyaḥ; antardaśadaleṣu skandha- dhātunāḍyaḥ; tato'ntardaleṣu ṣaṭ śūnyanāḍya iti
evaṃ ṣaṭcakrasaṃkhyā ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaika- śataṃ ṣaṭcakreṣu vakṣyamāṇeṣu vaktavyami [ ti] ṣaṭcakraniyamaḥ
mūle pṛthvyambu vāme pravahati hutabhuk dakṣiṇe mūrdhni vāyu- maṃdhye vyomadvayośca pravahati viṣuvaṃ nāsarandhrakrameṇa
bhindantyetāni śaktirvrajati parapadaṃ dvādaśāntaṃ kalāntaṃ kṣetrādyā hastapāde karakamalapuṭe'ṅguṣṭhakāntāḥ samastāḥ || 47
idānīṃ nāsāpuṭadvaye pañcamaṇḍalapravāha ucyate mūla ityādinā - iha vāmanāsāpuṭe dakṣiṇe ca mūle adho bhāge pṛthivīmaṇḍalaṃ vahati, ambu vāme vahati vāme da kṣiṇe ca nāsāpuṭe; nāsāpuṭadvayamadhyarekhābhimukhaṃ sarveṣāścottarastho meruriti jñāpakāt, pravahati hutabhu ṅmaṇḍalaṃ dakṣiṇe, dakṣiṇaṃ ca ubhayottarayorbāhayaṃ nāsārandhrabhāgamiti
mūrdhni vāyumaṇḍalaṃ vahati ubhayornāsāpuṭayoḥ madhye vyomadvayo- rnāsāpuṭayoḥ pṛthvyaptejovāyumaṇḍalamadhye vyomamaṇḍalaṃ vahati; vāmalagnasañcāre vāme, dakṣiṇa- lagnasañcāre dakṣiṇe maṇḍala pravāhaḥ
dvayornāsāpuṭayo: punaryaugapadyena viṣuvaṃ jñānamaṇḍalaṃ vahati prāṇaśaktiriti
bhindantyetāni nābhihatkaṇṭhalalāṭoṣṇīṣanāsāpuṭamaṇḍalāni bhindantī daṇḍa rāśi praharanakṣatratithi sandhyāmaṇḍalabhedairiti
śaktiḥ prāṇaśaktirvrajati parapadaṃ nirālambaṃ dvādaśāntaṃ dvādaśāṅgulāntamiti, kalāntaṃ ṣoḍaśakalāntamiti
iha pṛthivī- maṇḍalapravāha vāyurnāsāpuṭāt dvādaśāṅgulaṃ vrajati, tato nivartate; udakamaṇḍalavāyustrayo- daśāṅgulaṃ vrajati, tato nivartate; tejomaṇḍalavāyuścaturdaśāṅgulaṃ vrajati, tato nivartate; vāyumaṇḍalavāyuḥ pañcadaśāṅgulaṃ vrajati, tato nivartate; śūnyamaṇḍalavāyuḥ ṣoḍaśāṅgulaṃ vrajati, tato nivartate; jñānamaṇḍalavāyuḥ pṛthivīmaṇḍalānteṣu madhye praviṣṭā dvāda- śāṅgulaṃ vahati puṭadvaye, ākāśānte madhyamā praviṣṭā ṣoḍaśāṅgulaṃ vahati
iha yadā bāhye vṛddhiṃ gacchati, tadā'dhyātmani hrasvo bhavati; yadā ṣoḍaśādī bahihrasvo bhavati vāyu- nāḍīvaśena, tadā nābhau varddhante caturaṅgulānīti prāṇaśaktipravāhaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ kṣetrādaya ucyante kṣetra ityādinā- iha śarīre kṣetradvayaṃ bāhusandhidvaye, upakṣetramurusandha dvaye, chandohadvayaṃ upabāhu- paṭale ] cakravattimlecchayuddha kālacakrakulatantra nāḍīkulotpatti- mahoddeśaḥ sandhidvaye, upacchandohaṃ jānusandhidvaye, melāpakadvayaṃ karasandhidvaye, upamelāpakaṃ pādasandhi- dvaye, śmaśānadvayaṃ karataladvaye, upaśmaśānadvayaṃ pādataladvaye, caturmahāśmaśānāni karāṅguli- dvaye, upa śmaśānāni catvāri pādāṅgulidvaye iti kṣetrādini yamaḥ
iti śrīmūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ laghukālacakratantra rājaṭīkāyāṃ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ samudaya satyādimahoddeśo dvitīyaḥ || ||
cakravattimlecchayuddha - kālacakra kulatantra nāḍīkulotpatti-mahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ cakravattimlecchayuddhaṃ svadehe ucyate cakrītyādinā - cakrī vatre svadehe suravarapatayo dvādaśāṅgā niruddhāḥ samyagjñānaṃ hi kalkī gajaturagarathāḥ kiṅkarāryyapramāṇāḥ
pratyekaṃ rudrasaṃjñā prabhavati hanūmān śrāvakaṃ prāṇināṃ ca pāpaṃ mlecchendraduṣṭastvakuśalamapi yat kṛnmatirduḥkhadātā || ||
iha svadehe manuṣyadehe yo bāhya cakrī prathamapaṭale uktaḥ, sa dehe vajrī cittavajra ityarthaḥ
ye suravarapatayo dvādaśa īśvarādayaḥ, te dvādaśāṅgā niruddhā iti
yaḥ kalkī samyagjñānaṃ svadehe hi tasmāddhetoḥ
ye bāhya gajaturagarathāḥ kiṅkarā ārya-apramāṇā'- ścatvāro dehe, te yo bāhya rudraḥ sa dehe pratyekajñānasaṃjñī; yo bāhya hanūmān sa svadehe śrāvakajñānamiti svadehe mahā ' cakrī saparivāraṃ kalkī ca bhavati
tathā yo bāhya mlecchendraduṣṭo'sau pāpacittaṃ śarīre
yo bāhyaṃ kṛnmatirduḥkhadātā akuśalapatha iti dehe
aśvatthāmā tvavidyā danubalasakalaṃ mārapakṣaścaturdhā saṃhārastasya yuddhe bhavabhayanidhanaṃ śrījayo mokṣamārgaḥ
kailāśe dharmadānaṃ bhavabhayaharaṇaṃ dravyapūrṇā ca pṛthvī putro brahmāsureśastridaśanaraguroḥ pṛṣṭhataścāgrato yaḥ || ||
mañjuśrī rlokanāthastribhuvanavijayī śrīrajo bodhicittaṃ pṛṣṭhe brahmādivaṃśā vividhamahitale'nekabuddhā viśuddhāḥ
vimalaprabhāyāṃ evaṃ mlecchendrayuddhaṃ bhavati hi niyataṃ dehināṃ dehamadhye [ adhyātma- māyārūpaṃ tu bāhye khalu makhaviṣaye mlecchayuddhaṃ na yuddham || ||
yo bāhya aśvatthāmā sa dehe avidyāpravṛttiriti yad bāhya danubalaṃ hastyaśva- pādātikaṃ sa dehe mārapakṣacaturdhā
saṃhārastasya yuddha e, yo bāhya mleccheṣu tat svadehe bhavabhayasya nidhanam
śrījayo yo bāhya mahācakriṇaḥ kalkinaśca sa svadehe mokṣamārgaṃlābhaḥ
yad bāhya kailāśe dharmadānaṃ mahācakriṇaḥ tat svadehe bhavabhaya- haraṇam
yā bāhya dravyapūrṇā dharitrī sā śarīre dhātusamūho viśuddhaḥ
bāhya yau cakriṇaḥ putrau brahmāsureśāvabhūtām, pṛṣṭhataḥ ekakhaṇḍe dharmadeśakaḥ, agrato'grakhaṇḍe mleccha- dharmakṣayārthī
samañjuśrīrlokanāthastribhuvanavijayī tat svadehe rajo bodhicittaṃ sukhadamiti praṇidhānacittaṃ prasthānacittaṃ ca
pṛṣṭhe khaṇḍe ye brahmādivividhavaṃśāste svadehe anekabuddhā viśuddhāḥ skandhadhātvāyatanasvabhāvā iti mlecchendrayuddhaṃ bhavati hi niyataṃ dehināṃ dehamadhye
māyārūpaṃ tu bāhya khalu makhaviṣaye mlecchayuddha na yuddhamiti asya vistaro vakṣyamāṇe pañcamapaṭale paramākṣarajñānasiddhau vaktavya iti
idānīṃ madhyamā tripakṣaṃ trivarṣabhedaistrikulāditantrabhedaḥ samājādika ucyate kālābda ityādinā - kālābde vahnisaṃkhye'bdadinagaṇahate ṣaṭkulaṃ cāyanāṅkāt ṣaṭtriṃśanmāsabhedād rasaguṇitarasād yoginīnāṃ kulāni
vāracchedena labdhā prakaṭitaniyatā devatāḥ kālacako pakṣacchede'bhidhānaṃ bhavati narapate devatādevatībhiḥ || ||
iha kālābde madhyamā 'nāḍīvarṣe vahnisaṃkhye trivarṣasaṃkhye a bdadinagaṇahate ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatadinairhate sati aśītyuttarasahasro dinagaṇo bhavati
tadeva trivarṣaṃ trikulaṃ kāyavāka citta kulavarṣabhedena sattvarajastamaḥ svabhāvamiti tato dinagaṇādayanabhedena aśītyuttaraśatabhāgena labdhāni ṣaṭkulāni bhavanti
punarmāsabhedena tato dinagaṇāt triṃśadbhāgena labdhāni ṣaṭtraṃśat kulāni bhavanti
rasaguṇitarasāditi ṣaḍbhirguṇitāḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadyoginīnāṃ kulāni
evaṃ yoginīnāmupāyānāmiti tato'śītyuttarasahasra- dinagaṇāt vāracchedena labdhā prakaṭitaniyatā devatā kālacakra māṇḍaleyā iti
catuḥpañcāśadadhikaśataṃ śeṣaṃ prajñopāyayugalam; evaṃ ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaśataṃ kālacakram; sapta- bhāgāvaśeṣaṃ kulikāsthānādhidaivaṃ devatādevīcakraṃ kulikālakṣaṇamiti; punastasmāt pūrvadina- paṭale ] cakravattimlecchayuddha-kālacakrakulatantra nāḍīkulotpatti-mahoddeśaḥ gaṇādaśītyuttarasahasrāt pakṣacchede'bhidhānamiti pañcadaśabhāgena labdhābhirdevatādevatībhira- abhidhānaṃ bhavati
kālacakrādhipatimithunena sārddhaṃ dvāsaptatidevatādeva tīlakṣaṇamiti trivarṣabhederyoginītantraniyamaḥ; lokasaṃvṛtyā trikulaṣaṭ kulaṣaṭtriṃśatkulāni yoginītantra iti yoginītantraniyamaḥ; māyājālaṃ dināṅgāstri ( ṅgāt tri) vidhamapi bhavedrandhravedaiśca samyak bhūto bhūtārṇavaiḥ syācchikhijalanidhibhiḥ śrīsamājartubhedaḥ
tattvākhyaṃ ṣar̤abhirhīnaṃ tvaparamapi tayorardhabhedaivibhinno-
bhūyo miśro dvibhedastridaśanavadiśābhiśca randhraḥ satattvaiḥ || 52 ||
idānīṃ kriyāyoga- yogatantrāṇi triṣaṭprakārabhedenocyate (nte) māyājālami- tyādinā- iha madhyamātripakṣadināni pañcacatvāriṃśaddināni dvādaśaguṇitāni tāni dināṅgāni catvāriṃśadadhikapañcaśatasaṃkhyāni' bhavanti pratidinadvādaśalagnabhedāditi |
tasmād dināṅgāt catvāriṃśadadhikapañcaśatād vyavakalitaṃ māyājālaṃ trividha- mapi bhavet, eka randhravedaire konapañcāśadbhirdevatāyugmairbhavati bhūto dvitīyaṃ bhūtārṇavaiḥ syāditi pañcacatvāriṃśadbhiryugmairdevatānāṃ bhavatīti |
aparaṃ śikhijalanidhibhiḥ trayaśva- tvāriṃśadbhirdevatāyugmairiti |
ato dināṅgād ra vyavakalitastribhe[ daiḥ saptatriṃśada- dhikaśatasaṃkhyā bhavanti, dviguṇadevatādevyaḥ catuḥsaptatyadhikadviśatasaṃkhyā bhavanti, iti māyājālaniyamaḥ |
asya prapañcārthaḥ pañcamapaṭale vaktavya iti māyājālakriyāyogatantra- niyamaḥ |
idānīṃ ṣaṭprakāraḥ samājabheda ucyate- tasmāt vyavakalitāvaśeṣād dināṅgāt śrīsamājartubheda iti |
tattvākhyamiti pañcaviṃśatyātmakam, tasmād dināṅgāt pañcaviṃśadbhirvyavakalitairdevatāyugmairbhavati |
apara- mataḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ ṣaḍbhirhonamekonaviṃśatyātmakam, ekonaviṃśatibhirdevatābhirvyavaka- litairiti |
aparamapi tayoḥ pañcaviṃśatyekonaviṃśatyātmakayorardhabhedaivibhinna bhedo bhavati |
pañcaviṃśatyardhaḥ sārddhadvādaśaikonaviṃśatyarddhaḥ sārddhanava, ato'rddhaḥ sārddha dvādaśe praviśati |
tatra ekonavātmakaḥ samājaḥ, aparastrayodaśātmakaḥ |
bhūyo'paro miśro dvibhedo bhavati samājaḥ |
tridaśa iti trayodaśa |
navadiśā ekonaviṃśatiḥ |
ebhistridaśanava- diśābhiśca dvātriṃśadbhirdevatāyugmai samājo bhavati;
randhrernavabhiḥ satattvaiḥ pañcaviśadbhiḥ sahaḥ dvitīyaḥ samājaḥ catustriśadbhirdevatāyugmairbhavati;
evaṃ samājaḥ ṣaḍbhedabhinnaḥ |
END_OF_PDF_PAGE_229 vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ adhyātma- eṣu ṣaṭṣu tantrarājeṣu dvātriṃśadadhikaśatadevatāyugmaiḥ saṃkhyā dviguṇitā devatā- devatīsaṃkhyā catuḥṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatā iti |
evaṃ samājamāyājāleṣu navasu tantrarājeṣvadhi- patiyugmena sārddhaṃ catvāriṃśadadhika pañcaśata' dināṅgasaṃkhyā devatādevatyo bhavanti |
eṣu tantreṣu skandhadhātvāyatanacandrasūryacaraṇaniṣpattibhedena bhedo garbhe yathā tathā pañcamapaṭale āsanabhedena vaktavya iti yogatantraniyamaḥ |
iha garbhe jātakasya skandhadhātuniṣpattyā navātmakaḥ samāja:, tathā skandhadhātusahaja- dharmasambhoganirmāṇacakre niṣpattyā trayodaśātmakaḥ, tathā skandhadhātucakraviṣayaniṣpattyā ekonaviṃśatyātmakaḥ, tathā skandhadhātucakraviṣayaṣaṭ netrādiniṣpattyā pañcaviṃśatyātmaka iti |
tathā skandhadhātucakraviṣayendriyakarmendriyaguhyoṣṇīṣaka malaniṣpattyā dvātriṃśadā- [105b]tmaka iti |
tathā skandhadhātuṣaṭcakraviṣayendriyakarmendriyadivyendriyānandopetyā ( ānandānupetya) catustriśadātmaka: samāja iti |
garbhādhānāt ṣoḍaśavarṣāvadheryāvat ṣaṭprakāra utpādo bālānāmiti samājaniyamaḥ pañcamapaṭale vistareṇa vaktavyaḥ |
evaṃ māyājāle skandhadhātavo navalomādyaṣṭadhātavo dvādaśāyatanāni catuścakra- sahitāni vātapittaśleṣmasannipātadhātusahitāni ṣaṭkarmendriyāṇīti trayaścatvāriṃśa- dātmako māyājālaḥ |
uṣṇīṣaguhyakamalasahitaṃ pañcacatvāriṃśadātmakamiti rāgadveṣamoha- mānena sārddhamekonapañcāśadātmakamiti māyājālaniyamaḥ pañcamapaṭale vistareṇa vaktavya iti yoginīyogatantrotpādaniyamaḥ |
idānīṃ bāhyādhyātmamudrocyate - - iha sarvatra hetunā phalaṃ mudraṇīyam, phalena ca heturmudraṇīya iti |
rajodharmatvāt sūryaḥ prajñāhetuḥ, śukradharmitvāccandraḥ phalamupāyaḥ |
parāpekṣikayā śukrardhīmatvāccandraḥ prajñāhetuḥ, rajodharmatvādupāyaḥ sūryaḥ phalam |
evaṃ sarvatra prajñā śūnyatāhetuḥ upāyaḥ karuṇā phalamiti |
candrāṃśe ṣaḍ dinā ye (ni) khalu divasagaṇe vārabhāgena labdhāḥ ṣaṇmudrā vajriṇastāstvaparadinagaṇaḥ kāya vākcittamudrāḥ |
prajñākāye trilagnā prakaṭitaniyatā yoginī yogatantre śaktiḥ krodhāḥ krameṇa prabhavati daśakaṃ pañcakaṃ buddhadevyaḥ || 53 ||
atra ye candrāṃśe pañcacatvāriṃśaddinagaṇāstasmād vārabhāgena ?
labdhāḥ ṣaḍ dināni ṣaṇmudrā vajriṇo bhavanti - cakrī kuṇḍalaṃ kaṇṭhikā rucakaṃ mekhalā bhasmayajñopavīta- miti, bāhyamudrā kālacakrasya aparadinagaṇam ( : ) |
avaśeṣaṃ ra trisaṃkhyaṃ kāyavākucittamudrā adhyātmani vajriṇa iti bāhyādhyātmani nava mudrā veditavyāḥ |
prajñākāye prajñātantre upāyakāye upāyatantre trilagnā sattvarajastamātmakāḥ kāyavākcittalakṣaṇā iti yoginī yogatantre prakaṭito mudrāniyamaḥ |
[106a] END_OF_PDF_PAGE_230 paṭale ] cakravattimlecchayuddha-kālacakrakulatantra - nāḍīkulotpatti-mahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ kālacakradevatādevīsaṃkhyā ucyate śaktītyādinā - : iha ṣaṭcakranāḍīviśvasvabhāvo devatāgaṇa: |
śaktiḥ krodhāḥ krameṇa prabhavati daśakamiti daśa śaktayo daśa pāramitāḥ;
uṣṇīṣādayo daśakrodhā iti pratyekaṃ daśakam |
pañcakaṃ buddhadevya iti akṣobhyādayaḥ pañca buddhāḥ, vajradhātvīśvaryādayaḥ pañca devyaḥ iti pratyekaṃ pañcakam |
rūpādyakṣyādiṣaṭkaṃ punarapi nṛpate devavṛndaṃ dviṣaṭkaṃ yoginyo nāgacaṇḍāṣṭakamiti bhuvane sarvametad dviguṇyam |
etat śrīkālacakraṃ caturadhikaśataṃ tasya cārddha tridhātau sūryāṃśe yā ca ṣaṣṭiḥ pratidinaghaṭikā nirgatā māsamadhye || 54 ||
rūpādyakṣyādiṣaṭkamiti |
rūpavajrādayaḥ ṣaṭ kṣitigarbhādayaḥ ṣaṭ, evaṃ pratyekaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ punardevavṛndaṃ dviṣaṭkamiti indrādikaṃ dvādaśasaṃkhyam, yoginyo nāgacaṇḍāṣṭakamiti cacikādīnāmaṣṭakam, anantādīnāmaṣṭakam, śvānāsyādīnāmaṣṭakam, etat samastaṃ ( ) devatāgaṇam (:) sādhanāpaṭale pratyekaṃ nāmabhedairvaktavyamiti |
bhuvana iti bāhye'dhyātmani sarvametat punaḥ prajñopāyabhedena dviguṇyam;
etat śrīkālacakraṃ caturadhikaśataṃ tasya cāddhaṃ dvāpañcāśaddevatātmakam |
evaṃ sūryacandrabhedena punastadevaikatvena ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaika- śatadevātmakaṃ kālacakraṃ veditavyam iti kamalakaṇikāsthadevatāgaṇaniyamaḥ |
idānīṃ carcikādikamalapatradevya ucyante- sūryāṃśe dvātriṃśadbhāgena śvāsacakrāt pratidine ghaṭikādvayaṃ yallabdhaṃ māsasya madhye triṃśaddinaiḥ ṣaṣṭināḍyo bhavanti kulikārūpiṇyaḥ |
yoginyastāḥ samastāstribhuvananilaye lagnamiśrā dviguṇyā sūryāṃśe'haḥ prabhinnaṃ dviguṇitamapi yad yoginīvṛndamaṃtra- piṇḍībhūtāḥ samastāstriguṇaravidināssābhidhānā bhavanti candre pakṣaprabhedo bhavati dinakare vargabhedaḥ samastaḥ || 55 ||
tāśca yoginyo lagnamiśrā lagnacatuḥ sandhyākulikā vimiśrā catuḥṣaṣṭirbhavanti |
tāśca prajñopāyabhedena dviguṇitā aṣṭāviṃśatyadhikaśatasaṃkhyā bhavantīti |
punaḥ sūryāṃśe'haḥ prabhinnaṃ ṣaṣṭayuttaratriśatadinaprabhinnaṃ caitrāditithilakṣaṇaṃ devatāgaṇam |
tadeva dvādaśalāsyā- dibhiḥ sahitaṃ dviguṇitamapi viṃśatyadhikasaptaśatasaṃkhyaṃ devatīvṛndaṃ bhavatīti |
piṇḍībhūtāḥ samastā iti etā devyaḥ sābhidhānā dvāsaptatidevatābhiḥ sahitā mudrāṣaṭkena sārddhaṃ triguṇaravidinā trivarṣadinā aśītyuttarasahasrasaṃkhyā iti trivarṣadinagaṇasvabhāvā devatā- devyo veditavyā yoginītantre'[ itya]bhiprāyaḥ |
evaṃ sūryavarṣaprabhedo bhavati śaśadhare pakṣabhedaḥ samasto māyājālasamājayornavadhā catvāriṃśadadhikapañcaśatadevatāgaṇairiti |
kha kha?
ndhaḥ vimalaprabhāyāṃ evaṃ tatrādibuddhe svakaranṛpatayoḥ devatādevatīnāṃ
adhyātma- prajñopāyo niśāho bhavati hi samavibhāgo'rddharātre dinārddhe
yena jñātaṃ svadehe dinaniśisamayermāsasaṃkrāntibhedaiḥ sa śrīmān mañjuvajro bhavabhayamathano janmanīhaiva buddhaḥ || 56 ||
evaṃ sūryacandradevatāgaṇairekībhūtaiścādibuddha (ddho) bhavati
tasminnāvibubudhe svakaranṛpatayo viṃśatyadhikaṣoḍaśaśata saṃkhyā iti, teṣāṃ devatādevatīnāṃ prajñopāyaṃ (yo) niśāha iti
yathāsaṃkhyaṃ prajñā rātribhāgaḥ, upāyo divābhāgaḥ samavibhāgo madhyāhnā- darddharātre arddharātrānmadhyāhne sārddhatrayodaśanakṣatraiḥ daśādhikāṣṭaśatadaṇḍai[rvā ghaṭikā- bhirvā niśāvibhāgaḥ; evaṃ divāvibhāgaḥ
śrīmānna (ti) kṣatramaṇḍale ādibuddhe devatāgaṇo nakṣatranāḍīsaṃkhyātmako bhagavatokta iti
yathā bāhya nakṣatraghaṭikābhogaḥ sarvagrahāṇām, tathādhyātmani kāyavākcittajñāne ṣaḍindriyadharmāṇāmiti
yena jñātaṃ svadehe
evamukta- kramaiṇa prajñopāyātmakaṃ yenādibuddhaṃ (iti) svadehe jñātam
dinaniśisamayaiḥ sandhyā praharādi- bhedaiḥ māsasaṃkrāntibhedairdvādaśabhiḥ sa yogī śrīmān mañjuvajo bhavabhayamathano janmanīhaiva buddha iti laghutantra mūlatantradevatotpādaniyamaḥ
triśadbhāgena tasmāt triguṇitaniyatā devatāḥ kālacakre mudrāṣaṭkaṃ ca bāhyaṃ punarapi niyatāścakranāḍyastathaiva, uṣṇīṣe dvi: hṛdo'ṣṭau śirasi nṛpatayo dantasaṃkhyā ca kaṇṭhe nābhau cāṣṭāṣṭaguṇyā, dviguṇanṛpatayo guhyamadhye prasiddhāḥ || 57 ||
idānīmataḥ paramādibuddhāllaghutantrotpāda ucyate triṃśadityādinā - iha paramādibuddhāt viṃśatyadhikaṣoḍaśaśatāt triśadbhāgena labdhāḥ catupañcāśad- devatā bhavantīti
punaḥ kāyavākcittaguṇitāstriguṇā dvāṣaṣṭyadhikaśataṃ bhavati
eṣu māṇḍaleyāścatuḥpañcāśadadhikaśatasaṃkhyā maṇḍalesaṃ ( śaṃ) prajñopāyayugmaṃ bāhya mudrāṣaṭka- miti
kālacakraṃ paramādibuddhānnirgataṃ daśabhāgeneḍādisaṃ ( śaṃ) khinyantanāḍībhedeneti bāhya neyārthamiti laghukālacakraniyamaḥ
idānīṃ kālacakranāḍya ucyante cakranāḍyastathaivetyādinā - iha śarīre trikulanāḍyastisraḥ kāya vākcittavindudhāriṇyaḥ, nābhau guhyavajra- maṇāviti tathā ṣaṭkulanāḍyo lalanā - rasanā avadhūtī - viṇmūtra - śukravāhinya iti
tathā ṣaṭtriṃśat kulanāḍyaḥ uṣṇīṣādiṣaṭcakranāḍyaḥ ṣaṭ, dve śabdagrāhiṇyo, dve sparśa- grāhiṇyau, dve rasagrāhiṇyau, dve rūpagrāhiṇyo dve gandhagrāhiṇyo; etā daśa- nāḍyaḥ
nābhicakramadhye'paramaṇḍaleṣu dvādaśasaṃkrāntināḍyaḥ, aṣṭapraharanāḍyaḥ
evaṃ sarvāḥ ṣaṭtriṃśat kulanāyikā bhavanti - ṣaḍ rasarūpiṇyaḥ, ṣaḍ dhāturūpiṇyaḥ, ṣaḍindriyarūpiṇyaḥ, ṣaḍaviṣayarūpiṇya, ṣaṭ karmendriyarūpiṇyaḥ, ṣaṭ karmendriyaviṣayarūpiṇyaḥ, evaṃ ṣaḍ rūpapravartinyaḥ paṭale ] cakravatti mlecchayuddha kālacakrakulatantra nāḍīkulotpatti-mahoddeśaḥ ṣaṭ vedanāpravartinyaḥ, ṣaṭ saṃjñāpravartinyaḥ, ṣaṭ saṃskārapravartanyaḥ, ṣaḍ vijñānapravartinyaḥ, ṣaḍ jñānapravartanya iti ṣaṭtriṃśat kulanāḍyo'pyādhārādheyasambandhena dvāsaptatiriti jñeyāḥ
- tathāha — kālacakranāḍyaḥ uṣṇīṣe'dviriti caturdalarūpiṇyaḥ, catasra' 'ścatuḥ sandhyā- prarvartinyaḥ

atrādha ūrdhvaṃ dvināḍyorādheyo vāyuriti

dantasaṃkhyā ca kaṇṭha iti kaṇṭhacakre aṣṭāviṃśati nakṣatrāṇi, catvāri daṇḍanakṣatra pravartinyo dvātriṃśaditi
nābhau nābhikamale rāśināḍikābāhye ghaṭīnāḍyaḥ aṣṭābhiraṣṭaguṇitāścatuḥṣaṣṭināḍyaḥ ṣaṣṭimaṇḍalavāhinyaścatuḥ śūnyapravāhinyaḥ catuḥṣaṣṭi- daṇḍa pravartinya iti
dviguṇanṛpataya iti
dvātriṃśadguhyamadhye guhyakamalanāḍyaḥ śukrādi- dvātriṃśaddhātupravartanya iti
ṣaṇṇāḍyaścakrarodhā daśaviṣayaharāḥ sannipātasvabhāvā bhūyo bhūyo dviguṇyāḥ punarapi guṇitāḥ śleṣmapittānilāṃśāḥ
etā vai mṛtyunāḍyo guruniyamavaśādāyurārogyadāśca ṣaṭcakra e vāyunāḍyo maraṇabhayaharā yogināṃ nātra citram || 58 ||
eṣu ṣaṭ nāḍyaḥ ṣaṭ cakrarodhā bhavanti; daśa viṣayahāriṇyo bhavanti
tāḥ sannipātasvabhāvā iti tāḥ punadvaguṇyāḥ śleṣmāṃśāḥ viṃśatiḥ bhūyo dviguṇyā pittāṃśāḥ catvāriṃśad bhavanti, punarapi anilāṃśāstā'śītirbhavanti, iti ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaśatanāḍyaḥ ṣaṭcakreṣu vyavasthitāḥ kālacakranāḍyo'vagantavyāḥ
etā vai mṛtyu nāḍyo guru- niyamavaśādāyurārogyadāśca
ṣaṭcakre kālanāḍya uktā maraṇabhayaharā gurūpadeśena bhāvitāḥ satyo yogināṃ nātra citramiti
uṣṇīṣe'bdhirlalāṭe jaladhihatayugāḥ śleṣmadhātuprakopāḥ kaṇṭhe dantā hṛdabje nayanahatayugāḥ pittadhātuprakopāḥ
nābhau guhye'bdhiṣaṣṭirnṛpatirapi tathā vāyudhātuprakopā guhye'nyādiṣaṭkaṃ prakaṭitaniyatāḥ sannipātā nirodhāḥ || 59 ||
uṣṇīṣe'bdhiścatasraḥ jaladhihatayugā iti ṣoḍaśa lalāṭe, etā viṃśati nāḍyaḥ śleṣmadhātuprakopā iti
kaṇṭhe dantā iti dvātriṃśannāḍyaḥ
hṛdabje nayanahatayugā iti aṣṭa nāḍyaḥ, etāścatvāriśannāḍyaḥ pittadhātuprakopā iti nābhau ca guhyaṃ ca yathāsaṃkhyam abdhiṣaṣṭiriti catuḥṣaṣṭināḍyaḥ
nṛpatirapi guhyakamale bāhye parimaṇle nāḍyaḥ ṣoḍaśa, etā aśīti nāḍyo vāyudhātuprakoṣā iti
guhye'nyādik ceti daśa nāḍyo madhya- parimaṇḍale
