paṭale ] ariṣṭamaraṇalakṣaṇa- nāḍīccheda-mahoddeśaḥ karmacakra kriyāca madhyamādhaḥ parvasandhī ṣaḍnāḍīṣu |
evaṃ kha khi khṛ khla khaṃ iti caturthamudakamaṇḍalaṃ ṣaḍnāḍīṣu; karmacakre kriyācakre tarjanyadhaḥ parvasandhau ṣaḍnāḍīṣu; tathā ka ki kṛ ku kula kaṃ[ 118a] iti pañcame pṛthivīmaṇḍalaṣaḍ nāḍīṣu karmacakre ṣaṇmātrakakārasya kriyācakre'ṅguṣṭhādhaḥ prathamaparvasandhau ṣaḍnāḍīṣu |
evamekaṛtau dakṣiṇe vāme makarakumbhamāsayoḥ ṣaṣṭikādayo mātrāḥ saṃhārasṛṣṭibhedeneti tataśca vargāt samātrān mīnameṣabhedena kramati dakṣiṇabāhūpabāhusandhī, karmaca ke triṃśannāḍīṣu pṛthivyādi- pañcamaṇḍaleṣu ṣaḍdinabhedena cavargān sasvarān kramati |
saṃhārastadyathā - ccāḥ cclū ccū ccū ccī ccā ityavanau |
chchā chch chchū chchū chchī chchā iti jale |
jjāḥ jjlū jjū jj jjī jjā iti agnau |
jhjhāḥ jhjhḷ jhūjhū jhajhajhajhī jhjhā iti vāyau |
ññāḥ ñ ña ñña ññī ñña ityākāśe |
triṃśa- nmātrāḥ karmacakre kriyācakre'pi dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhamadhyaparvāt kaniṣṭhāmadhyaparvānte pañcasandhiṣu ṣaṭpaṭnāḍīṣu cādīni triṃśadakṣarāṇi saṃhārakrameṇa jñātavyāni iti |
evaṃ vāmabhujopabhuja- sandhau karmacakre triśannāḍīṣu kriyācakre'pi kaniṣṭhāṅgalīmadhyaparvāt aṅgaṣṭhamadhyaparvānte pañcasandhiṣu ṣaṭpaṭnāḍīṣu trādayo varṇāstraśad bhavanti tadyathā - ña ñi ñū ña ñ iti ākāśamaṇḍale, jhajhi jha jhu jhūla jhaṃ iti vāyau, ja ji nṛ ju jḷ jaṃ iti tejasi, cha chi chṛ chu chla chaṃ iti toye, ca ci ca cu cla caṃ iti pṛthivyām |
evaṃ vasantaṛtau kramati cavargaḥ rāśi' bhedeneti |
tato dakṣiṇakaropabāhusandhau karmacakre triṃśannāḍīṣu kriyāca dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhordhvaparvādipañcasandhiṣu ṣaṭṣaḍnāḍīṣu saṃhārakrameṇa ṭavargān kramati vṛṣabhedeneti; tadyathā - ṭṭāḥ ṭūla ṭū ṭṭu ṭṭī ṭṭā iti avanau, ṭhṭhāḥ laṭhṭha ṭha ṭhṭhī ṭhṭhā ityudake, ḍḍāḥ ḍḍḷ ḍḍḍū ḍḍḍa ḍḍī ḍḍā ityagnau, ḍhḍhāḥ ḍh ḍhḍhū ḍhar̤ha ḍhḍhī ḍhḍhā iti vāyau, ṇṇāḥ ṇa ṇṇū ṇṇū ṇṇī ṇṇā ityākāśe, evaṃ saṃhāreṇa |
tato vāmaka ropabāhusandhau sṛṣṭikrameṇa karmacakre triṃśannāḍīṣu kriyācakre vāmakarakaniṣṭhordhvaparvādaṅguṣṭhaparvasandhiṣu ṣaḍnāḍīṣu ākāśādimaṇḍalakrameṇa ṇakārādīnyakṣarāṇi; tadyathā - ṇa ṇi ṇū ṇu ṇ ṇaṃ [118b] ityākāśe, ḍhaḍhidṛढ़ dala ḍhaṃ iti pavane, ḍa ḍi ḍa ḍu ḍla ḍaṃ ityagnau, ṭhaṭhi ṭha ṭhu ṭhūla ṭhaṃ ityudake, ṭaṭiṭṭa ṭu ṭulu ṭaṃ iti pṛthivyām, vṛṣamithuna rāśibhedena kramati grīṣmaṛtau |
evamuttarāyaṇe aśītyuttaraśatākṣarāṇi kramati dinakaraścāyanaṃ rāśibhaidairiti |
paścādekottareṇotkramati dinadine iti paścādakṣiṇāyane candrāpānavāyau mṛtyuḥ kra.mati, sūrya utkramati ra hīno bhavati |
candrasya rātre vṛddhirbhavati, yasya vṛddhistasya caraṇeṣu mṛtyuḥ kramati iti nyāyāt sūrya utkramati hastāṅgulī mūrdhnisandhibhyo vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ adhyātma- yāvad dakṣiṇaskandhabāhusandhiprathamanāḍyāḥ paryantamiti |
atra dakṣiṇāyane candraḥ kramati dakṣiṇakaṭyurusandho karmacakre viśannāḍīṣu |
dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhādhaḥ parvāt kaniṣṭhādhaḥ parvāntaṃ yāvat saṃhārakrameṇa kriyācakre pañcāṅgulīparvasandhiṣu ṣaṭṣaḍ nāḍīṣu pavargākṣarāṇi triṃśat kramati, karkaṭabhedena trivarṣādhipatiḥ ; tadyathā - ppāḥ ppla ppū ppū ppī ppā ityavanau, phphā: phphlū phphū phpha phphī phphā iti jale, bbāḥ bblū bbū bbū bbī bbā iti tejasi, bhbhāḥ bhbhla bhbhū bhū bhī bhbhā ityanile, mmāḥ mm mmū mm mmī mmā ityākāśe, evaṃ karkaṭabhedeneti |
tato vāmakaṭyurusandhau sṛṣṭikrameṇākāśādimaṇḍalabhedena triṃśadakṣarāṇi vilomena:, tadyathā- mami mṛ mu mlṛ maṃ ityākāśe, bhabhi bhṛ bhu bhlṛ bhaṃ iti pavane, ba bi bṛ bu blū baṃ iti tejasi, pha phi pha phu phlu phaṃ ityudake, papi pṛ pu plū paṃ iti pṛthivyāṃ siṃhasaṃkrāntibhedena bhādrapadaṃ kramati |
evaṃ vārṣyaṛtuṃ candraḥ kramati, sūrya utkramati |
tato dakṣiṇorujānu- sandhau karmacakre kriyācakre pādāṅguṣṭhamadhyaparvāt kaniṣṭhāmadhyaparvāntaṃ pañcasandhiṣu ṣaḍnāḍīṣu tavargaḥ kramati tebhyo rāśisaṃhārabhedena triṃśadakṣarāṇi; tadyathā ttāḥ ḷ tū ttu ttī ttā ityavanau, ththāḥ ththḷ thyū thyū ththī thyā iti jale, ddāḥ dṛddī ddā iti tejasi, dhdhāḥ dhḷ dhdhū dhdhṛ dhdhī dhdhā iti vāyau, nnāḥ nn ntu nnu nnī nnā ityākāśe, evaṃ kanyā- bhedena kramati candra iti |
ta to vāmorujānusandhau karmacakre kriyācakre vāmapādaka- niṣṭhāmadhyaparvāṅguṣṭhaparyantaṃ pañcasandhiṣu ṣaṭṣaḍnāḍīṣu tadyathā- na ni nṛ nu (nḷ) naṃ ityākāśe, tathā dhadhi dhṛ dhu dhḷ dhaṃ iti vāyau, evaṃ da didṛdu dala daṃ iti tejasi, thathi thū thu thlṛ thaṃ iti jale, ta ti tṛ tu tlṛ taṃ iti pṛthivyām; evaṃ tulābhedena vāme sṛṣṭikrameṇa candraḥ kramati, sūrya utkramati śaradṛto iti |
tato dakṣiṇapādajānusandhau karmacakre triśannāḍīṣu kriyācakre dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhordhvaparvāt kaniṣṭhordhvapavaṃ yāvat pañcasandhiṣu ṣaṭṣaḍnāḍīṣu saṃhārakrameṇa vṛścikabhedena savargān triśadakṣarāṇi samātrāṇi kramati candraḥ; dakṣiṇe sūrya utkramati; tadyathā - ssā: sslū ssū ssa ssī ssā ityavanimaṇḍale, yyāḥ yyyyū yyū yyī yyā iti jale, ṣṣāḥ ṣṣlṛ ṣṣū ṣṣu ṣṣī ṣṣā iti tejasi, śśā: śla śśū śśū śśī iśā iti vāyau, evaṃ kā: klū kū kṛ kī kā ityākāśe ṣaḍnāḍīṣu triṃśadakṣarāṇi vṛścikabhedeneti |
tato vāmapādajānusandhau karmacakre triśannāḍīṣu kriyācakre kaniṣṭhāṅgulyūrdhvaparvādaṅguṣṭhordhvaparvaṃ yāvat pañcaparvasandhiṣu ṣaṭṣaḍnāḍīṣu sṛṣṭikrameṇa triṃśadakṣarāṇi candraḥ kramati |
sūryo vāmabhujāyāṃ skandhadhātūtkramati dhanurāśibhedeneti; tadyathā - ka ki kṛ ku kla kaṃ ityākāśe, tathā śaśi śṛ śu śla [śaṃ] iti vāyau, ṣa ṣi ṣṛ ṣu ṣla ṣaṃ iti tejasi, yya yyi yyū yyu yyla yyaṃ ityudake, evaṃ sa si sṛ su sla saṃ iti pṛthivyām, evaṃ triṃśadakṣarāṇi dhanubhedeneti |
ataḥ śiśiraṛtorārabhya candrasya kramaṇābhāvaḥ |
paṭale ] ariṣṭama raṇalakṣaṇa-nāḍīccheda-mahoddeśaḥ punarmakarādau sūryasya vṛddhiriti |
prathamavarṣe sattvaguṇabhedena kālaḥ sūryacandrābhyāṃ maha krama (ma)ti, tato dvitīye varṣe rajobhedena guṇavṛddhisvaraiḥ kādayo varṇāstriṃśat triṃśannā- ḍīṣu karmacakre kriyācakre pūrvavad veditavyā iti |
makare saṃhārakrameṇa tadyathā - kkāḥ kkāl kkau kkār kkai kkā ityavanau, tathā khkhāḥ khkhāl khkhī khkhār khkhai khkhā ityudake, ggāḥ ggāl ggauggār ggai ggā iti tejasi, ghghāḥ ghghār ghghau ghghār ghye ghghā iti vāyau, ṅṅāḥ ṅṅāl ṅṅau ṅṅār ṅa ṅā i[tyākāśamaṇḍale, dakṣiṇabāhoḥ pūrvavat makarabhedena sūryaḥ kramati, candro dakṣiṇapāde utkramati, tathā kumbhabhedena sṛṣṭikrameṇa guṇa- sahitebhyaḥ kramati, vāmabāhusandhau; tadyathā — ṅa ṅa ṅa ṅo ṅa ṅa ityākāśe, gha ghe ghar gho ghal ghaṃ iti vāyau, ga ge gar go gal gaṃ iti tejasi, kha khe khar kho khala khaṃ ityudake, ka ke kar ko kala kaṃ iti pṛthivyām, kumbhabhedena vāme sṛṣṭikrameṇa; evaṃ nīnameṣayoḥ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ cavargākṣarāṇi triṃśat-triṃśat-saṃhārasṛṣṭikrameṇeti dakṣiṇavāme sandhināḍīṣu vasantaṛtau, tathā ṭavargākṣarāṇi grīṣmaṛtau |
tataḥ sūrya utkramati, candraḥ kramati |
karkaṭake siṃghe(he) pādasandhau triṃśannāḍīṣu dakṣiṇe vāme pūrvavat vṛddhiguṇasahite- bhyatriṃśadakṣarebhyaḥ kramati |
vārṇyaṛtau pavargākṣarāṇi vṛddhiguṇairyuktāni kramati candraḥ, śaradi tavargākṣarāṇi, śiśire savargākṣarāṇi saṃhārasṛṣṭikrameṇeti |
evaṃ dvitīyavarṣe ṣaṣṭayuttaratriśatamātrān candrasūryau kramata iti; tatastṛtīye varṣe tamobhedena kālaḥ sūryeṇa sārddhaṃ kādivargebhyaḥ sasvarebhyaścarati' |
atra yaṇādeśāḥ svarā ucyante- taiḥ sārddhaṃ triṃśat-triṃśadakṣarāṇi tamoviṣayakālaścarati iti saṃhārasṛṣṭibhedena pūrvavaddhastapādasandhiṣu karmacakre kriyācakreṣviti; tadyathā - kkhā: kklā kkvā kkā kkyā kkhā ityavanau makarabhedeneti; [ tathā ' ] khkhhā : khlā rūkhvā khkhā khkhyā khkhhā iti jale; tathā gahā: galā gvā ggrā gayā rahā ityanale ghhā: ghghlā ghghvā ghghrā ghyā ghghhā iti vāyau ṅhāḥ lā vā ṅkā ṅayāṅaṅahā ityākāśe |
evaṃ sūryaḥ kramati dakṣiṇe, tato vāme sṛṣṭikrameṇeti |
haṅaya ṅ ṅva ṅlaṅahaṃ ityākāśe |
ghha ghya ghra ghva ghla ghhaṃ iti vāyau |
gha gya gragva gla gre iti tejasi |
khha khya khra khva
khla khhaṃ' ityudake
kha kya kra kva kla khūṃ ityavanau
evaṃ vāme sṛṣṭikrameṇa triṃśanmātrāṃ- śvarati sūryaḥ śiśira ṛtau
evaṃ vasante cavargaḥ, grīṣme ṭavargaḥ
tataḥ sūrya utkramati ṣaṭsandhibhyaḥ
candraḥ pādasandhau kramati
vārṣṇe pūrvavat pavargaḥ
śaradi tavargaḥ
hemante savarga:, saṃhārabhedena sṛṣṭibhedeneti
evaṃ tṛtīye varṣe'pi ṣaṣṭhayuttaratriśatamātrān kramati dinakaraḥ
kālavarṣa hi yasmāt tasmādevāśītyuttarasahasra dina ' kṣayastayorveditavya iti trivarṣanāḍīcchedo vyañjanānāmiti niyamaḥ
tataḥ chinne'bde sati pakṣamadhye pañcacatvāriṃśat hrasvadīrghapluta svaradharmeṇa svara- divasavaśāt pañcacatvāriṃśaddinavaśād rohaṇaṃ ca mṛtyoḥ sūryabhedena sūryasya, candrabhedena candrasya
rohaṇamiti kaṇṭhe aṣṭāviṃśad dināni, lalāṭe caturdaśadināni pūrvoktāni, guhye tridaśakamiti triṃśad dināni tataḥ kaṇṭhe dinacatuṣṭayam, lalāṭe dinadvayaṃ jīvitasya trirātram
aparamatra svaracchede tithiḥ ṣaṭpañcāśaddaṇḍātmikā grāhyā
evaṃ pañcacatvāriṃśattithayaḥ svaradharmiṇyaḥ tisraḥ śūnyadharmiṇya iti vyañjanadharme- 'pyevaṃ dinadvayam
etāsu dināṣṭakaṃ hṛdayakamale ārohate, tato daśavāyūnāṃ samāhāraḥ
kaṇikāyāṃ niḥśvāsocchvāsacakrasya yāvad hānidinaniśisamaye jīvitasyaikarātraṃ bhava- tīti
tataḥ karmavaśādanyatra vijñānasaṃkramaṇamiti niyamo madhyamāmaraṇe sati prāṇināṃ śatāyuṣāmiti nāḍīcchedaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ nirmāṇacakrādiṣu ṣaṭdināvadhermaraṇādavaśeṣanāḍikā daśavāyuvāhinya ucyante nābhāvityādinā - nābhau kaṇṭhe lalāṭe svahṛdayakamale nāḍikāḥ sāvaśeṣā- stiṣṭhatyarko raso'gniḥ svahṛdayakamale sārddhanāḍī tathaiva
pāṇeḥ pādasya sandhau tithiguṇitayugāḥ ṣaḍdinaṃ yāvadeva ante sarvatra sūkṣmā pravahati divasa ca tyajantī hṛdabjam //
iha nābhicakre bāhyamaṇḍalavāhinī nāḍīkṣayaparityāgāt trivarṣodhvaṃ tripakṣaro- haṇe vyañjanārohaṇe ṣaḍdinaṃ yāvad dvādaśarāśināḍyaḥ śūnyamaṇḍalapṛthivīmaṇḍalātmikāḥ śvāsacāreṇāvatiṣṭhante (te) arka iti
evaṃ kaṇṭhe ṣaṇmuhūrttavāhinyo rasa iti candrasyārddhapraharavāhinyaḥ tisro'gniriti hṛtkamale praharavāhinī sārddhanāḍī svahṛdaya- kamale sārddhaṃ nāḍī tathaiva sāvaśeṣā iti
pāṇeḥ pādasya sandhāviti ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśata- nāḍīmadhye karmacakre kriyācakre sandhiṣu tithiguṇitayugā iti ṣaṣṭināḍyo daśavāyuvā- hinya iti dvādaśasandhīnāṃ pratyekasandhau pañcapañcanāḍyo daśavāyuvāhinya iti śeṣāḥ kālamṛtyurvāyurmahātamo dharmo kriyācakre ṣaṣṭisandhiṣu ekaikanāḍī daśavāyuvāhinī pañcapañca- kālamṛtyumahāvāyuvāhinya iti
evaṃ ṣaḍdinaṃ yāvat tataḥ ṣaḍdinairindriyaviṣayanāḍīkṣayaṃ kṛtvā'nte maraṇadine sarvatra sūkṣmā madhyamāvadhūtī vahati candrasūryaviṇmūtravāhinīṃ tyaktvā mahātamaḥ kālavāyurmaraṇāntaṃ vahati yāvat śvāsacakrakṣayo bhavatīti niyamaḥ divasaṃ ca tyajantīti hṛdabjamiti
evaṃ sūryacandrārohaṇanāḍī vyañjanakarmakriyācakrāvaśeṣanāḍī- śvāsacchedakālamaraṇaniyamo bhagavatoktaḥ ariṣṭamaraṇalakṣaṇa- nāḍīccheda-mahoddeśaḥ
idānīṃ candrasūryacārakṣayeṇa kālavṛddhirucyate ṣatriṃśadbhirityādinā - ṣaṭtriṃśadbhiḥ sahasraṃ dhruśatadinagaṇaṃ rohate kālanāḍī yāṃ yāṃ sūryenduśaktidina niśisamaye pūritaṃ svasvacāraiḥ sūryā'haḥ sūryacāre tvṛṇamapi ca bhaveccandracāre tadarddha nakṣatrāhaḥ prabhede raviśaśicaraṇaṃ śūnyamāse ca mṛtyoḥ //
70 //
iha varṣaśate dinagaṇe kṛte ṣaṭtraṃśatsahastradināni bhavanti dhruriti varṣasaṃjñā taiḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadbhiḥ sahasai dhruśatadinagaṇai rohate kālanāḍī mṛtyuḥ nāḍīṣu varddhate dvāsaptati- sahasreṣu mṛtyuvāyuḥ praviśati pañcamaṇḍalavāyuḥ kramato'pasarati ṣaṣṭhāṃśaṃ tatra sthāpayi- tveti kāṃ rohate kālanāḍīvāyuḥ yāṃ yāṃ pūritaṃ sūryendorna śaktidina niśisamaye svasvacārairnakṣatracāreṇa sūryasyāśaktiriti tithicaraṇaiścandrasyāśaktiriti sūryā'ha iti dvādaśadināni sūryacāre tvaṇamapi bhaveccandracāre tadarddha ṣaḍdināni
atra pratimāse triśaddinairna rāśimekaṃ sūryaśca rati na candraḥ ṣaṣṭidaṇḍāniti dyau carati ato nakṣatrāhaḥ prabhedai raviśaśicaraṇaṃ śūnyamāse ca mṛtyoḥ tayoryatrāśaktiḥ sa śūnyamāso dvātriṃśanmāsānte sa ca catuḥṣaṣṭīnāḍyātmaka iti tāsu catuḥṣaṣṭināḍīṣu śūnyavāyuḥ prarohate nābhikamale dvāsaptatisahasrāṇāṃ madhye pratyahaṃ nāḍīdvayaṃ rohate iti sāmānyaśchūla- niyamaḥ
idānīṃ pratyahaṃ śvāsabhedenārohaṇamucyate prāṇā ityādinā - prāṇā dehe'dhikā ye prakaṭitaviṣuve vedapādonaṣaṣṭi: sarve saṃkrāntibhedaiḥ pratidinasamaye nāḍiyugmaṃ nihanti bhūyo bhūyo'bdamadhye raviśaśicaraṇāt śūnyanetrādrisaṃkhyā evaṃ kālaḥ śatābdaiḥ kramati suranṛṇāṃ svāyuṣaṃ svasvamānaiḥ //
71 //
iha ṣaṭśatādhikaikaviṃśatsahasrāṇāṃ madhye ye jñānamaṇḍalavāhino bhavanti te dehe śvāsādhikāra ityucyante prakaṭitaviṣuve ubhayalagnamadhye pāde vedonaṣaṣṭiḥ sapādaṣaṭ- pañcāśaditi te sarve dvādaśasaṃkrāntibhedaiḥ pañcasaptatyadhikapuṭaśataṃ bhūtvā pratidinasamaye nāḍīyugmaṃ nihantītyāgamapāṭhaḥ ghnantīti rūpam idaṃ nāḍīyugmaṃ vāme dakṣiṇe dvāsaptati- nāḍīsahasrāṇāṃ madhye nāḍīyugmaṃ ghnanti pañcamaṇḍalavāyusañcāravināśo bhavatīti śeṣāḥ pañcacatvāriṃśacchvāsāḥ tiṣṭhanti bhūyo bhūyo'bdamadhye evaṃ pratidinaṃ nāḍī yugmaṃ hanyamānā 'bdamadhye'bdadinaiḥ ṣaṣṭhyuttaratriśatadina raviśaśicaraṇāt dakṣi[ṇa vāmamaṇḍalasañcārāt śūnyanetrādrisaṃkhyā iti viṃśatyadhikasaptaśatānīti
evaṃ kālaḥ śatābdaiḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadbhiḥ sahasradinaiḥ kramati suranṛṇāṃ vā (svā) puṣa [ ] dvāsaptatināḍī sahasraṃ pañcamaṇḍala- vāhakam candrādityacaraṇamiti svasvamānaiḥ pūrvoktaḥ sūkṣmatanu ' [ja] bhūtadevādidinairiti nāḍīcchedaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ kālanāḍīsvabhāva ucyate daṣṭamityādinā - daṣṭaṃ vyādhiḥ prahāro yadi bhavati nṛṇāṃ kālanāḍyāṃ kadācit śīghra tenāśrayeṇa praviśati sahasā mūlacakreṣu kālaḥ ruddhvā cakreṣu nāḍī raviśaśigamanaṃ chedayitvā samastaṃ madhye sūkṣmāśritaṃ yad harati narapate jīvitaṃ prāṇināṃ ca //
72 //
iha śarīre kālanāḍyāṃ madhyamāpravāhakāle yadi daṣṭaṃ bhavati vyādhirbhavati prahāro vā nṛṇāṃ kadācit tadā śīghraṃ tenāśrayeṇa praviśati sahasā mūlacakreṣu ṣaṭsu kālo mahātamo vāyuḥ sa ca praviṣṭaḥ san ṣaṭcakreṣu ṣaṭpañcāśadama [ dhi ] kanāḍīśatam asau ruddhvā raviśaśigamanaṃ savyetaranāḍyāṃ chedayitvā samastaṃ madhye sūkṣmāśritam avadhūtīnāḍyāśritaṃ yajjīvitaṃ prāṇavāyuḥ tad haratira narapate jīvitaṃ prāṇināmiti kālanāḍīniyamaḥ
idānīṃ dharmacakrādo candracaraṇānyucyante hṛtpadma ityādinā - hṛtpadme śrīlalāṭe carati śaśipadaṃ bhūtadigvāsarākhyaṃ sambhoge nābhicakre navadaśasahitaṃ rudrayugmaṃ jinākhyam tattvākhyaṃ saptarātrāttyajati punarasāvutkraman yaḥ śaśāṅkī bindī pūrṇāṃ prakṛtya praviśati hṛdaye śuklapakṣe krameṇa //
73 //
iha candrasya caraṇabhedena caturdaśabhāgena samaviṣamagatena tithiṣu vṛddhirhānirvā sā saptamadine saptatithiṣu pañcaviṣayaguṇā bālakumārādibhedena saptame dine vṛddhi- rhānirvā nivartate viṃśatyadhikaśatacaraṇeṣu pūrṇeṣu atra viṃśatyadhikaśatāṃśānyucyante- iha caturdaśabhāgāvaśeṣe ekacaraṇe dṛṣṭe taddinakalāyāḥ ṣaṣṭināḍikāṃśānāṃ madhye pañcanāḍikāṃśāḥ kalāyā dhane'dhikā bhavanti kṛṣṇāyāḥ śuklāyā vā ṛṇasthāne hīnā bhavanti dvitīye dine dvitīyāyāḥ pañcāṃśāḥ prathamāyāḥ kumārabhedena dviguṇā jñātavyā iti
evaṃ tṛtīyadine tṛtīyāyāḥ pañcāṃśāḥ evaṃ dvitīyāyāḥ daśa prathamāyā vā[yu ] bhedena pañcadaśa evaṃ bālaprabhedena caturthyāḥ pañcāṃśāḥ tṛtīyāyā daśa dvitīyāyāḥ pañcadaśa prathamāyā yuvābhedenaikonaviṃśatiḥ evaṃ pañcame dine pañcamyāṃ pañcāṃśāḥ caturthyā daśa tṛtīyāyāḥ pañcadaśa dvitīyāyā ekonaviṃśatiḥ prathamāyā vṛddhabhedena dvāviṃśatiḥ evaṃ ṣaṣṭhe dine ṣaṣṭhyāḥ pañcāṃśāḥ, pañcamyā daśa, caturthyāḥ pañcadaśa, tṛtīyāyā ekonaviṃśatiḥ, dvitīyāyā dvāviṃśatiḥ prathamāyā ativṛddhi (ddha) bhedena caturviṃśatiḥ
evaṃ saptame dine saptamyāḥ pañcāṃśāḥ, ṣaṣṭhyā daśa, pañcamyā pañcadaśa, caturthyā ekonaviṃśatiḥ, tṛtīyāyā dvāviṃśatiḥ, dvitīyāyāḥ caturviṃśatiḥ, prathamāyāḥ pakvāvasthābhedena saptame dine pañcaviṃśa-
tyaṃśāḥ dhanaṃ vā ṛṇaṃ vā caraṇavaśād bhavanti
evaṃ bālādikrameṇa sarveṣāṃ prameyo jñeyaḥ
evaṃ saptatithīnāṃ caraṇāṃśān gṛhītvā hṛtkamalādiṣu viṃśatyadhikaśatadaleṣu śaśāṅkaḥ kramati, tataḥ punarutkramati
śaśipaḍhaṃ svakīyaṃ padamityarthaḥ
hṛdaye'ṣṭadaleṣvaṣṭāṃśāḥ, ṣoḍaśadaleṣu ṣoḍaśāṃśāḥ, sambhoge dvātriṃśadaleṣu dvātriṃśadaṃśāḥ, nābhikamale catuḥṣaṣṭidaleṣu catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśāḥ
evaṃ viṃśatyadhikaśatāṃśān bhuktvā candrakalāyā hānirvā vṛddhirvā nivartate
hṛtpadme lalāṭe ca bhūtadigvāsarākhyaṃ sambhoge nābhau ca navadaśasahitaṃ rudrayugmaṃ jinākhyam
tattvākhyaṃ saptarātrāttyajati punarasau utkraman yaḥ śaśāṅkaḥ
prathamakalāyāḥ pañcaviṃśatyaṃśāḥ paripakvāyā bhavanti sā cāṣṭame dine nābhāva [[]dhidevatā bhavati
punaḥ pūrṇamāsyāṃ nābhihṛtkaṇṭhalalāṭeṣu pūrṇaṃ karoti
bindusthāne bindoṃ pūrṇāṃ prakṛtya iti, tato nābheḥ punarutkraman catuḥṣaṣṭidaleṣu catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśāḥ, hṛdaye'ṣṭāṃśāḥ, kaṇṭhe dvātriṃśadaṃśāḥ, lalāṭe ṣoḍaśāṃśāḥ evaṃ caturdaśadinaiḥ padānāṃ pūrṇaṃ bhavati
tasmin pūrṇāṃ prakṛtya praviśati hṛdaye śuklapakṣe dhanapakṣe krameṇa
bhūyaḥ kṛṣṇe ca tadvad vrajati padapadān nābhipadme hi yāvada- ṣṭāre ṣoḍaśāre hṛdi śirasi tathā kṛṣṇaśukla bjasaṃjñe
kaṇṭhe dvātriṃśadāraṃ dviguṇitamaparaṃ raktapītaṃ ca nābhau dvaṣṭamyau nābhikaṇṭhe śirasi ca hṛdaye śvetakṛṣṇā ca pūrṇā || 74 ||
ādyāstriśat svarā ye hayaravalayutā bindubhiḥ śrīvisarge - bhinnāḥ ṣaṣṭirbabhūvuryuganṛpavasudantāśca te keśarāgre

kutaḥ ? yataḥ catuḥṣaṣṭyā bhāgalabdhe 'hargaṇe ṛṇaṃ bhavati; ataḥ pratyahaṃ sapādaṣaṭpañcāśatpāṇīpalāni catuḥṣaṣṭidinaidinamṛṇaṃ kalākṣayamityarthaḥ

saṃsāre ātmā kartā na tatra prabhavati nṛpate duḥkhasaukhyoḥ pradātā, duḥkhasaukhya- pradātā na kaścidasti tribhuvananilaye cittaśakta vivarjyaṃ

iha guhye daśa nāḍyaḥ sannipātakāriṇyaḥ ; tāsāṃ dhātusamatvakaraṇāyordhvādhaḥ prāṇāpānayonirodha iṣṭa iti jvare ceṣṭaḥ |
kasmāddhetoḥ ?
yato nābhyadhvaṃ vahati prāṇavāyuḥ, ataḥ sakalarujaharo nābhi- mūle apāno vahata, nābheradhaḥ sakalarujaharo'paraśceti vātādirogopaśamaniyamaḥ |
idānīṃ jaṭhararogopaśamanāya pratividhānamupadiśannāha ākuñcyetyādinā - ākuñcayāpānavāyuṃ trividhapathagataṃ prāṇamevordhvataśca saṅghaṭṭe yāvadagniḥ prabhavati balavān vyāpnuvan sarvakāye |
jagrī plīhārṣaṃ rogānapi jaṭharagatān māsayogācca hanti ūrdhvaśvāsaṃ ca kāśaṃ trividhamapi viṣaṃ netrarogādikañca || 111 ||
iha jaṭhare yadā jagracādikaṃ bhavati, tadā ākuñcyāpānavāyum adhastrividhapatha- gatamiti viṇmūtraśu krapathagataṃ prāṇamevoddha taścākuñcanīyaṃ candrasūryāgni- pathagatam; tayo saṅghaṭṭe yāvadagnirjaṭharāgniḥ prabhavati balavān vyāpnuvan sarvakāye |
asau'gnirjagrī plīhā rogānapi jalodarādīni māsayogācca hanti; ūrdhva- śvāsaṃ ca kāśaṃ ca trividhamapi viṣaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ kṛtrimañca netrarogādikaṃ śiraḥśūlaṃ hantīti jaṭhararogopaśamaniyamaḥ |
kakṣāt savyāvasavyāt stanamapi balavat pīḍitaṃ prāṇarodhāt savyād vāmā na nāḍī pravahati sahasā vāmakakṣā ca savyā' māsārddhenāpyariṣṭaṃ harati maraṇadaṃ yogayuktaśca yogī pādau vajrāsanasthau dhṛtakarakamalau pṛṣṭhaśūlasya nāśam || 112 ||
idānīmariṣṭavañcanāya nāḍībandhakaraṇamucyate kakṣādityādinā - iha yadā yogī madhyamāyāmahorātraṃ prāṇaṃ praveṣṇaṃ śaknoti, tadā vāmāriṣṭe dakṣiṇe sañcāraṃ prāṇasya karoti, dakṣiṇāriṣṭe ca vāme sañcāraṃ karoti, māsārddhaṃ māsamekaṃ yāvaditi |
anenoktena kāraṇena kakṣāt savyāvasavyāt stanamapi balavat pīḍitaṃ prāṇarodhāt |
savye stane pīḍite na vāmanāḍī vahati, vāmakakṣāt pīḍite na savyā nāḍī vahati; evaṃ māsārddhenāpyariṣṭaṃ harati maraṇadaṃ yogayuktaśca yogīti, yogaḥ pūrakarecakayorekatā kumbhaka iti, tena yukto yogayukta iti; haratītyapanayatītyakāla- maraṇavañcanāniyamaḥ |
nātyūrdhvaṃ ;
ga. nāstyūrdhvaṃ ;

prāṇamevoddhataṃ cākuñcanīyaṃ |
paṭale ] rasāyanādibālatantramahoddeśaḥ idānī pṛṣṭhaśūlanāśakaraṇamucyate- pādau vajrāsanastha iti vajrāsanaṃ savyorumūrdhni vāmapādaṃ vāmorumūrdhni dakṣiṇapādama, to pādau pṛṣṭhasthabāhuvajrabandhena vāmakareṇa dakṣiṇapādo dhṛto dakṣiṇena vāmapādaḥ, tau pādau dhṛtakarakamalau pṛṣṭhaśūlasya nāśaṃ kuruta iti |
uddhava pādau śiro'dho vyapaharati tanau śleṣmarogaṃ samastaṃ prasrāvaḥ prāṇarodhāt katipayadivasairmūtrakṛcchraṃ nihanti |
pratyūṣe'nāmikābhyāṃ jalavigatamukhe dantapaṃkti praghṛṣya netrāṇāmañjanaṃ syānnayanarujaharaṃ tāḍanaṃ vā jalena || 113 ||
ūdhava pādau śiro'dho vyapaharati tanau śleṣmarogaṃ samastamiti |
prasrāvaḥ prāṇarodhāt katipayadivasaimaṃtrakṛtsnaṃ nihanti |
pratyūṣe anāmikābhyāṃ jalavigata- mukhe'prakṣālite dantapaṃkti pragharṣaṇa ghṛṣṭvā netrāṇāmañjanaṃ syānnayana rujahara bhavati |
pratyūṣe tāḍanaṃ vā jalena netrayo rujaharaṃ bhavatīti |
supto nottāra nābhid dhṛ takarakamalā'jīrṇaśūlaṃ nihanti gaṇḍānāmudbhave vai saha ghṛtalavaṇaiḥ svedanaṃ vṛddhināśaḥ arkakṣīrapralepastvatha bhavati kadācid dantakīṭaḥ saśūlo vyāghrībījasya dhūmo dhṛtamiha nalikādyaiśca kīṭādināśaḥ || 114 ||
supto nottāra nābhirvāmakaraṇe dhṛtā yā sā dhṛtaka rakamalā'jīrṇaśūlaṃ nihanti prāṇāyāmasahiteti |
gaṇḍānāmudbhava iti gaṇḍapiṭakādīnāmudbhavakāle saha ghṛtalavaṇai- vastrabaddhaḥ svedanaṃ vṛddhināśa eva |
akaṃkṣīrapralepastvatha gaṇḍādīnāmudbhava iti niyamaḥ; kadācid dantakoṭaḥ saśulo bhavati, tadā vyāghrībījadhūmo dhṛto nalikādyaiḥ koṭāvināśa eva |
vyāghrīti kaṇṭha kārīti sāmānya rogopaśamananiyamaḥ |
harati idānīṃ kuṣṭharogopaśamanāya pratividhānamucyate varṣetyādinā - varṣārddha śvetakuṣṭhaṃ harati varatanau mantriṇāṃ kiṃ tadanyat prajñāsa su cittaṃ suli tamapi sadā prāṇavāyonirodhāt |
saptatyabdāṃ jarāṃ vai sapalitāṃ ca dvivarṣaprapūrṇe mudrāsiddhi tadūrdhvaṃ bhavati katidinairmāgacittaprasaṅgāt || 115 ||
aso mahāyogaḥ pūrvadhūmādinimittena prāṇāyātmana sādhito'kṣarasukhakṣaṇaḥ, bodhicittaṃ mudrāsaṅgena skhalu yitvā tadeva bodhicittaṃ mudrāyogena skhalitaṃ samu khaṃ prāṇāpānayonirodhāt varṣārddha śvetakuṣṭhaṃ harati varatanau mantriṇāṃ ki tadanyat |
yadi kuṣṭhaṃ na harati tadā ṣoḍaśābdikāṃ mudrāṃ sevayati yogī pratyahaṃ māṃsabhojanamadyapānam, vimalaprabhāyāṃ [adhyātma- tasya ṣaḍamāsābhyantareṇa yonimanthāne bodhicittaṃ skhalitaṃ vidhṛtaṃ sat kuṣṭharogaṃ harati, kumbhakayogena, atra nāsti sandehastathāgatavacane |
na kevalaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ harati, api tu saptatyabdāṃ jarāṃ vai sapalitāṃ dvivarṣaprapūrṇe mudrāsaṅge bodhicittaṃ skhalitamiti |
anenaiva yogena yoginaḥ katipayadivasaiḥ kālacakratrivarṣatripakṣa dinaistadūrdhvamiti jarāvināśordhvaṃ mudrāsiddhirbhavati mārgacittaprasaṅgāditi kuṣṭhopaśamananiyamaḥ |
yacchando hṛtpradeśe bhavati varanṛṇāṃ śrūyate śrotrarandha- stasmādūrdhvaṃ hi muṣṭī vra jati samarasaṃ tyājitaṃ caikabhūtam |
yacchabdaṃ jīvaloke bhavati ca balajaṃ tattu deva śṛṇoti vijñānaṃ caiva dūrā śravaṇamapi vibho yo ginā bhāvanīyam * | | 116 ||
kṛtvā paryaṅkabandhaṃ vikasitavadano'nyo'nyadantaṃ spṛśenna ākṛṣṭo bāhyavātastvamṛtarasasamo nābhicakre praviṣṭaḥ |
santāpaṃ kṣutpipāsāṃ harati varatanau sanniruddho viṣaṃ ca śveto bindurlalāṭe su raparikarito muñcamāno'mṛtaṃ vā || 117 ||
ghrāṇe randhradvayena tvapi vi hitamukhe bāhyavātaḥ samastaḥ prāṇenākṛṣya vegāt taḍidanalanibho ghaṭṭito'pānavāyuḥ |
kāle nābhyāṃ sa yogād vrajati samarasaṃ candrasūryāgnimadhye annādyāṃ kṣutpipāsāṃ harati varatanau cāmaratvaṃ dadāti || 118 ||
* taḥ paryantaṃ ṣaṭślokānāmanuvādo bhoṭānuvāde nopalabhyate; teṣāmupari vimalaprabhāvyākhyā ca saṃskṛtapratīṣu bhoṭānuvādeṣu vā nopalabhyate |
atha caitadviṣaye ācārya-khesa-ḍūba-je tatpūrvavattibhiḥ bu-ton — mī - pham - rinpoche- ādi -ācāryairapi sva-sva- vyākhyāne'tra śaṅkā notthāpitā, atastaiḥ maunamevācaritam |
sambhāvaye tatra hetuḥ tathāvidhasaṃskṛtapratīnāṃ bhoṭadeśe aprāptiḥ yatraṣāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ sanniveśaḥ syāt |
asmin viṣaye prāptasaṃskṛtapratīnāmapi dvidhā sthitiḥ - kutracidupalabdhiḥ ṣaṇṇāṃ kutracinneti |
prastutasaṃskaraṇasyādhārabhūtāyāṃ pratau yathā ṣaṭślokā upalabdhā dṛśyante etādṛśā eva ca anyā api dvi-tri-pratayaḥ santyeva ḍā0 lokeśacandrasaṃrakṣita - prācīnagranthasaṃgrahālaye |
kintu bihārarājye paṭanāsthita kāśīprasādajāyasavāla -anusandhāna-saṃsthāna - saṃrakṣitāyāṃ purātanyāṃ vibhūticandralikhitāyāṃ pratau ślokā ete naiva prāpyante, syustathāvidhā anyā api kāścit pratayaḥ |
etasyāṃ sthitau aprāptivaśādeva bhoṭānuvādo bhoṭavidvad- bhirna kṛtaḥ syāt |
paṭale]
rasāyanādibālatantramahoddeśaḥ
svacchāyāmātapasthāmaparamukharaveḥ stabdhadṛṣṭyāvalokya paścād vyomābhivīkṣye t samarasapuruṣo dṛśyate dhūmravarṇaḥ
ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogādava nigatanidhi darśaye cchidrabhūmi vṛkṣacchāyāṃ praviśyattatha gaganatale bhāvitā bindumālā || 119 ||
yā śaktirnābhimadhyād vrajati pa rapadaṃ dvādaśāntaṃ kalāntaṃ sā nābhau sanniruddhā tadidananamitā daṇḍarūpotthitā vā
cakrāccakrāntaraṃ vai mṛdulalitagatindhāritā madhyanāḍyāṃ yāvaccoṣṇīṣarandhraṃ spṛśati haṭhatayā sūcibaddhā hyaca || 120 ||
dvā āpānaṃ tatra kāle paramahaṭhatayā prerayedūdūrdhvamārge uṣṇīṣaṃ bhedayitvā vrajati pa puraṃ vāyuyugme niruddhe
evaṃ vajraprabodhāt satanu manasi sa viṣayā khecaratvaṃ prayāti pañcābhijñāsvabhāvā bhavati punariyaṃ yogināṃ viśvamātā || 121 ||
idānīṃ prāṇāyāmaniyama ucyate- prāṇāyāmaṃ prakuryād hṛdi śirasi tathā yāvadagnirvyathā'bhūt tasmādūrdhvaṃ hi mūrcchā vrajati sukamale'yantrito vā balena
uṣṇīṣaṃ bhedayitvā vrajati parapuraṃ skandhadhātūn vihāya mudrāsaṅgapraṇaṣṭo na hi sukhaphalado janmanīhaiva puṃsām || 122 ||
iha śarīre prāṇāyāmaṃ kumbhakaṃ kuryāt mantrī hṛdi śirasi tathā yāvadagnirvyathā- bhūt tāvad hṛdayaṃ dahyate, śirasi ca vyathā bhavati
tata ūrdhvaṃ na kuryāditi niyamaḥ, yadi karoti tasmādūrdhvaṃ hi mūccha vrajati sukamale, nābhikamale prāṇo mūrcchā vrajatīti
ayantrito vā balenoṣṇīṣaṃ bhedayitvā vrajati hi maraṇaṃ skandhadhātūn vihāya yataḥ, ato mudrāsaṅgapraṇaṣṭo na hi sukhaphaladaḥ prāṇāyāmo janmanīhaiva puṃsāmiti prāṇāyāma- niyamaḥ
mudroktā bhāvānārthaṃ dinaniśisamaye neva rāgakṣayārthaṃ vāgvajraṃ tarpaṇārthaṃ na khalu madakaraṃ mantriṇāmuktamevam
ato'tyadhikametad vicāraṇīyaṃ yat saṃskṛtapratiṣu tatra satsvapi ṣaṭślokeṣu teṣāṃ vyākhyā kathaṃ na labhyata iti
asmin viṣaye anekāvidhāḥ sambhāvanāḥ samuditāḥ bhaveyuḥ
etat sarvaṃ tathyajātamādhṛtya ālocanīyaṃ bhūmikāyām, tadatra viramyate
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [adhyātma- sarvāhāraḥ sukhārthaṃ pratidinasamaye'jīrṇahetorna coktaḥ śrīcaryāsiddhito maṇamapi cittau (tau) kīlanārthaṃ na rātrī || 123 ||
[ idānīṃ yogināṃ mudrāsvādanāhāracāraniyama ucyate mudreti- iha bhagavatā mudroktā yogamudroktā bhāvanārtham, naivarāgakṣayārthaṃ hetoḥ dinaniśi- samaye strīkrīḍām; tathā madyapānaṃ kiñcit mukhatarpaṇārtham, na kalaśādivat
na hi madaḥ yogīndrāṇāṃ mokṣadaḥ bhavati; tathā āhārakaraṇamatra sarvāhāraḥ sukhārtham, yat kiñcidā- hārakaraṇaṃ tat sarvaṃ svalpasukhāya bhavati
tena pratidinasamaye'jīrṇahetorna karttavya ityāhāraniyamaḥ
śrīcaryā yogacaryā siddhihetoriti siddherhetoḥ
tena śmaśānopaśma- śānādiṣu bhramaṇaṃ rātrau na krīḍārthamityarthaḥ
yogacaryāniyamaḥ

rNal ḥByor Gyi sPyod Paḥi Nes Paḥo (T 324, 3-4)
asya ślokasya vyākhyā saṃskṛtapratiṣu nopalabhyate, kintu bhoṭānuvāde sā labdhā, atra ko heturiti vivecayatā ācārya - khes ḍraba-je mahābhāgairuktaṃ yat vyākhyāyāḥ viśuddha- bhāratīya saṃskṛtapratiṣu 'idānīṃ yogināṃ mudrāsvādanāhāracāraniyama ucyate iti subodham' iti mātraṃ labhyate eva
so'pyaṃśo mayā saṃskṛtapratiṣu na dṛṣṭaḥ
atra khes-ḍraba-je-mahābhāgānāṃ svamatamullekhanīyamasti
tena uktaṃ yad anye'pi paṭale ] rasāyanādibālatantramahoddeśaḥ idānīmavadhūtayogibhaiṣajyamucyate- akṣobhyaṃ kiñciduṣṇaṃ mukharujaśamanaṃ dantaśūlasya caiva pratyūṣe'kṣobhyanasyaṃ śirasi rujaharaṃ toyanasyaṃ tathaiva
karṇe netraṃ (tre) praviṣṭaṃ tva ( hya) bhayarujaharaṃ mūtramuṣṇaṃ ca śītaṃ bhūtārte'kṣobhyanasyaṃ trikaṭukasahitaṃ saukhyadaṃ cāpi daṣṭe || 124||
iha yadā yogino mukharogo bhavati, akṣobhyaṃ kiñciduṣṇaṃ kṛtvā mukhe dhṛtaṃ mukhara- (ru) jasa (śa) manaṃ bhavati
dantaśūlasya caiveti cakārā [ d dantakīṭasya ca
yadā śirasi rogo bhavati, tadā pratyūṣe akṣobhyaṃ nasyaṃ kṛtaṃ śirasi rujaharaṃ bhavati
toyanasyaṃ tathaiva yogināmiti
yadā karṇarogo bhavati, tadā mūtramuṣṇaṃ kṛtaṃ karṇe praviṣṭaṃ netraroge śītaṃ kṛtaṃ netre praviṣṭaṃ yathāsaṃkhyamubhayarujaharam, mūtramuṣṇaṃ ca śītamiti
bhūtārtta iti bhūtapretādigraste akṣobhyanasyaṃ trikaṭukena sahitaṃ saukhyadaṃ cāpi daṣṭe, sarpadaṣṭe'pi saukhyadaṃ bhavatīti mukharogādyupasa (śa) mananiyamaḥ
viṇmūtraṃ śukraraktaṃ nṛpata (la) lasahitaṃ bhakṣitaṃ cāyudaṃ syāt sadhyānaṃ puṣpanasyaṃ harati sapalitānaṅgajātān jarāṃśca
bhuktaṃ pañcapradīpaṃ sakalarujaharaṃ makṣikācchardimiśraṃ strīpuṣpaṃ śukramiśraṃ tvapaharati rujaṃ bhakṣitaṃ varṣaṃyogāt || 125 ||
idānīṃ pañcāmṛtayoga āyuvṛddhyarthamucyate viḍityādinā - iha 'yathā bāhye tathā dehe', ( pṛ) iti vacanāt bāhye pañcadravyāṇi, adhyātmani pañcadravyāṇyekīkṛtya pañcāmṛtayogaḥ, tataḥ pañcāmṛtaṃ bhakṣitaṃ yogināmāyudaṃ syāditi
bhoṭācārya śrīpaṅlo-prabhṛtibhirapi svīkriyate yat bhoṭānuvāde upalabdho'yaṃ vyākhyāṃśaḥ mūlasaṃskṛtasya nāsti, api tu kenacid bhoṭadeśīyena viduṣā bhoṭānuvāde ayamaṃśa āropitaḥ

T 325 vimalaprabhāyāṃ [adhyātma- bāhye viṭśabdena vairocanirvalirvalervaśād gandhako viḍucyate, adhyātmani viḍeva, anayo- stulyabhāgaḥ | mūtraṃ bāhye viṣṇu nṛpo bhi (bhṛ) ṅgarājarasam, adhyātmani mūtrameva | raktaṃ bāhye abhrakam, adhyātmani strīpuṣpam, tulyabhāgamiti | śukra bāhye pāradaḥ, adhyātmani śukrameva, anayoḥ samabhāgamiti | nṛmāṃsaṃ bāhye triphalā, adhyātmani majjā, anayorapi samabhāgamiti | evaṃ viḍekabhāgaḥ, māṃsasya pādonabhāgaḥ, raktārddhabhāgaḥ, śukrasya ekapādaḥ, etadekīkṛtya mūtreṇa saptavārān bhāvayet
ātape bhūyo bhūyaḥ śoṣayitvā tataḥ pratyahaṃ karṣamātraṃ ghṛta-madhubhyāṃ bhakṣitaṃ ṣaṇmāsāvadherāyudaṃ bhavati, śākāmlatailalavaṇavarjanāditi pañcāmṛtaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ puṣpanasya ucyate sadhyānamityādinā- iha dhyānaṃ madhyamāyāṃ prāṇapraveśa:, tena dhyānena saha sadhyānamiti
puṣpaṃ strīrajaḥ, bāhye keśarājikā bhṛṅgarājaḥ, tasya rasaḥ strīpuṣpatu lyaṃ sadhyānaṃ puṣpanasyaṃ sadhyānānāṃ harati sapalitānaṅgajātān jarāṃśca ṣaṇmāsāvadheriti [ nasya ] ' niyamaḥ

iha prathama koṣṭhapatitadravyasya netramiti dvau bhāgau, dvitīye induriti eko bhāgaḥ, tṛtīye'gniriti trayaḥ, caturthe'bdhi riti catvāraḥ, pañcame bāṇā iti pañca, ṣaṣṭhe guṇā iti trayaḥ, saptame jaladhiriti catvāraḥ, aṣṭame śarā iti pañca, navame hasta iti dvau daśame indurityekaḥ, ekādaśame iṣuriti pañca, dvādaśe netramiti dvau trayodaśe candra ityekaḥ, caturdaśe agniriti trayaḥ, pañcadaśe abdhiriti catvāraḥ, ṣoḍaśe indurityekaḥ, saptadaśe kāla iti trayaḥ, aṣṭādaśe yuga iti catvāraḥ, ekonaviṃśatime śara iti pañca viṃśatime nayana iti dvau, ekaviṃśatime abdhiriti catvāraḥ, vimalaprabhāyāṃ iṣuriti pañca, trayoviṃśatime netra iti dvau caturviṃśatime indurityekaḥ, pañcaviṃśatime loka iti trayaḥ

eṣāṃ pure koṣṭhe pātanīyaṃ krameṇa - jātīphalakoṣṭhe lākṣā, elākoṣṭhe sarjarasam, latākoṣṭhe duḥkha- miti śrīvāsam dalakoṣṭhe puramiti gurgu lam, kakkolakoṣṭhe sitamiti kundu- rukam
evaṃ dhūpakāryeṣu dhūpam, pañcadravyaiḥ pūrvoktabhāgaiḥ kuryāditi niyamaḥ
karpūrakhaṇḍaiḥ saha yatra khaṇḍam, tatra madhu deyaṃ khaṇḍena sārddhaṃ vahnibhāgairnakhaiśca sārddham
evamaṣṭādaśa bhāgaiḥ ṣaḍaṅgo dhūpo bhavati, karpūrakhaṇḍamadhukastūrikāsahito daśāṅga iti dhūpakakṣapuṭe dravyaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ dhūpavatarucyate piṣṭaṃ tadityādi- iha dhūpakakṣapuṭoktaṃ madhūkavirahitaṃ gandhodakena kiñcit khaṇḍamiśreṇa piṣṭvā dhūparvāta tena kuryānnārācākārām, dhūpanāya vastrādikaṃ saddharmapratimārthamiti niyamaḥ
nābhyādau siṃhamūtre śaśigaganapure pātayet snānayoge granthi vyāghraṃ hareṇuṃ hatamapi ca tathā śaṅkhatvaktrivibhāgāḥ

idānīṃ snānakakṣapuṭa ucyate nābhyādāvityādinā - iha pūrvakakṣapuṭapātitadravyāṇāṃ madhye catvāri dravyāṇi nābhyādīnyuddhṛtya teṣāṃ koṣṭheṣu yathāsaṃkhyaṃ snānakakṣapuṭe'nyāni deyānīti
tatra nābhikoṣṭhe granthiparṇaṃ pātayet, paṭale ] rasāyanādibālatantramahoddeśaḥ siṃhamūtra koṣṭhe vyāghraṃ nakham, śaśipure hareṇum, gaganapure hatamiti kacorakam
api ca, tathā yathā dhūpakārye saṃkha miti nakhaṃ tasya tribhāgam, evaṃ tvagvibhāgāstrayo deyāḥ, snānayoge snānadravyāṇāṃ madhye
evamaṣṭādaśabhāge ṣaḍdravyaiḥ snānaṃ bhavati
snānakakṣapuḍhe dravyaniyamaḥ

dhānyamiti kastumburum, murvīti maruvakam, śatāhveti śatapuṣpā, damamiti damanakam madhuro eṣāṃ pañcānāṃ trayo bhāgāḥ - evamaṣṭādaśabhāgenodvartanaṃ bhavati
udvartana- dravyaniyamaḥ

tailakakṣapuṭadravyaniyamaḥ
evaṃ cūrṇādikam - cūrṇe granthi ca tadvad bhavati tanuhata pānavāse mukhe ca tvagvolaṃ granthiśaṅkhaṃ phaladalapuṭapāke ca haṃsādike ca
evaṃ triśatprabhedeḥ suracitavividhān gandhadhūpādiyogān kuryād dravyaiviśuddheḥ phalapuṭapacitairvāsitairvedhitaiśca // 139 //
cūrṇe cūrṇaviṣaye granthi ca tadvaditi nakhavatpañcadravyeṣu deyaṃ granthiparṇakam
tatra hataṃ pānavāse deyaṃ bhavati, mukhavāse ca tvagvolaṃ granthiśaṅkhamiti dravyacatuṣṭayasya bhāgatrayaṃ phalapāke dalapuṭapāke haṃsapāke ādito dolāyā pā ke vakṣyamāṇe deyamiti

viśuddhaiḥ phalapuṭapacitai- rvāsitervedhitairiti dravyasaṃgraha niyamaḥ
idānīṃ gandhasya dhūpapāka ucyate aṣṭāṃśādāvityādi- aṣṭāṃśādau kaṣāyo bhavati dalavaśāt tadviguṇyogradhūpaḥ paścād dravyapramāṇo guḍa iti ca bhaved varddhate grīṣmayogāt
pādāṃśaṃ śaṅkhadhūpaṃ madhukamapi sitāṃ nirdahed dravyatulyāṃ piṇḍaṃ śaṅkhapramāṇaṃ malayalaghucalaṃ candrayuktaṃ ca tadvat // 140 //
iha gandhayoge trividhaṃ dalam - adhamamadhyamottamam
tatrādhamaṃ mustakam, se (śai)- leyam, uśīrakam, vā (pā) lakam, kapittham, vilvam, murā, māṃsīti; etāni dalānya- dhamāni
eṣāṃ vaśād dalavaśādādau kaṣāyo guḍena moditaṃ harītakīcūrṇaṃ dhūpo bhavati;
tena dalamaṣṭāṃśenādau dhūpayed dinamekam, tataḥ puṣpavāsaṃ kṛtvā dinadvayam, tṛtīye dine madhyamadalaṃ miśrayet tatastadviguṇyogradhūpa iti
tasya pūrvāparasya dviguṇya ugradhūpa iti
lākṣām, sarjjarasam, śrīvāsam, ra gugguluḥ, kundurukam - ebhiḥ pañcāgrairguḍena moditairugradhūpo bhavati;
kaṣāyasya dviguṇyo deyaḥ, dalasya pādāṃśamiti
atra madhyamadalaṃ puṣpavargam, candanam, agurum phalavargam, nakham tvagvargam, niryāsa vargamati
evaṃ dinadvayam ugradhūpena dhūpayet; ekāntaritaṃ dinadvayaṃ puṣpavāsaṃ kuryāt, paścād dravyapramāṇaṃ guḍam api ca dhūpena sārddhaṃ nirdahet grīṣme;
tato varddhate grīṣmayogāt, vārṣe dravyadviguṇyaḥ, hemante triguṇyo deyaḥ, tato guḍe dagdhe sati śaṅkhamiti nakhaṃ guḍena sārddhaṃ pādāṃśaṃ nirdaheda dinadvayaṃ pūrvavidhinā
tato madhukaṃ sitāṃ sa ( rā ) rkarāṃ dravyatulyāṃ nirdahet piṇḍadhūpena sārddham
dhūpagrāsasyādyāvasāne kevalaṃ nirdahet
evaṃ guḍo'pi pratidinaṃ grāsatrayaṃ dagdhvā viśrāmayet, pratidinaṃ dravyasyāṣṭāṃsaṃ ( śaṃ) dhūpaṃ nirdahet, anyathā'nenāpakvo bhavati, adhikena dagdhagandho bhavati, apakve amlo bhavatīti niyamaḥ
piṇḍamiti piṇḍadhūpam, kakṣapuṭoktaṃ karpūrasahitam ugradravyavarjitam, pañcadravyai- madhusa (śa) kaṃrāmoditaiḥ piṇḍadhūpam, tadeva saṃkha ( śaṅkha) pramāṇamiti dravyapādāṃśaṃ nirdahet

tadvaditi piṇḍadhūpadravyasadṛśaṃ nirdahed madhusa (śa) kairāsahitam
atra guḍo vaṭikāguḍo grāhyaḥ, na dravyapūrvaka iti niyamaḥ
pakvaṃ gandhaṃ supuṣpaiḥ katipayadivasaṃ vyāsayed yāvadiṣṭaṃ paścād vedhaṃ śatāṃśaṃ triphalaśaśimadeḥ kārayet sāsavaiśca

atha kharapākena gandhe dagdhe sati niśāyāṃ śaśāṅkakiraṇaiḥ spṛśed gandham; tadabhāve jalatīre sthāpayed yāvad dagdhadoṣopasa (śa) mo bhavati

idānīṃ puṣpatailārthaṃ gandhatailāya ca tilaśuddhirujya (cya) te kṛsvetyādi - kṛtvā śuddhi tilānāṃ kvathita dalajalairdhūpalepādibhiśca paścājjātyādipuṣpaiḥ katipayadivasaṃ vāsayed yāvadiṣṭam

// 145 // iha tilān paripakkān navān saṃgṛhya kvathitadalajalairiti dalānyāmrapatrāṇi, evaṃ jambū - kapittha- mātuluṅga - vilvānāṃ pañcavṛkṣāṇāṃ patrāṇi, taiḥ kathitaṃ jalaṃ tairdalajalairmardayitvā tilānāṃ tuṣā panayanaṃ prathamaśuddhiḥ
tato jālāyantropari vastraṃ datvā tadupari tilāna- parabhāṇḍe pihitvā dhūpayet, dvitīyā śuddhiḥ
lepādibhiriti triphalairlepo deyo gandhatoyena piṣṭaiḥ, āditaḥ sūryaraśmibhiḥ śoṣayet
evaṃ tilānāṃ śuddhi kṛtvā pañcā (ścā) jjātyā- dipuṣpaiḥ katipayadivasaṃ pakṣaṃ vā daśadinaṃ vā nirantaraṃ vāsayed yāvanmaditānāṃ vāsita- puṣpagandhamudvahati, tata iṣṭaṃ vāsanaṃ bhavati
tataḥ kolu (lhu) kayantreṇa tailaṃ gṛhītvā nṛpa ityāmantraṇam, nipuṇatayā kācabhāṇḍe sthāpayet
tat tailaṃ snāne vā'bhyaṅgane vā bhavati madakaraṃ puṣpatailaṃ hyapakvamiti
atha pakatailaṃ kartukāmaḥ, tadā tadeva tailaṃ samatoyena sugandhena kāthayed yāvat tailaṃ phenaṃ muñcati
tato'vatāraṇakāle aṣṭāṃśena gandhadravyaṃ gandhodakena piṣṭṭā deyam, paścādavatārayed yāvat śītalaṃ bhavati
tataḥ kastūrikādyairvedhaṃ datvā kācabhāṇḍe sthāpayet
tade va madāsavena pādāṃśena miśritaṃ lākṣābhāṇḍe rasāyanādibālatantramahoddeśaḥ sūyaṃtāpaiḥ saptāhaṃ taptaṃ kastūrikātailaṃ bhavati

idānīṃ gurviṇīnāṃ prasavanārthaṃ sarvataścatustriśatikaṃ yantramucyate bhūbhūdityādinā - bhūbhṛtsūryendumanvakṣi madanavasavo rudrarājāgnayaśca digbhūtā randhraṣaṭkaṃ tithijalanidhayaḥ sthāpanīyāśca koṣṭhe
saṃkhyākoṣṭhaiścaturbhirjalanidhiśikhino lekhayitvā samastaṃ śrīcakraṃ mānapṛṣṭhe prasavanasamaye darśayed gurviṇīnām || 146 ||
iha yadā gurviṇīnāṃ prasavanakāle garbhastambhanaṃ bhavati bāhyadūtīdoṣeṇa tadā idaṃ yantraṃ mānapṛṣṭhe likhet; mānamityāḍhakam
tasya pṛṣṭhe ṣoḍaśakoṣṭhakān kṛtvā prathamakoṣṭhe bhūbhṛt sapta, dvitīye sūrya dvādaśa, tṛtīye indurityekam, caturthe manuścaturdaśa, pañcame'kṣi hau, ṣaṣṭhe madaneti trayodaśa, saptame vasavo'ṣṭau, aṣṭame rudra ekādaśa, navame rājānaḥ ṣoḍaśa, daśame'gnaya iti trayaḥ, ekādaśe digiti daśa, dvādaśe bhūtā iti pañca, trayodaśe randhrā iti nava, caturdaśe ṣaṭkamiti ṣaṭ pañcadaśe tithiriti pañcadaśa, ṣoḍaśe jalanidhaya iti catvāraḥ; sthāpanīyāścara koṣṭhe ṣoḍaśe
eṣāmaṅkānāṃ catuḥ koṣṭhe sthitānāṃ saṃkhyā ekapiṇḍitaṃ jalanidhiśikhina iti catustriṃśat sarvatra
etad yantraṃ likhitvā samastaṃ śrīcakraṃ mānapṛṣṭhe prasavanasamaye darśayed gurvigonāmiti garbhamocananiyamaḥ
idānīṃ garbhādibālatantramucyate yoginya ityādi- yoginyo'ṣṭāṣṭakā yāḥ prakaṭamahitale mātaro yāḥ prasiddhā garbhākhyā vāsarākhyā triguṇanavadaśaikādaśānyā stripañca
māsākhyā vatsarākhyā sakalabhuvitale tāḥ pragṛhṇanti bālaṃ garbhe śūlaṃ ca pīḍāṃ prasavanasamaye'pyeva kurvanti yonau || 147 ||
iha mahītale yāḥ prakaṭāḥ catuḥṣaṣṭiyoginyaḥ, tāstā ( a ) ṣṭāṣṭakā mātaraḥ prasiddhāḥ, tāsāṃ madhye garbhākhyāstriguṇanava iti saptaviṃśatiḥ, vāsarākhyā daśa, ekādaśa māsākhyā:, anyāstriyaṃ ( pa ) ñceti pañcadaśa vatsa rākhyāstāstrayaḥṣaṣṭiḥ bālaṃ gṛhṇanti sakalabhuvitale garbhe śūlaṃ ca pīḍāṃ prasavanasamaye'pyeva kurvanti yonāviti

yaḥ karoti sa kartā, yat kriyate tat karma ; tasya kṛtasya karmaṇaḥ phalabhoktā karmakartā
na ca karmaṇā vinā kartā siddhayati ; yathā kumbhaṃ karotīti kumbhakāraḥ
evaṃ yaḥ karma karoti sa karteti nyāyaḥ
āha- nāpīśaḥ karmakartā, svatantraḥ prayojaka iti
iha yadi karmakartā na bhavati, tadā sarvakartā samantāditi nirarthakam
iha kartā yadi preritaḥ san karma karoti, tadā kartuḥ parādhī- natā
yasya parādhīnatā tasya prayojakaḥ kathaṃ viruddhakarmaṇi kṛte sati nigrahaṃ na karoti; svatantratayā vinā, svatantratā īśvareṇa vyāptā
evaṃ karmaphalābhāvaḥ kartṛvādināṃ siddha:, na caivam; tasmāt kartā na kaścid īśo'śubhaśubhaphalada: prāṇināṃ karma muktveti svakamaṃ- phalopabhogaḥ siddhaḥ kartā[raṃ ] vinā
idānīṃ svatantrasya kartuḥ parāpekṣikatvamucyate pṛthvītyādi - pṛthvītoyāgnivātārṇava iha yadi khe karturādau na santi dravyābhāve na viśvaṃ viṣayavirahitaḥ sarvakartā karoti
na pratyakṣaṃ parokṣaṃ viṣayavirahitasyāsya kartuḥ pramāṇaṃ saṃyogādeva sarvaṃ bhavati narapate necchayā karmarūpam || 169 ||
iha yadi khe ākāśe pṛthivyādiparamāṇavo na santi karturādau tadā dravyābhāve na viśvaṃ karoti
viṣayavirahito niṣkalaḥ, sarvakartā katham ?
asya viṣayavirahitasya kartuḥ sādhakaṃ na pratyakṣaṃ parokṣaṃ pramāṇaṃ yasmāt tasmād dravyasaṃyogādeva sarvaṃ viśvaṃ carācaraṃ bhavati, necchayā kartuḥ karmarūpamiti nyāyaḥ; itīcchāpratiṣedhaḥ kartuḥ
paṭale ] svaparadarśananyāyavicāramahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ pratītyotpāda ucyate saṃyogādityādi- saṃyogādindukānterbhavati ca salilaṃ darpaṇe vastubimbaṃ jihvāśrā (srā) vo'mlahetoḥ svaravata itaraḥ śuddhabojāṅkaraḥ syāt
kāntāccāyaḥ śalākā bhramaṇamapi bhavennecchayā kiñcideṣāṃ vastūnāṃ śaktireṣā tribhuvananilaye nirmitā kenacinna || 170 ||
iha sarvavastūnāṃ saṃyogādutpādaḥ - idaṃ prāpya idamutpadyate
saṃyogāditi candrakiraṇa- saṃyogāccandrakānterbhavati ca salilam cakārāt sūryakānteragnirbhavati
darpaṇe vastusaṃyo- gāt vastupratibimbo bhavati
anyasyāmlabhakṣaṇasaṃyogādanyasya jihvāśrā (skhā)vo bhavati, amlahetoḥ sakāśāditi
kūpādau svaravasaṃyogāt pratiravo bhavati
śuddhaboje'ṅa kuraḥ
sthāt, pṛthvotoyādisaṃyogāditi
kāntāditi kāntapāṣāṇāt ayaḥ sa (śa) lākābhramaṇaṃ bhavati, saṃyogāditi
necchayā kiñcideṣāṃ vastūnāṃ vastu bhavati, kintu vastūnāṃ śaktireṣā
tribhuvananilaye nirmitā kenacinneti pratotyotpādaḥ siddhaḥ
āha-iha kāraṇena vinā kāryaṃ na bhavati yasmāt tasmāt kāraṇamastīti, ata īśvarādikaṃ siddhamiti
āha - iha kāraṇe kāryaṃ yad bhavati, tat kiṃ satkāryam, asad vā ?
kāraṇe satkāryaṃ na bhavati, vidyamānasya ghaṭasya mṛdādayaḥ kāraṇabhūtā na bhavanti, sattvāt asatkāryaṃ na asattvāt, kūrmaromavat, tathā paṭasya [ta]ntu [tu]rīvemādayaḥ kāraṇabhūtā na bhavanti
ubhayātmakaṃ kāryaṃ na bhavati, parasya (paraspara) virodhāt
yat sat tadasanna bhavati, yadasat tat sanna bhavati, virodhāt
ato na satkāryam, nāsatkāryam, na sadasatkāryaṃ ' kāraṇe bhavatīti siddham
āha-iha kāraṇasya pratiṣedhena kāryasyāpi pratiṣedho bhavati; ubhayapratiṣedhāt sarvābhāva iti siddham
āha - iha sarvābhāvo na parāpekṣikatvāditi
iha kāraṇe yat kāraṇatvaṃ tat kāryamapekṣya parikalpyate, kāryaṃ ca kāraṇamapekṣya evaṃ parāpekṣikatvādubhayorapi kāraṇatva- prasaṅgaḥ
ubhayasya kāraṇatvāt kāryābhāvaḥ, tadabhāve kāraṇābhāvaḥ kāraṇasya kāryāpekṣikatvād aniyatatvaprasaṅgaḥ
tasmād aniyatatvād akāraṇatvaprasaṅgaḥ
evaṃ sarveṣāmīśvarādīnāṃ kāraṇānām aniyatatvam akāraṇatvaṃ siddham
āha - nāpekṣikā siddhiḥ kāraṇasya ca yat kāraṇaṃ tat kāraṇameva, yat kāryaṃ tat kāryamiti siddham
bho. Nes Pa Med Pa Nid ( aniyatatva )
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ adhyātma- āha--- iha tavecchātaḥ siddhirna mameti vaiṣamikatvam
yadīcchātaḥ siddhaṃ bhavati, tadā mamāpīcchātaḥ
yat tava' sat tad mamāsat siddham, yuktivivarjitatvāt
āha - āptāgamādasmākaṃ samaya eṣaḥ
samayo'siddhaḥ
samaya iti vaktaṃ na labhyate, uktaṃ śāstravidbhiriti
āha - kadācid yuktirucyate
iha upādānakāraṇāt sarvasambhavābhāvāt śaktasya śakyakaraṇāt satkāryasiddhiriti
āha-iha yuṣmākaṃ heturvṛthā
katham ?
yadi tava pakṣasya (pakṣaḥ) sādhakalakṣaṇaprāptaḥ, tadā mamāpi pakṣaṃ sādhayiṣyati
atha dūṣaṇalakṣaṇaprāptastava pakṣasya (pakṣaḥ), tadā mamāpi pakṣaṃ dūṣayiṣyati, yathāgnirubhayadāhako nāsāvekasyeti
iha yathā śabdavādināṃ pratijñā - nityaḥ śabdaḥ; ko hetuḥ ?
amūrtatvāditi
ko dṛṣṭāntaḥ ?
ākāśavat ; yathā ākāśamamūrtatvānnityam, tathā śabdo'pi yasmāt tasmāt śabdo nityaḥ siddha iti
āha-neyaṃ pratijñā, paro'pi vakṣyati - anityaḥ śabdaḥ
ko hetuḥ ?
kṛtakatvā- diti
ko dṛṣṭāntaḥ ?
ghaṭavat; yathā ghaṭo mṛddaṇḍacakrasūtra puruṣahastavyāyāmāt kṛtakaḥ, tathā śabdo'pi kaṇṭhatālvādibhiḥ prayatnato janito yasmāt tasmādanityaḥ śabdaḥ siddhaḥ
ato hetuvyapadeśamātrataḥ kāryasiddhirna bhavati
yastu yāthātathyaṃ brūyāt, tat pramāṇaṃ syānna hetuvyapadeśata iti
evaṃ heturvṛthā
anyacca; sādhyānāṃ pratijñāvirodhena hetuḥ sādhako na bhavati
iha yasmin kāle pratijñā tasmin kāle heturnāsti; yasmin kāle hetustasmin kāle pratijñā nāsti
atha kasyāsau hetuḥ pratijñayā vinā ayugapaddharmitvāt
yasmin kāle 'pra' kārastasmin kāle na 'ti'kāro 'jñā' kāraśca
evaṃ pakārarephākārāḥ, yathā 'pra'kārasyākṣarākṣarasya
na hyajātena mṛtena vā putreṇa putrakāryaṃ kartuṃ śakyate, evaṃ hetunāpi
tasmāt kāraṇopalambhāt kāryaṃ na bhavati, ahetutaḥ siddhatvāt
ahetuta iti hetuḥ kāraṇamityanarthāntaram
evaṃ na kāraṇe kāryam, nāpyahetutaḥ kāryaṃ bhavati; ataḥ kāryaṃ svato na bhavati, parato na bhavati, ubhayato na bhavati, ahetu [157a]kaṃ na bhavatīti siddhaṃ kartṛkāraṇanityadūṣaṇamiti
idānīmātmano dūṣaṇamucyate yadītyādi- yadyātmā sarvagaḥ syādanubhavati kathaṃ bandhuviśleṣaduḥkhaṃ nityaścāyaṃ yadi syānmadanaśarahato'vasthatāṃ kiṃ prayāti
yadyāsīt sakriyaśca vrajati kathamimāṃ mūḍhatāṃ suptakāle evaṃ vai sarvagaḥ syād vibhurapi ca purā sakriyo'yaṃ na cātmā //
paṭale ] svaparadarśananyāyavicāramahoddeśaḥ iha yadyātmā sarvagaḥ syādanubhavati kathaṃ bandhuviśleṣaduḥkhamiti
iha ya ātmā sarvagaḥ sa eko bhavati, tasya bandhuviśleṣaduḥkhaṃ na bhavatīti
ekasattvasya duḥkhena sarva- sattvānāṃ duḥkhaṃ bhavati, ātmanaḥ sarvagatvāditi
athānekātmānaḥ, tadā anekātmanāṃ sarvagatvābhāva iti
nityaścāyaṃ yadi syād 'madanaśarahato'vasthatāṃ ki prayātīti
iha yo nityastasyāvasthāntaraṃ nāsti, vikārarahitatvāditi, tat kathamimāṃ kāmāvasthāṃ daśa- vidhāṃ madanaśarahato gacchatīti; tasmādātmanā'nityena bhavitavyam vikārasaṃyogāditi
yadyāsīt sakriyaśca vrajati kathamimāṃ mūḍhatāṃ suptakāle iti
iha yadyāsīt kāle jāgradavasthā lakṣaṇo sakriyaḥ, cakārānnityaśca tadā suptakāle mūḍhatāṃ kriyārahitaḥ (tatāṃ) kathaṃ vrajatīti
evaṃ vai ekāntaṃ vicāryamāṇaḥ sarvagaḥ syānna vibhurapi ca svāmī nityo na sakriyo yanna cātmā iti siddham
idānīṃ buddhabhagavataḥ pravacanamucyate nāstyātmetyādi- nāstyātmā sambhavo vāstyaśubhaśubhaphalaṃ cāsti kartrā vihīnaṃ gantā nāstyasti mokṣāya gamanamakhilaṃ cāsti bandho na badhyaḥ
bhāvo'bhāvo'pi cāsti kṣaṇikavirahito niḥsvabhāvo bhavo'sti etanme satyavākyaṃ satyavākyaṃ suraphaṇivacane saṃgrahairhanyate na //
iha pratītyasamutpannadharmāṇāṃ nirodhādutpāda utpādānnirodhaḥ
evaṃ nirodhadharmāṇā- mātmā nāsti, ātmī [157b]yābhāvāt
utpādadharmāṇāṃ sambhavo'sti, punarjanmagrahaṇāt
svādhyāyādidṛṣṭāntaireṣāṃ siddhiriti vakṣyamāṇe vaktavyā
aśubhaśubhaphalaṃ cāstīti utpādadharmāṇāṃ śubhāśubhaphalamasti, nirodhadharmāṇāmabhāvena
kartrā vihīnaṃ kartrā vinetyarthaḥ
gantā nāsti nirodhadharmasamūhaḥ, mokṣāya ca gamanamasti, anyeṣāmanyat tadrūpam ” ityādivacanāt akhilaṃ samastam
asti bandho na badhya iti, ihotpādadharmāṇāṃ bandho'sti, badhyo nirodhadharmo (dharmāṇāṃ ) ra nāsti
bhāvo'bhāvo'pi cāsti kṣaṇika- birahito niḥsvabhāvo bhavo'stīti
iha bhāvābhāvai kalolībhūto niḥsvabhāvo dravyavikalpa- rahitaḥ pratisenātulyaḥ kṣaṇikavirahita utpādavyayarahito bhāvo buddhānāṃ dharmacakrapravartanā- yāstīti
etanme sarvagrahavinirmuktaṃ vacanaṃ yat tat suraphaṇivacanaiḥ saṃgrahairhanyate na
iha yathā grahagrasto mallo grahamuktamallaṃ hantuṃ na śaknoti tathā vikalpagrahagrasto (stā ) vikalpagrahamuktaṃ hantuṃ na śaknuvantoti nairātmyādisiddhiḥ saṃkṣepeṇātroktā, vistaro vistarā- gamena jñeya iti niyamaḥ
idānīṃ vaibhāṣika-sautrāntika- yogācāra - matadūṣaṇamucyate yastatvamityādi- yastattvaṃ pudgalākhyaṃ vadati tanugataṃ tatsvabhāvāt sa naṣṭa: saṃvṛtyā cārthavādī tvaviditaparamārtho hyasanmanyamānaḥ
bho. bGog Pahi Chos rNam La (nirodhadharmāṇām )
vimalaprabhāyāṃ vijñānaṃ manyamānastribhuvanasakalaṃ caiva vijñānavādī yo'naṣṭo naṣṭapakṣaḥ sa bhavati karuṇāśūnyatādvaitavādī // 173 //
iha tīrthika bauddhānāmeṣāṃ pakṣagrahaḥ, tena svapakṣagraheṇa parapakṣasyāpi grahaṇaṃ bhavati, taddharmeṇa tadvaidharmyeṇa vā teṣāṃ bālamatīnām

iti

vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ adhyātma- idānīṃ pūrvakarmopabhogavartamānakarmasañcayapratiṣedha ucyate janturityādi- jantuḥ pūrvāṇi karmāṇyanubhavati kṛtānye hikānyanyajātyā yadyevaṃ karmanāśo na hi bhavati nṛṇāṃ jātijātyantareṇa, saṃsārānnirgamaḥ syādaparimitabhavairnaiva mokṣapraveśa etad ve tāyināṃ tu prabhavati hi mataṃ cānyajātiprahīṇam ||| 174 | iha yeṣāṃ mataṃ jantuḥ pūrvakṛtāni karmāṇi bhuṅkte iha janmani kṛtānyanyajātyā- miti; yadyevaṃ tadā karmanāśo na hi bhavati nṛṇāṃ jātijātyantareṇa, karmaphalopabhogata iti
evaṃ na saṃsārānnirgamaḥ syādaparimitabhavairnaiva mokṣe praveśo bhavatīti
etad vai tāyi (ji) nāṃ prabhavati hi matam, kintu anyajātiprahoṇamiti tāyi (ji) nāṃ mlecchānāṃ matam

idānīṃ cārvāka matadūṣaṇamucyate bhūtairityādi- bhūtairyakabhūtaiḥ prabhavati madirāśaktivat sākṣicittaṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ kinna hi syāt kṣitijalahutabhugmārutākāśayogāt
nāstyeṣāṃ jantuśaktistvatha paramamṛṣā bhūtasaṃyogaśakti- retaccārvākavākyaṃ na hi sukhaphaladaṃ mārganaṣṭaṃ narāṇām ||175|| iha pūrvoktairbhūtaiḥ pṛthvyādibhirekībhūtairyadi harītakīguḍadhātako saṃyogena madirā- śaktivat sendriyaṃ cittaṃ narāṇāmiti siddham, tadā vṛkṣāṇāṃ pṛthvyādibhirekībhūtānāṃ kinna bhavati sendriyaṃ cittamiti bhūtasaṃyogāt
athaiṣāṃ sthāvarāṇāṃ jantuśaktirnāstīti, tadā paramamṛṣā bhūtasaṃyogaśaktiriti; tasmādetaccārvākavākyaṃ na hi sukhaphaladaṃ mārganaṣṭaṃ narāṇāmiti lokāyatamatadūṣaṇaniyamaḥ
[159b] idānīṃ kṣapaṇaka matadūṣaṇamucyate jīva ityādi- jīvaḥ kāyapramāṇo yadi karacaraṇacchedanānnasya (śya) te kiṃ nityaḥ kāyaprabhāvādaṇurapi ca bhavet sthūlatāṃ kiṃ prayāti
saṃsārāt karmamukto vrajati sukhapadaṃ yat sthitaṃ lokamūrdhni trailokyaṃ cāṇubhiryad racitamapi sadā śāśvataṃ tanna kālāt ||176|| svaparadarśananyāyavicāramahoddeśaḥ iha kṣapaṇaka siddhānte jīvo nityaḥ, sa ca kāyapramāṇa iti siddham iti cet, tadā kāyāvayave karacaraṇādau chinne sati kiṃ vinasya (śya) te, chinnāvayavamūrterabhāvā- diti
nityaḥ kāya prabhāvādaṇurapi ca bhavet, sūkṣmakāyagrahaṇāt, sthūlakāyagrahaṇāt', sthūlatāṃ kiṃ prayātīti ? iha yo nityaḥ so'vikārī, yo vikārī so'nityaḥ siddha iti
āha - dravyaparyāyābhyāṃ nityānityamiti syādvādaḥ
āha-iha yathā suvarṇaṃ kuṇḍalābhyāṃ nityānityam, tathā dravyaparyāyābhyāṃ jīvadravyaṃ nityaṃ vikāro'nitya iti, tathā ca syādvādaḥ
kathei jīvo hoi valio kathei kammāi bhonti valiāi
jīvasya (ssa) a kammasya (ssa) a pūrva (pubba) ṇibarddhā ( ṇibaddhā ) i ve (ve ) rāi || iti
asyā gāthāyā arthamāha- kutraciditi
mokṣaviṣaye jīvo balavān, kaivalyajñāna- balena
kutraciccaturgatisaṃsāraviṣaye karma balavat, ajñānabalena; evaṃ jīvasyāpi karmaṇaśca pūrvā'nādikālanibaddhāni verāṇīti siddham
evaṃ dravyotpattivyayātmā svaguṇayuta iti nānyathā siddhiriti siddhāntaḥ
āha - dravyaparyāyayorekatvamanyatvaṃ vā ? iha yadyekatvam, tadā dravyaparyāyayorbhedo nāsti; athānyatvam, tadā dravyaivinā paryāyo bhavati, na caivaṃ dṛśyate sūtraivinā paṭaḥ
evaṃ
jātivyaktyorapi nityānityasaṃyogadoṣa iti
nityānityayorekatvaṃ nāsti, paraspara- virodhāt, asadṛśasadṛśayoryathā
ato jīvajātyādidravyamanityamiti siddham
tathā saṃsārāt karmamukto vrajati sukhapadaṃ yat sthitaṃ lokamūrdhnāti
iha kṣapaṇaka siddhānte ekameva tribhuvanam dvitīyaṃ nāsti; tenedaṃ tribhuvanam anādinidhana - mutpādavyayarahitaṃ sarvaṃ vajramayaṃ na kadācit kṣayaṃ yāsyatīti
yadyasya bhuvanasya kṣayo bhavati, tadā'nyatribhuvanābhāvāt sarve prāṇinaḥ kutra sthāsyanti
tena kāraṇenedaṃ nityam; jīvo'pi nityaḥ saṃsārāt karmamuktaḥ san vrajati mokṣaṃ pañcacatvāriṃśadyojanalakṣaṃ sukhapadam, pudgalarahitamiti siddham
āha - iha trailokyam aṇubhirjātam, nāṇubhirvinā, cakārānmokṣo'pi tat kathaṃ nityaṃ bhavati, yadanubho racitamapi śāśvatamapi nityaṃ sadā sarvakālaṃ na bhavati, saṃhāra- kālavaśāt kṣayaṃ yāsyatīti
tatkṣayāt siddhāntānāmapi kṣa [160a] yo bhaviṣyatīti siddhamiti nyāyāt
kiñcānyat
iha pūrvoktānāṃ ṣaḍjīva kāya leśyānāṃ madhye vanaspatīnāṃ jīvaḥ ; sa ki pratyeka vanaspatikāya ekaḥ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ vā karmavaśenānubhavati, athāneka ityāha- T vimalaprabhāyāṃ [adhyātma- eko jīva ekaṃ pudgalaṃ gṛhṇāti yasya prabhāvena vanaspatīnāṃ sa (śa) syādīnāṃ jīvitasaṃjñā
yadi jīvo nāsti, tadā pādapa iti kathaṃ siddham
na cājīvāḥ kāṣṭhā udakaṃ pītvā puṣpādi ṛtu prakurvantīti siddham
āha-yadi iha pratyekapudgale pratyekam ekaiko jīvaḥ, tadā ikṣudaṇḍe khaṇḍe khaṇḍe kṛte'nekakhaṇḍāni bhavanti teṣāṃ madhye ekasmin khaṇḍe sa jīvo nityaḥ karmavaśāt saṅkucan praviṣṭaḥ
kāni tena parityaktāni na caivaṃ yuktyā ghaṭate
vicāryamāṇaḥ kuto yatasteṣāṃ punarbhūmyāmāropitānāmaṅkurādikaṃ pratyekakhaṇḍe dṛśyate
tasmād vastusvabhāvo vanaspatīnām' aṅkurādiśaktiriti siddham
iti kṣapaṇakamatadūṣaṇaniyamaḥ
vistaro'neko'neka pramāṇaśāstreṇa madhyamakena nirākaraṇīyastīrthikānāṃ siddhāntaḥ
yaḥ saṃvṛtyā vivṛtyā vā sambuddhavacanasamaḥ, sa na dūṣaṇīya iti kālacakra ādibuddhabhagavato niyamaḥ
tadyathā - ityādijñānahetoḥ prakaṭayati mahau deśanāṃ kālacakraḥ puṃsāṃ cittānusārāṃ mṛdukaṭhinaparāṃ vāsanāyā balena
cittaṃ vai bhāvarāgaiḥ sphaṭikavadupadhād rāgatāṃ yāti yasmāt tasmād dharmo na kaścit svaparakulagato yoginā dūṣaṇīyaḥ || 177||
- svaśaktiriti siddham
svapar darśananyāyavicāramahoddeśaḥ dharmaḥ sattvopakāro viṣayavirahitaścāpakā ro'pyadharmaḥ hiṃsā vedapramāṇā na hi sukhaphaladā duḥkhadā sarvakālam
sanmaitro mūrkhavākyāt paramasukhakarā sarvasattvānuraktā tasmāt sattvārthamekaṃ kuru nṛpa manasā bhāvanāṃ niḥsvabhāvaṃ ||178 ||
indro'haṃ svargaloke tridaśana ragururbhūtale cakravartī pā tāle nāgarājaḥ phaṇikulanamitaḥ sarvagaścottamo'ham
jñānaṃ buddho munīndro'kṣaraparamavibhuryogināṃ vajrayogo vedo'ṅkāraḥ pavitro vraja mama śaraṇaṃ sarvabhāvena rājan || 179 ||
iti śaraṇaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ sūryarathasya namaskāraḥ- tvaṃ mātā tvaṃ pitā tvaṃ jagati gururapi tvaṃ ca bandhuḥ sumitraṃ tvaṃ nāthastvaṃ vidhātā hitaṃ aghaharaṇa tvaṃ padaṃ sampadāṃ ca
tvaṃ kaivalyaṃ padaṃ tvaṃ varaguṇanilayo dhvastadoṣastvameva tvaṃ donānātha cintāmaṇirapi śaraṇaṃ tvāṃ gato'haṃ jinendra ||180||
iti śrīmadādibuddhoddhṛte śrīmahākālacakre' adhyātmanirṇayo dvitīyapaṭalaḥ ||2||
iti sūryaratho gurunamaskāreṇa mañjuśriyaṃ bhagavantaṃ stutvā pādadvayaṃ śirasi kṛtvā punaḥ svakīyāsane niṣannaḥ
iti śrīmūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ svaparadarśana nyāyavicāramahoddeśaḥ saptamaḥ ||7||
samāpteyaṃ ṭokā adhyātmapaṭalasyeti ||2||
uparilikhitabhoṭāṃśasya saṃskṛtānuvāda:-- pramāṇaśāstreṣu mādhyamikaśāstreṣu vā nirdiṣṭaivistṛtadūṣaṇaprakāraistairthikāḥ dūṣaṇīyāḥ
taithiketare ye saṃvṛti vā paramārthaṃ vā aṅgīkṛtya buddhavacanānusāreṇaiva svapakṣaṃ sthāpayanti, te naiva dūṣaṇīyāḥ”
ka. śrīmahākālacakratantrarāje
ka. dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ
da hāda vimalaprabhāyāṃ śrīmattantre prathamapaṭale bhājanībhūtalokaḥ spaṣṭoddiṣṭa [:] prabhavalayasaṃsthānamānādibhedāḥ
